# Stored Procedures — Batch 1

Documentation for 129 stored procedures (batch 1 of 4), reverse-engineered directly from the live `SalesrollDB_ZAS` T-SQL definitions in `procs_batch1.txt` (the file was regenerated by the coordinator after an initial sqlcmd truncation bug was fixed — this batch uses the corrected, full-body version). Application callers were located by grepping the literal procedure name (in quotes) across `Library.DAL`, `Library.BLL`, and `Solution.Web`. Where no caller was found, the procedure is marked `UNUSED — no application caller found`. Table/column facts (FROM/JOIN/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE) are read directly out of each body; business-logic descriptions are based only on what the T-SQL does. Where intent could not be determined from the body alone, it is marked `UNKNOWN — HUMAN VERIFICATION REQUIRED`.

## DeleteEmployee

**Parameters:**
- `@EmployeeID INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Employee
**Tables written:** DELETE → Employee

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Deletes a single row from the generic `Employee` table by `@EmployeeID`. This looks like a leftover scaffold/demo procedure (table name and header comment are generic, not part of the ZAS schema seen elsewhere in this batch).

**Result set:** No result set (pure DELETE).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for deleting an employee
CREATE PROCEDURE DeleteEmployee
    @EmployeeID INT
AS
BEGIN
    DELETE FROM Employee
    WHERE EmployeeID = @EmployeeID;
END;
```

## DynamicAttendanceMonthly

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@EmployeeId nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ActiveStatus NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Shift NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail, dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail, tblDoctorDegree, tblDoctorSpeciality, tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblMarketAttendance_Master_webapi, tblUser
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes a dynamic PIVOT query (via `EXEC(@SqlStatement)`) that turns a day-by-day attendance punch-in log into a pivoted grid (one column per day/value passed in `@ListToPivot`), for one employee/company/shift/status combination, joining the local employee table with the cross-database `ZASHRIS` HR employee table.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: employee code/name plus one pivoted column per day showing max punch-in time for that day.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicAttendanceMonthly]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	 @EmployeeId  nvarchar(Max), 
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ActiveStatus NVARCHAR(max),
	  @Shift NVARCHAR(max)


AS
BEGIN
 --STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.DegreeName , '''') FROM tblDoctorDegree mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.DegId=mm.DegreeId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,'''') AS Degree,ISNULL(STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.SpecialityName , '''') FROM tblDoctorSpeciality mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.SpecialityId=mm.SpecialityId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,''''),'''')  as [Speciality], pt.ProgramTypeName [Provider Type],dt.DoctorTypeName [Doctor Type], gr.GroupCode [Group Code],  gr.GroupName [Group Name],rg.RegionCode [Zone Code],rg.RegionName  [Zone Name],Ar.AreaCode [Area Code],Ar.AreaName [Area Name],Tr.TerritoryCode [Territory Code],Tr.TerritoryName [Territory Name],subTr.SubTerritoryCode [Sub-Territory Code],subTr.SubTerritoryName [Sub-Territory Name],mr.MarketCode [Market Code],mr.MarketName [Market Name]
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)
   
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
 select       e.EmpMasterCode [Emp. ID], e.EmpName [Employee Name], CONVERT(date,mas.AttendanceDate)  AttendanceDate, mas.PunchInTime    from  tblEmpGeneralInfo e   WITH (NOLOCK) 
 left  join tblMarketAttendance_Master_webapi mas  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.EmpInfoId=e.EmpInfoId
  inner join tblUser ss on ss.EmpInfoId=e.EmpInfoId  and ss.IsAppsUser=1
   inner join ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo hris on hris.EmpMasterCode=e.EmpMasterCode
  
 

  where  mas.ShiftId ='+@Shift+'  and Month(mas.AttendanceDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.AttendanceDate)='+@Year+'    and mas.EmpInfoId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @EmployeeId+''' ,'''') ,mas.EmpInfoId )   and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId) and hris.EmployeeStatus= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ActiveStatus+''' , '''') ,hris.EmployeeStatus)
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
          max(PunchInTime)
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 
 
 
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

## DynamicDatebyMonthYearForStuff

**Parameters:**
- `@Month NVARCHAR(255)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(255)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** sys.all_objects
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given a month and year, generates a comma-separated, bracket-quoted list of every calendar date in that month (e.g. `[2024-05-01],[2024-05-02],...`) using a `sys.all_objects` numbers-table trick — a helper used to build the column list for other `DynamicPivot*` procedures.

**Result set:** Single result set: one row/column `mainDate` containing the concatenated date list string.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
create PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicDatebyMonthYearForStuff]
   
  @Month NVARCHAR(255),
  @Year NVARCHAR(255)
AS
BEGIN
 declare @Date datetime
 set @Date=CONVERT(Date,'01-'+@Month+'-'+@Year)
 

 

 select 
STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',',  '['+ CONVERT(nvarchar(max),CONVERT(date,DATEADD(dd,a.n-1,datefromparts(@Year,@Month,1))))+']'  , '')   
from    (
         select top  (DAY(EOMONTH(@Date))) ROW_NUMBER() over (order by a.object_id) as n
         from   sys.all_objects a
      ) a
	  
	  
	   where   DATEPART(mm,DATEADD(dd,a.n-1,datefromparts(@Year,@Month,1)))=@Month   FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'') as  mainDate



	   END
```

## DynamicPivotBrandWiseDCR

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ApprovalStatus nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ProviderType NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblProduct, tblProductSQ, tbl_DCRInfo, tbl_DcrDetails
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes a dynamic PIVOT (`EXEC(@SqlStatement)`) of DCR (daily call report) product quantity by brand for a given month/year, filtered by provider type / pharma platform / company, with a branch for `@ApprovalStatus='Select'` (no status filter) vs a specific status.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: brand name, DCR date, plus one pivoted column per value in `@ListToPivot` summing `ProductQty`.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotBrandWiseDCR]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	@ApprovalStatus  nvarchar(Max),
	  @ProviderType NVARCHAR(max),
	  @PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)
AS
BEGIN
 
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)
   if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select  proBrnd.ProductSQName  [Brand Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.DcrDate)  DcrDate,   ISNULL((ProductQty),0) ProductQty   from tbl_DcrDetails dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_DCRInfo mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DcrId=dcrDtl.DcrId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
 inner join tblProductSQ proBrnd  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductBrandId=proBrnd.ProductBrandId
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'  and mas.DoctorProgramypeId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorProgramypeId )   and mas.SmcTypeId_DCR= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SmcTypeId_DCR)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      SUM([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 end

 else
 begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select  proBrnd.ProductSQName  [Brand Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.DcrDate)  DcrDate,  (ProductQty) ProductQty   from tbl_DcrDetails dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_DCRInfo mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DcrId=dcrDtl.DcrId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
 inner join tblProductSQ proBrnd  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductBrandId=proBrnd.ProductBrandId
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'
      and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'   and mas.DoctorProgramypeId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorProgramypeId )   and mas.SmcTypeId_DCR= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SmcTypeId_DCR)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      SUM([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 end
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

## DynamicPivotBrandWiseRX_new

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ApprovalStatus nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ProviderType NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@DoctorTypeSelect NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblProduct, tblProductSQ, tblProgramType, tbl_PrescriptionMaster, tbl_PrescriptionProductDetail
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Same dynamic-PIVOT pattern as `DynamicPivotBrandWiseDCR` but sourced from prescription (RX) detail/master tables instead of DCR tables, also filterable by doctor type.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: brand/product name, prescription date, plus pivoted `ProductQty` per pivot value.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotBrandWiseRX_new]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	@ApprovalStatus  nvarchar(Max),
	  @ProviderType NVARCHAR(max),
	  @PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max),
	  @DoctorTypeSelect  NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)
AS
BEGIN
 
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)

  if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select  proBrnd.ProductSQName [Brand Name], pro.ProductCode+'' : ''+pro.ProductName [Product Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((dcrDtl.ProductId),0) ProductQty   from tbl_PrescriptionProductDetail dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.PrescriptionId=dcrDtl.PrescriptionId
  left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorProgramypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
 inner join tblProductSQ proBrnd  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductBrandId=proBrnd.ProductBrandId
  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'   and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName )  and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId) 
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      SUM([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';


  end 

  else

  begin
    SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select  proBrnd.ProductSQName [Brand Name], pro.ProductCode+'' : ''+pro.ProductName [Product Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((dcrDtl.ProductId),0) ProductQty   from tbl_PrescriptionProductDetail dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.PrescriptionId=dcrDtl.PrescriptionId
  left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorProgramypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
 inner join tblProductSQ proBrnd  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductBrandId=proBrnd.ProductBrandId
  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'  and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName ) and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX) 
  and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'   and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId) ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      SUM([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';


  end
 
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

## DynamicPivotDoctorWiseDCR_New_ForSearch

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ApprovalStatus nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ProviderType NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** View_DCR, dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail, dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail, tblDoctorDegree, tblDoctorSpeciality
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Dynamic PIVOT of DCR visit counts per doctor, sourced from the `View_DCR` view (already denormalizes doctor/degree/speciality/geography), filtered by pharma platform/provider type/company and optionally approval status.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: doctor id/code/name/speciality etc. plus a pivoted count-of-doctor-id column per pivot value.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotDoctorWiseDCR_New_ForSearch]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	@ApprovalStatus  nvarchar(Max),
	  @ProviderType NVARCHAR(max),
	  @PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)

AS
BEGIN
 --STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.DegreeName , '''') FROM tblDoctorDegree mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.DegId=mm.DegreeId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,'''') AS Degree,ISNULL(STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.SpecialityName , '''') FROM tblDoctorSpeciality mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.SpecialityId=mm.SpecialityId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,''''),'''')  as [Speciality], pt.ProgramTypeName [Provider Type],dt.DoctorTypeName [Doctor Type], gr.GroupCode [Group Code],  gr.GroupName [Group Name],rg.RegionCode [Zone Code],rg.RegionName  [Zone Name],Ar.AreaCode [Area Code],Ar.AreaName [Area Name],Tr.TerritoryCode [Territory Code],Tr.TerritoryName [Territory Name],subTr.SubTerritoryCode [Sub-Territory Code],subTr.SubTerritoryName [Sub-Territory Name],mr.MarketCode [Market Code],mr.MarketName [Market Name]
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)
   if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
 select DoctorId,DcrDate ,[Doctor Code],  [Doctor Name], [Doctor Speciality],[Provider Type], [Pharma Platform], [Doctor Type],[Group],[Region],[Area],[Territory],[Market] from   View_DCR mas with (nolock)
  
  
 

  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'  and mas.[Pharma Platform]= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.[Pharma Platform] )   and mas.[Provider Type]= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.[Provider Type] )   and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
          count(DoctorId)
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 
 end 

  else

  begin
   SET @SqlStatement = N'
   SELECT * FROM (
  select DoctorId,DcrDate ,[Doctor Code],  [Doctor Name], [Doctor Speciality],[Provider Type], [Pharma Platform], [Doctor Type],[Group],[Region],[Area],[Territory],[Market] from   View_DCR mas with (nolock)
  
  
 
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'
      and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'   and mas.[Pharma Platform]= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.[Pharma Platform] )   and mas.[Provider Type]= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.[Provider Type] )  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
          count(DoctorId)
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 

end
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

## DynamicPivotDoctorWiseDoctorVisitPlan

**Parameters:**
- `@param NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail, dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail, tblDoctorDegree, tblDoctorMaster, tblDoctorSpeciality, tblDoctorType, tblProgramType, tbl_DoctorTourPlanDetail, tbl_DoctorTourPlanMaster
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes a dynamic aggregate query (not a true PIVOT — uses GROUP BY) counting planned doctor visits from `tbl_DoctorTourPlanDetail`/`tbl_DoctorTourPlanMaster`, with an arbitrary caller-supplied `@param` string appended directly into the WHERE clause.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: doctor name/code/degree/speciality/program type/doctor type plus a visit count.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotDoctorWiseDoctorVisitPlan]
  @param  NVARCHAR(max) 
AS
BEGIN
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
select COUNT(DoctorCount) DoctorCount ,DoctorName,DoctorCode,Degree,Speciality,ProgramTypeName,DoctorTypeName from (
select     (tpDtl.DoctorId) DoctorCount,   doc.DoctorName  ,doc.DoctorCode ,STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.DegreeName , '''') FROM tblDoctorDegree mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.DegId=mm.DegreeId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=tpDtl.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,'''') AS [Degree],ISNULL(STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.SpecialityName , '''') FROM tblDoctorSpeciality mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.SpecialityId=mm.SpecialityId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=tpDtl.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,''''),'''') as [Speciality], pt.ProgramTypeName ,dt.DoctorTypeName    from  tblDoctorMaster doc WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join tbl_DoctorTourPlanDetail tpDtl  WITH (NOLOCK)    on tpDtl.DoctorId=doc.DoctorId
 inner join tbl_DoctorTourPlanMaster tpMas  WITH (NOLOCK)    on tpMas.DocTPMaster=tpDtl.DocTPMaster
 left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on doc.ProgramTypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
 left join tblDoctorType dt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on doc.DoctorTypeId=dt.DoctorTypeId

  where  tpMas.DocTPMaster is not null '+@param+' ) tbl



  group by DoctorName,DoctorCode,Degree,Speciality,ProgramTypeName,DoctorTypeName

 
 '

  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

## DynamicPivotDoctorWiseRX_New

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ApprovalStatus nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ProviderType NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@DoctorTypeSelect NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail, dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail, tblDoctorDegree, tblDoctorMaster, tblDoctorSpeciality, tblProgramType, tbl_PrescriptionMaster
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Dynamic PIVOT of prescription counts per doctor from `tbl_PrescriptionMaster`, filtered by provider type/doctor type/pharma platform/company, with degree/speciality expanded via inline correlated subqueries (STUFF/FOR XML PATH string concatenation).

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: doctor id/code/name/degree/speciality/etc. plus a pivoted count-of-doctor-id column per pivot value.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotDoctorWiseRX_New]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	@ApprovalStatus  nvarchar(Max),
	  @ProviderType NVARCHAR(max),
	  @PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max),
	  @DoctorTypeSelect  NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)
AS
BEGIN
 --STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.DegreeName , '''') FROM tblDoctorDegree mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.DegId=mm.DegreeId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,'''') AS Degree,ISNULL(STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.SpecialityName , '''') FROM tblDoctorSpeciality mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.SpecialityId=mm.SpecialityId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,''''),'''')  as [Speciality], pt.ProgramTypeName [Provider Type],dt.DoctorTypeName [Doctor Type], gr.GroupCode [Group Code],  gr.GroupName [Group Name],rg.RegionCode [Zone Code],rg.RegionName  [Zone Name],Ar.AreaCode [Area Code],Ar.AreaName [Area Name],Tr.TerritoryCode [Territory Code],Tr.TerritoryName [Territory Name],subTr.SubTerritoryCode [Sub-Territory Code],subTr.SubTerritoryName [Sub-Territory Name],mr.MarketCode [Market Code],mr.MarketName [Market Name]
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)
   if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
 select      mas.DoctorId, CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  doc.DoctorCode [Doctor Code],    doc.DoctorName [Doctor Name],STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.DegreeName , '''') FROM tblDoctorDegree mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.DegId=mm.DegreeId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,'''') AS [Degree Name],ISNULL(STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.SpecialityName , '''') FROM tblDoctorSpeciality mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.SpecialityId=mm.SpecialityId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,''''),'''') as [Doctor Speciality], pt.ProgramTypeName [Provider Type] ,mas.SMCType_RX [Pharma Platform],mas.DoctorType_RX [Doctor Type] ,  mas.GroupCode_RX [Group], mas.RegionCode_RX   [Region],mas.AreaCode_RX  [Area],mas.TerritoryCode_RX  [Territory] ,mas.MarketCode_RX   [Market] from tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas   WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join tblDoctorMaster doc  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorId=doc.DoctorId
 left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorProgramypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
  
  
  
 

  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'    and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName ) and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX) and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId)
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
          count(DoctorId)
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 
 end 

  else

  begin
   SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
 select      mas.DoctorId, CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  doc.DoctorCode [Doctor Code],    doc.DoctorName [Doctor Name],STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.DegreeName , '''') FROM tblDoctorDegree mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.DegId=mm.DegreeId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,'''') AS [Degree Name],ISNULL(STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT('','', mm.SpecialityName , '''') FROM tblDoctorSpeciality mm   WITH (NOLOCK)  INNER JOIN dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd   WITH (NOLOCK)  ON mgd.SpecialityId=mm.SpecialityId WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('''') ),1,1,''''),'''') as [Doctor Speciality], pt.ProgramTypeName [Provider Type] ,mas.SMCType_RX [Pharma Platform],dt.DoctorTypeName [Doctor Type] ,  mas.GroupCode_RX [Group], mas.RegionCode_RX   [Region],mas.AreaCode_RX  [Area],mas.TerritoryCode_RX  [Territory] ,mas.MarketCode_RX   [Market] from tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas   WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join tblDoctorMaster doc  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorId=doc.DoctorId
 left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorProgramypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
  
  
  
  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'
      and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'  and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )    and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX) and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId) ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
          count(DoctorId)
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 

end
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

## DynamicPivotProductdWiseDCR

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ApprovalStatus nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ProviderType NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblProduct, tbl_DCRInfo, tbl_DcrDetails
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Dynamic PIVOT of DCR product quantity by product (not brand) for a month/year, same filter pattern as `DynamicPivotBrandWiseDCR`.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: product code/name, DCR date, plus a pivoted sum of `ProductQty` per pivot value.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotProductdWiseDCR]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	@ApprovalStatus  nvarchar(Max),
	  @ProviderType NVARCHAR(max),
	  @PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)
AS
BEGIN
 
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)

   if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select pro.ProductCode [Product Code], pro.ProductName [Product Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.DcrDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((ProductQty),0) ProductQty   from tbl_DcrDetails dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_DCRInfo mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DcrId=dcrDtl.DcrId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'   and mas.DoctorProgramypeId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorProgramypeId )   and mas.SmcTypeId_DCR= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SmcTypeId_DCR) and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      SUM([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 

 end

 else 

 begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select pro.ProductCode [Product Code], pro.ProductName [Product Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.DcrDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((ProductQty),0) ProductQty   from tbl_DcrDetails dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_DCRInfo mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DcrId=dcrDtl.DcrId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'
   and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'   and mas.DoctorProgramypeId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorProgramypeId )   and mas.SmcTypeId_DCR= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SmcTypeId_DCR)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      SUM([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 end
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),

  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'    and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName )  and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )    and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX) and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId)

    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 

  end 

  else

  begin

   SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select pro.ProductCode [Product Code], pro.ProductName [Product Name],     CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((dcrDtl.ProductId),0) ProductQty   from tbl_PrescriptionProductDetail dcrDtl    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.PrescriptionId=dcrDtl.PrescriptionId
 inner join tblProduct pro  WITH (NOLOCK)    on pro.ProductId=dcrDtl.ProductId
   left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorProgramypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'    and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName )   and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX) 
    and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId)  ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (

## DynamicPivotUserWiseDCR

**Parameters:**
- `@ColumnToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ListToPivot NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Month NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@Year NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ApprovalStatus nvarchar(Max)`
- `@ProviderType NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max)`
- `@ComId NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** View_Webapi_EmployeeFieldForceInfo, tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblRoleType, tblUser, tbl_DCRInfo, tbl_UserRoleInfo
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Dynamic PIVOT of DCR entries per sales user (joins `tblUser`/`tbl_UserRoleInfo`/`tblRoleType`/`tblEmpGeneralInfo` and the `View_Webapi_EmployeeFieldForceInfo` view for territory/region/group codes based on role type), for a month/year.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: employee name (with role), region/territory code, DCR date, plus a pivoted count column per pivot value.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotUserWiseDCR]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),
  @Month NVARCHAR(max),
  @Year NVARCHAR(max),
	@ApprovalStatus  nvarchar(Max),
	  @ProviderType NVARCHAR(max),
	  @PharmaPlatform NVARCHAR(max),
	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)
AS
BEGIN
 
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)

   if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select emp.EmpMasterCode  +'' - ''+  emp.EmpName+'' (''+usrRT.RoleType+'')'' [Employee Name],  EFF.RegionCode,  case when usrRT.RoleTypeId=4   then EFF.GroupCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=3  then  EFF.RegionCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=2  then  EFF.AreaCode   else EFF.TerritoryCode end TerritoryCode,   CONVERT(date,mas.DcrDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((mas.EntryBy),0) ProductQty   from tbl_DCRInfo mas    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tblUser usr WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.EntryBy=usr.UserId
 inner join  tbl_UserRoleInfo usrRl WITH (NOLOCK)    on usr.UserRoleID=usrRl.UserRoleID
 inner join  tblRoleType usrRT WITH (NOLOCK)    on usrRT.RoleTypeId=usrRl.RoleTypeId
 inner join  tblEmpGeneralInfo emp WITH (NOLOCK)    on usr.EmpInfoId=emp.EmpInfoId
  inner join View_Webapi_EmployeeFieldForceInfo EFF  WITH (NOLOCK) on emp.EmpInfoId=EFF.EmpInfoId 
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'   and mas.DoctorProgramypeId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorProgramypeId )   and mas.SmcTypeId_DCR= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SmcTypeId_DCR)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId )
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      count([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 

 end 
 else

 begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select emp.EmpMasterCode  +'' - ''+  emp.EmpName+'' (''+usrRT.RoleType+'')'' [Employee Name],  EFF.RegionCode,  case when usrRT.RoleTypeId=4   then EFF.GroupCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=3  then  EFF.RegionCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=2  then  EFF.AreaCode   else EFF.TerritoryCode end TerritoryCode,   CONVERT(date,mas.DcrDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((mas.EntryBy),0) ProductQty   from tbl_DCRInfo mas    WITH (NOLOCK) 
 inner join  tblUser usr WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.EntryBy=usr.UserId
 inner join  tbl_UserRoleInfo usrRl WITH (NOLOCK)    on usr.UserRoleID=usrRl.UserRoleID
 inner join  tblRoleType usrRT WITH (NOLOCK)    on usrRT.RoleTypeId=usrRl.RoleTypeId
 inner join  tblEmpGeneralInfo emp WITH (NOLOCK)    on usr.EmpInfoId=emp.EmpInfoId
  inner join View_Webapi_EmployeeFieldForceInfo EFF  WITH (NOLOCK) on emp.EmpInfoId=EFF.EmpInfoId 
  where Month(mas.DcrDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.DcrDate)='+@Year+'
     and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+'    and mas.DoctorProgramypeId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ProviderType+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorProgramypeId )   and mas.SmcTypeId_DCR= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SmcTypeId_DCR)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId ) ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      count([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 
 end
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)
 
END
 
```

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DynamicPivotUserWiseRX_New]
  @ColumnToPivot  NVARCHAR(max),
  @ListToPivot    NVARCHAR(max),

  @Year NVARCHAR(max),

	  @ComId NVARCHAR(max)
AS
BEGIN
 
  DECLARE @SqlStatement NVARCHAR(MAX)
   if(@ApprovalStatus='Select')
	begin
  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select emp.EmpMasterCode +'' - ''+ emp.EmpName+'' (''+usrRT.RoleType+'')'' [Employee Name],  EFF.RegionCode,  case when usrRT.RoleTypeId=4   then EFF.GroupCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=3  then  EFF.RegionCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=2  then  EFF.AreaCode   else EFF.TerritoryCode end TerritoryCode,   CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((mas.EntryBy),0) ProductQty   from tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas    WITH (NOLOCK)

  inner join View_Webapi_EmployeeFieldForceInfo EFF  WITH (NOLOCK) on emp.EmpInfoId=EFF.EmpInfoId 
  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'  and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName )  and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId) 
    ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      count([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
 end 

  else

  begin

  SET @SqlStatement = N'
    SELECT * FROM (
     select emp.EmpMasterCode +'' - ''+ emp.EmpName+'' (''+usrRT.RoleType+'')'' [Employee Name],  EFF.RegionCode [Region Code],  case when usrRT.RoleTypeId=4   then EFF.GroupCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=3  then  EFF.RegionCode when usrRT.RoleTypeId=2  then  EFF.AreaCode   else EFF.TerritoryCode end [Territory Code],   CONVERT(date,mas.PrescriptionDate)  DcrDate,  isnull((mas.EntryBy),0) ProductQty   from tbl_PrescriptionMaster mas    WITH (NOLOCK) 
	  left join tblProgramType pt  WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.DoctorProgramypeId=pt.ProgramTypeId
 inner join  tblUser usr WITH (NOLOCK)    on mas.EntryBy=usr.UserId
 inner join  tbl_UserRoleInfo usrRl WITH (NOLOCK)    on usr.UserRoleID=usrRl.UserRoleID
 inner join  tblRoleType usrRT WITH (NOLOCK)    on usrRT.RoleTypeId=usrRl.RoleTypeId
 inner join  tblEmpGeneralInfo emp WITH (NOLOCK)    on usr.EmpInfoId=emp.EmpInfoId
  inner join View_Webapi_EmployeeFieldForceInfo EFF  WITH (NOLOCK) on emp.EmpInfoId=EFF.EmpInfoId 
  where Month(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Month+' and Year(mas.PrescriptionDate)='+@Year+'
      and  mas.ApprovalStatus='+@ApprovalStatus+' and pt.ProgramTypeName= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @ProviderType+''' , '''') ,pt.ProgramTypeName )  and mas.DoctorType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+ @DoctorTypeSelect+''' , '''') ,mas.DoctorType_RX )   and mas.SMCType_RX= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@PharmaPlatform+''' , '''') ,mas.SMCType_RX)  and mas.CompanyId= COALESCE( NULLIF('''+@ComId+''' , '''') ,mas.CompanyId)   ) StudentResults
    PIVOT (
      count([ProductQty])
      FOR ['+@ColumnToPivot+']
      IN (
        '+@ListToPivot+'
      )
    ) AS PivotTable
  ';
  end
  EXEC(@SqlStatement)

## ExecuteAllSqlQueryByStoreProcedure

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @Query NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** (none)
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** A generic SQL-injection-shaped wrapper: takes an arbitrary `@Query` string and runs it via `EXEC(@Query)`. Used internally by the DAL's common command builder (`ClsCommonInternalDAL.GetStoredProcCommand`) as the vehicle for executing ad-hoc/dynamically-built SQL text through the same ADO.NET stored-procedure-command plumbing used for real procs.

**Result set:** Whatever result set the dynamically-supplied `@Query` produces (proc itself defines none of its own columns).

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/InternalCls/ClsCommonInternalDAL.cs — used repeatedly inside `ClsCommonInternalDAL` wherever the DAL needs to run a raw/ad-hoc SQL string instead of a named stored procedure.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ExecuteAllSqlQueryByStoreProcedure]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@Query NVARCHAR(MAX)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
	EXEC (@Query)
END
```

## GetEmployee

**Parameters:**
- `@EmployeeID INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Employee
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Returns all columns for one row of the generic `Employee` table by `@EmployeeID`. Same scaffold/demo family as `DeleteEmployee`/`InsertEmployee`/`UpdateEmployee`/`ListEmployees`.

**Result set:** Single result set: `SELECT * FROM Employee WHERE EmployeeID = @EmployeeID`.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for getting a single employee record
CREATE PROCEDURE GetEmployee
    @EmployeeID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SELECT * FROM Employee
    WHERE EmployeeID = @EmployeeID;
END;
```

**Tables written:** INSERT → Employee

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Inserts a new row into the generic `Employee` table and returns the new identity via `SCOPE_IDENTITY()`; also declares (but does not set) an `@EmployeeID OUTPUT` parameter — the actual new id is only returned through the trailing SELECT, not the OUTPUT param.

**Result set:** Single result set: `SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY()` (the new EmployeeID as a generic result column, not bound to the declared OUTPUT parameter).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for inserting a new employee
CREATE PROCEDURE InsertEmployee
 @EmployeeID INT out,
    @FirstName NVARCHAR(50),
    @LastName NVARCHAR(50),
    @Department NVARCHAR(50),
    @Position NVARCHAR(50),
    @Salary DECIMAL(10, 2)
AS
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO Employee (FirstName, LastName, Department, Position, Salary)
    VALUES (@FirstName, @LastName, @Department, @Position, @Salary);

	select SCOPE_IDENTITY()
END;
```

## SP_Apps_WingsSalesCollectionSummary

**Parameters:**
- `( @Month INT , @Year INT )`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CollectionData, ReturnData, SalesData, dbo.tblCompanyInfo, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblCustPayDetail, dbo.tblCustomerPay, dbo.tblInvoice
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and runs (via `sp_executesql`) a dynamic report comparing each company/"wing"'s current-month vs last-month-same-date sales (from `tblInvoice`) and collections (from `tblCustPayDetail`/`tblCustomerPay`), plus sales returns from the accounting voucher tables, for a given month/year; rolls up a grand total row and computes a running Due figure.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set (via ROLLUP): wing name, sales/last-month-sales/today-sales/last-month-same-day-sales, collection equivalents, and a computed Due, one row per company plus a Grand Total row.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE   PROCEDURE dbo.SP_Apps_WingsSalesCollectionSummary
(
      @Month INT
    , @Year  INT
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @ReportDate DATE;

    IF @Month = MONTH(GETDATE()) AND @Year = YEAR(GETDATE())
        SET @ReportDate = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);
    ELSE
        SET @ReportDate = EOMONTH(DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, 1));

    DECLARE @CurrentMonthStart DATE;
    DECLARE @LastMonthSameDate DATE;
    DECLARE @LastMonthStart DATE;

    SET @CurrentMonthStart = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(@ReportDate), MONTH(@ReportDate), 1);
    SET @LastMonthSameDate = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @ReportDate);
    SET @LastMonthStart = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(@LastMonthSameDate), MONTH(@LastMonthSameDate), 1);

    DECLARE @TodaySalesHeader NVARCHAR(100);
    DECLARE @LastMonthSameDaySalesHeader NVARCHAR(100);
    DECLARE @TodayCollectionHeader NVARCHAR(100);
    DECLARE @LastMonthSameDayCollectionHeader NVARCHAR(100);

    SET @TodaySalesHeader =
        'Today Sales (' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @ReportDate, 105) + ')';

    SET @LastMonthSameDaySalesHeader =
        'Last Month Same Date Sales (' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LastMonthSameDate, 105) + ')';

    SET @TodayCollectionHeader =
        'Today Collection (' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @ReportDate, 105) + ')';

    SET @LastMonthSameDayCollectionHeader =
        'Last Month Same Date Collection (' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LastMonthSameDate, 105) + ')';

    DECLARE @SQL NVARCHAR(MAX);

    SET @SQL = N'
;WITH SalesData AS
(
    SELECT
        INV.ComUnitId,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN INV.InvoiceDate >= @CurrentMonthStart
             AND INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
            THEN ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS CurrentMonthSales,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN INV.InvoiceDate >= @LastMonthStart
             AND INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @LastMonthSameDate)
            THEN ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS LastMonthSales,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN INV.InvoiceDate >= @ReportDate
             AND INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
            THEN ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS TodaySales,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN INV.InvoiceDate >= @LastMonthSameDate
             AND INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @LastMonthSameDate)
            THEN ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS LastMonthSameDaySales,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
            THEN ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS TotalSalesUptoDate

    FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV
    WHERE INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
    GROUP BY INV.ComUnitId
),

CollectionData AS
(
    SELECT
        INV.ComUnitId,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN CP.PaymentDate >= @CurrentMonthStart
             AND CP.PaymentDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
            THEN ISNULL(CPD.PaymentAmount, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.AIT, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.Discount, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS CurrentMonthCollection,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN CP.PaymentDate >= @LastMonthStart
             AND CP.PaymentDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @LastMonthSameDate)
            THEN ISNULL(CPD.PaymentAmount, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.AIT, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.Discount, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS LastMonthCollection,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN CP.PaymentDate >= @ReportDate
             AND CP.PaymentDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
            THEN ISNULL(CPD.PaymentAmount, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.AIT, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.Discount, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS TodayCollection,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN CP.PaymentDate >= @LastMonthSameDate
             AND CP.PaymentDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @LastMonthSameDate)
            THEN ISNULL(CPD.PaymentAmount, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.AIT, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.Discount, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS LastMonthSameDayCollection,

        SUM(CASE
            WHEN CP.PaymentDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
            THEN ISNULL(CPD.PaymentAmount, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.AIT, 0)
               + ISNULL(CPD.Discount, 0)
            ELSE 0
        END) AS TotalCollectionUptoDate

    FROM dbo.tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCustomerPay AS CP
        ON CP.CustPayId = CPD.CustPayId
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice AS INV
        ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
    WHERE CPD.InvoiceId IS NOT NULL
      AND CP.PaymentDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @ReportDate)
    GROUP BY INV.ComUnitId
),

ReturnData AS
(
    SELECT
        INV.ComUnitId,
        SUM(ISNULL(D.DebitAmountBDT, 0)) AS ReturnUptoDate
    FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS M
    LEFT JOIN CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS D
        ON M.VoucherMasterId = D.VoucherMasterId
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice AS INV
        ON M.CommonPK_ID = INV.InvoiceId
    WHERE ISNULL(M.IsInventoryValuation, 0) = 0
      AND M.TableID = 2018
    GROUP BY INV.ComUnitId
)

SELECT
    CASE
        WHEN GROUPING(CI.CompanyName) = 1 THEN ''Grand Total''
        ELSE CI.CompanyName
    END AS [Wings Name],

    SUM(ISNULL(S.CurrentMonthSales, 0)) AS [Sales],
    SUM(ISNULL(S.LastMonthSales, 0)) AS [Last Month Sales],
    SUM(ISNULL(S.TodaySales, 0)) AS ' + QUOTENAME(@TodaySalesHeader) + N',
    SUM(ISNULL(S.LastMonthSameDaySales, 0)) AS ' + QUOTENAME(@LastMonthSameDaySalesHeader) + N',

    SUM(ISNULL(C.CurrentMonthCollection, 0)) AS [Collection],
    SUM(ISNULL(C.LastMonthCollection, 0)) AS [Last Month Collection],
    SUM(ISNULL(C.TodayCollection, 0)) AS ' + QUOTENAME(@TodayCollectionHeader) + N',
    SUM(ISNULL(C.LastMonthSameDayCollection, 0)) AS ' + QUOTENAME(@LastMonthSameDayCollectionHeader) + N',

    SUM(ISNULL(S.TotalSalesUptoDate, 0))
  - SUM(ISNULL(C.TotalCollectionUptoDate, 0))
  - SUM(ISNULL(R.ReturnUptoDate, 0)) AS [Due]

FROM dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS CU
LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI
    ON CU.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
LEFT JOIN SalesData AS S
    ON CU.ComUnitId = S.ComUnitId
LEFT JOIN CollectionData AS C
    ON CU.ComUnitId = C.ComUnitId
LEFT JOIN ReturnData AS R
    ON CU.ComUnitId = R.ComUnitId

WHERE
       ISNULL(S.CurrentMonthSales, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(S.LastMonthSales, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(S.TodaySales, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(S.LastMonthSameDaySales, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(C.CurrentMonthCollection, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(C.LastMonthCollection, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(C.TodayCollection, 0) <> 0
    OR ISNULL(C.LastMonthSameDayCollection, 0) <> 0
    OR (
        ISNULL(S.TotalSalesUptoDate, 0)
      - ISNULL(C.TotalCollectionUptoDate, 0)
      - ISNULL(R.ReturnUptoDate, 0)
    ) <> 0

GROUP BY ROLLUP(CI.CompanyName)

ORDER BY
    GROUPING(CI.CompanyName),
    CI.CompanyName;
';

    EXEC sp_executesql
          @SQL
        , N'@ReportDate DATE,
             @CurrentMonthStart DATE,
             @LastMonthSameDate DATE,
             @LastMonthStart DATE'
        , @ReportDate = @ReportDate
        , @CurrentMonthStart = @CurrentMonthStart
        , @LastMonthSameDate = @LastMonthSameDate
        , @LastMonthStart = @LastMonthStart;
END;
```

## SP_Apps_WingsWiseSalesSummary

**Parameters:**
- `( @Month INT, @Year INT, @CustomerTypeId INT )`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblCompanyInfo, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblCustMaster, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and runs (via `sp_executesql`) a dynamic per-company sales summary for a given month/year and customer type: confirmed sales, in-transit (unconfirmed) sales, their total, and current-date invoice value, joining invoice/detail/company tables.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: company name, Sales, In Transit, Total (Sales + InTransit), and a dynamically-named "Current Date Invoice (...)" column, grouped/ordered by company.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_Apps_WingsWiseSalesSummary]
(
    @Month INT,
    @Year INT,
    @CustomerTypeId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @fDate DATE;
    DECLARE @tdate DATE;

    DECLARE @sql NVARCHAR(MAX);
    DECLARE @TodayHeader NVARCHAR(100);

    ---------------------------------------------------------
    -- First Date of Month
    ---------------------------------------------------------
    SET @fDate = DATEFROMPARTS(@Year, @Month, 1);

    ---------------------------------------------------------
    -- Last Date of Month
    ---------------------------------------------------------
    SET @tdate = EOMONTH(@fDate);

    ---------------------------------------------------------
    -- Dynamic Header
    ---------------------------------------------------------
    SET @TodayHeader = 'Current Date Invoice (' 
                     + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), GETDATE(), 105) + ')';

    ---------------------------------------------------------
    -- Dynamic Query
    ---------------------------------------------------------
    SET @sql = N'

    SELECT
        CI.CompanyName AS [Wings Name],

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Sales
        -------------------------------------------------
        ISNULL(SUM(
            CASE 
                WHEN INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'', ''Partial'')
                     AND INVD.Quantity > 0
                     AND INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0
                THEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount
                ELSE 0
            END
        ), 0) AS [Sales],

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- In Transit
        -------------------------------------------------
        ISNULL(SUM(
            CASE 
                WHEN INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NULL
                THEN INVD.UnitPrice * INVD.Quantity
                ELSE 0
            END
        ), 0) AS [In Transit],

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Total (Sales + In Transit)
        -------------------------------------------------
        ISNULL(SUM(
            CASE 
                WHEN INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'', ''Partial'')
                     AND INVD.Quantity > 0
                     AND INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0
                THEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount

                WHEN INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NULL
                THEN INVD.UnitPrice * INVD.Quantity

                ELSE 0
            END
        ), 0) AS [Total (Sales + InTransit)],

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Current Date Invoice
        -------------------------------------------------
        ISNULL(SUM(
            CASE 
                WHEN CAST(INV.InvoiceDate AS DATE) = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
                THEN INVD.UnitPrice * INVD.Quantity
                ELSE 0
            END
        ), 0) AS ' + QUOTENAME(@TodayHeader) + N'

    FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV

    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCustMaster AS CMS 
        ON INV.CustomerMasterId = CMS.CustomerMasterId

    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD 
        ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId

    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT 
        ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId

    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI 
        ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId

    WHERE
        (
            @CustomerTypeId <> 2
            OR CMS.CustomerTypeId = @CustomerTypeId
        )

        AND INV.InvoiceDate >= @fDate
        AND INV.InvoiceDate < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @tdate)

    GROUP BY
        CI.CompanyName

    ORDER BY
        CI.CompanyName;
    ';

    ---------------------------------------------------------
    -- Execute Dynamic SQL
    ---------------------------------------------------------
    EXEC sp_executesql 
        @sql,
        N'@fDate DATE, @tdate DATE, @CustomerTypeId INT',
        @fDate = @fDate,
        @tdate = @tdate,
        @CustomerTypeId = @CustomerTypeId;

END
```

## SP_DataMigration_Item

**Parameters:**
- `@ItemDescriptinID INT OUT`
- `@ProductCodePram NVARCHAR(200)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemCategory, CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemDescription, CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemGroup, CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemName, CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemType, Product, tblProCategory, tblProType, tblProduct
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemDescription, CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemName

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** One-time/ad-hoc data-migration helper: given a `@ProductCodePram`, finds one matching `tblProduct` row (via a cursor limited to TOP 1) that hasn't yet been migrated to the cross-database `CSTL_ERPDB` item catalog, creates the matching `tblItemName` row if missing, then creates a `tblItemDescription` row (with hardcoded defaults for brand/colour/size/manufacturer/etc.) if an equivalent description doesn't already exist.

**Result set:** No result set; returns the new/existing id via the `@ItemDescriptinID OUTPUT` parameter (or 0 if a duplicate was found).

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/ProductDAL.cs — `CretaeItem(Product aProduct)`, via `aInternalDal.SaveAction("SP_DataMigration_Item", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date>
-- Description:	Data migration from Product to ItemDescription
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_DataMigration_Item] 

	@ItemDescriptinID INT OUT,
	@ProductCodePram NVARCHAR(200)

AS
BEGIN


	-- Declare constants and default values

	DECLARE @ItemDescriptionCode NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
	
	DECLARE @BrandId INT = 1
	DECLARE @ColourId INT = 1
	DECLARE @SizeDetailId INT = 1
	DECLARE @ManufacturerId INT = 11
	DECLARE @CountryID INT = 107
	DECLARE @StockUnitId INT = 4
	DECLARE @DefaultUnitId INT = 21
	DECLARE @EntryBy NVARCHAR(MAX) = '160'
	DECLARE @EntryDate DATETIME = GETDATE()
	DECLARE @IsActive BIT = 1
	DECLARE @SizeId INT = NULL
	DECLARE @InventoryTypeId INT = 2

	DECLARE @IsPackApplicable BIT = 0
	DECLARE @IsExpireApplicable BIT = 1
	DECLARE @IsWarrentyApplicable BIT = 1

	-- Product details

	DECLARE @ProductCode NVARCHAR(500)
	DECLARE @ProductName NVARCHAR(500)
	DECLARE @ProductDescription NVARCHAR(500)
	DECLARE @Description NVARCHAR(500)
	DECLARE @ItemGroupId INT = NULL
	DECLARE @ItemTypeId INT = NULL
	DECLARE @ItemCategoryId INT = NULL

	DECLARE @ComId INT = 0
	DECLARE @TempProductId INT = 0

	-- Cursor to get products that need migration

	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR

	SELECT DISTINCT TOP 1 
		PD.CompanyId,
		PD.ProductId,
		PD.ProductCode,
		PD.ProductName,
		PD.ProductName + ' (' + PD.ProductCode + ')' AS ProductDescription,
		PD.Description,
		GP.ItemGroupId,
		IT.ItemTypeId,
		ITC.ItemCategoryId  
	FROM tblProduct AS PD
	LEFT JOIN tblProType AS PT ON PD.ProTypeId = PT.ProTypeId
	LEFT JOIN tblProCategory AS CTG ON PD.CategoryId = CTG.CategoryId
	LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemType AS IT ON LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(PT.ProTypeName))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(IT.ItemTypeName)))
	LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemCategory AS ITC ON LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(CTG.CategoryName))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(ITC.ItemCategoryName)))
	LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemGroup AS GP ON LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER('Medicine'))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(GP.ItemGroupName)))
	LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemDescription AS ID ON PD.ProductId = ID.TempProductId
	WHERE IT.ItemTypeId IS NOT NULL 
		AND ITC.ItemCategoryId IS NOT NULL 
		AND ID.TempProductId IS NULL 
		AND LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(PD.ProductCode))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@ProductCodePram)))
	ORDER BY PD.ProductCode

	-- Process cursor

	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor INTO 
		@ComId, @TempProductId, @ProductCode, @ProductName, @ProductDescription, 
		@Description, @ItemGroupId, @ItemTypeId, @ItemCategoryId

	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN

		DECLARE @ItemNameId INT = 0
		SELECT @ItemNameId = ISNULL(ItemNameId, 0)
		FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemName 
		WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(ItemName))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@ProductName)))

		-- Insert ItemName if not exists

		IF (@ItemNameId = 0)
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @ItemNameCode NVARCHAR(200) = 'ITE-' + @ProductCode
			INSERT INTO CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemName
				(ItemNameCode, ItemName, IsActive, ItemCategoryId, ComId) 
			VALUES
				(@ItemNameCode, @ProductName, @IsActive, @ItemCategoryId, @ComId)
			
			SET @ItemNameId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		END

		-- Check if ItemDescription already exists

		DECLARE @CheckCount INT = 0
		SELECT @CheckCount = COUNT(*) 
		FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemDescription 
		WHERE RTRIM(LTRIM(UPPER(ItemDescription))) = RTRIM(LTRIM(UPPER(@ProductDescription)))

		-- If not exists, insert new ItemDescription

		IF (@CheckCount = 0)
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @Code NVARCHAR(MAX)
			SET @Code = 
				(SELECT ShortName FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemGroup WHERE ItemGroupId = @ItemGroupId) + '-' +
				(SELECT ShortName FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemType WHERE ItemTypeId = @ItemTypeId) + '-' +
				(SELECT ShortName FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemCategory WHERE ItemCategoryId = @ItemCategoryId)

			SELECT @ItemDescriptionCode = @Code + '-' + 
				(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), (COUNT(ItemDescriptionId) + 1000001))) 
			FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemDescription

			INSERT INTO CSTL_ERPDB..tblItemDescription 
			(
				ItemDescriptionCode,
				ItemDescription,
				ItemNameId,
				BrandId,
				ColourId,
				SizeDetailId,
				ManufacturerId,
				CountryID,
				StockUnitId,
				DefaultUnitId,
				EntryBy,
				EntryDate,
				IsActive,
				SizeId,
				Description,
				IsPackApplicable,
				IsExpireApplicable,
				IsWarrentyApplicable,
				InventoryTypeId,
				ComId,
				TempProductId
			)
			VALUES 
			(
				@ItemDescriptionCode,
				@ProductDescription,
				@ItemNameId,
				@BrandId,
				@ColourId,
				@SizeDetailId,
				@ManufacturerId,
				@CountryID,
				@StockUnitId,
				@DefaultUnitId,
				@EntryBy,
				@EntryDate,
				@IsActive,
				@SizeId,
				@Description,
				@IsPackApplicable,
				@IsExpireApplicable,
				@IsWarrentyApplicable,
				@InventoryTypeId,
				@ComId,
				@TempProductId
			)

			SET @ItemDescriptinID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @ItemDescriptinID = 0
		END

		-- Next record

		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor INTO 
			@ComId, @TempProductId, @ProductCode, @ProductName, @ProductDescription, 
			@Description, @ItemGroupId, @ItemTypeId, @ItemCategoryId
	END

	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor


END
```

## SP_Upload_CustomerPayDetails

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Table_Temp_CustPayDetails, tblCustPayDetail, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** INSERT → tblCustPayDetail; UPDATE → INV, Invoice

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Batch-processes a staging table (`Table_Temp_CustPayDetails`) of uploaded customer payments: for each row not already present in `tblCustPayDetail`, inserts it there, then recomputes and updates the parent invoice's `PaymentAmount`/`AIT`/`DiscountOnPayment` totals from the aggregated payment-detail rows.

**Result set:** No result set (cursor-driven INSERT + UPDATE only).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_Upload_CustomerPayDetails]

AS
BEGIN

	--TRUNCATE TABLE SalesRollDB_ZAS..tbl_MC_ProductRawMaterialDetails

	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
    DECLARE @PaymentAmount DECIMAL(18,2)
    DECLARE @IsPrint BIT
    DECLARE @IsPosting BIT
    DECLARE @CashAccId INT
    DECLARE @BankAccId INT
    DECLARE @Discount DECIMAL(18,2)
    DECLARE @PaymentRemarks NVARCHAR(MAX)
    DECLARE @TransctionDetailId INT
    DECLARE @ProcessTime DATETIME
    DECLARE @AIT DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @CustPayId INT

    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------


	SELECT D.InvoiceId,D.PaymentAmount,D.IsPrint,D.IsPosting,D.CashAccId,D.BankAccId,
	D.Discount,D.PaymentRemarks,D.TransctionDetailId,D.ProcessTime,D.AIT, D.CustPayId FROM Table_Temp_CustPayDetails AS D
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON D.InvoiceId  =INV.InvoiceId
	LEFT JOIN tblCustPayDetail AS CPD ON D.CustPayId = CPD.CustPayId
	WHERE CPD.CustPayId IS NULL

	
    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO 
	   @InvoiceId
      ,@PaymentAmount
      ,@IsPrint
      ,@IsPosting
      ,@CashAccId
      ,@BankAccId
      ,@Discount
      ,@PaymentRemarks
      ,@TransctionDetailId
      ,@ProcessTime
      ,@AIT
	  ,@CustPayId
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN

	INSERT INTO tblCustPayDetail
           (InvoiceId
           ,PaymentAmount
           ,CustPayId
           ,IsPrint
           ,IsPosting
           ,CashAccId
           ,BankAccId
           ,Discount
           ,PaymentRemarks
           ,TransctionDetailId
           ,ProcessTime
           ,AIT)
     VALUES
           (@InvoiceId
           ,@PaymentAmount
           ,@CustPayId
           ,@IsPrint
           ,@IsPosting
           ,@CashAccId
           ,@BankAccId
           ,@Discount
           ,@PaymentRemarks
           ,@TransctionDetailId
           ,@ProcessTime
           ,@AIT)


		   -- Update Invoice

		UPDATE INV
		SET 
		    PaymentAmount = AGG.PaymentAmount,
		    AIT = AGG.AIT,
		    DiscountOnPayment = AGG.Discount
		FROM tblInvoice AS INV
		INNER JOIN (
		    SELECT 
		        InvoiceId,
		        SUM(ISNULL(PaymentAmount, 0)) AS PaymentAmount,
		        SUM(ISNULL(AIT, 0)) AS AIT,
		        SUM(ISNULL(Discount, 0)) AS Discount
		    FROM tblCustPayDetail
		    WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		    GROUP BY InvoiceId
		) AS AGG ON INV.InvoiceId = AGG.InvoiceId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId;
		
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId
      ,@PaymentAmount
      ,@IsPrint
      ,@IsPosting
      ,@CashAccId
      ,@BankAccId
      ,@Discount
      ,@PaymentRemarks
      ,@TransctionDetailId
      ,@ProcessTime
      ,@AIT
	  ,@CustPayId
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
          

END
```

## TargetReport

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** View_MioWiseInvoiceSummary, dbo.tblArea, dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblMIOInfo, dbo.tblTerritory, tblCompanyInfo, tblMIATargetProductWise, tblProduct
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Ad-hoc/hardcoded sales-vs-target report: joins `tblMIATargetProductWise` (targets) to a pre-aggregated `View_MioWiseInvoiceSummary` view on MIO/company/year/period/product, computing achievement percentage; the WHERE clause is hardcoded to a specific year ('2024'), period ('October') and MIA id (1284), and the from/to date columns are literal strings rather than parameters — this looks like a saved ad-hoc query rather than a general-purpose reusable procedure.

**Result set:** Single result set: company/unit/MIO/area/territory/product info, hardcoded from/to dates, quantity, value, target quantity, and achievement percentage.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for deleting an employee
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[TargetReport]
  

AS
BEGIN
   

   SELECT ISNULL(VM.CompanyId,TRGT.CompanyId) CompanyId ,ISNULL(VM.CompanyCode,CI.CompanyCode) CompanyCode , ISNULL(VM.CompanyName,CI.CompanyName) CompanyName,VM.ComUnitCode,VM.ComUnitName,ISNULL(VM.MIO,tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmpName) MIO,ISNULL(VM.AreaName,ARA.AreaName) AreaName, ISNULL(VM.TerritoryName,TTR.TerritoryName) TerritoryName,ISNULL(VM.ProductCode,PD.ProductCode) ProductCode,ISNULL(VM.ProductName,PD.ProductName) ProductName,ISNULL(VM.PackSize,PD.PackSize) PackSize,
'01-Oct-2024' AS FromDate, '20-Oct-2024' AS ToDate,ISNULL(VM.DeliveryTotalQuantity,0) Qty,ISNULL(VM.DeliveryNetAmount,0) Value,ISNULL(TRGT.TargetQty,0) TargetQty,
CASE WHEN ISNULL(TRGT.TargetQty,0) > 0 THEN (ISNULL(VM.DeliveryTotalQuantity,0)* 100) / ISNULL(TRGT.TargetQty,0) ELSE 0 END AS Achivment FROM tblMIATargetProductWise AS TRGT
LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON TRGT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId   
LEFT JOIN tblProduct AS PD ON TRGT.ProductId = PD.ProductId 
LEFT JOIN dbo.tblMIOInfo ON dbo.tblMIOInfo.MIOId=TRGT.MiaId 
LEFT JOIN dbo.tblTerritory AS TTR ON TTR.TerritoryId = tblMIOInfo.TerritoryId  
LEFT JOIN dbo.tblArea AS ARA ON ARA.AreaId = TTR.AreaId 
LEFT JOIN dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo ON dbo.tblMIOInfo.EmployeeId=dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmpInfoId  
LEFT JOIN (
SELECT VM.MIO, VM.Year, VM.MONTH, VM.ComUnitId, VM.MiaId, VM.CompanyId,VM.CompanyCode,VM.CompanyName, VM.ComUnitCode, VM.ComUnitName, VM.AreaName, VM.TerritoryName, VM.ProductId, VM.ProductCode, 
VM.ProductName, VM.PackSize, SUM(ISNULL(VM.DeliveryTotalQuantity, 0)) AS DeliveryTotalQuantity, SUM(ISNULL(VM.DeliveryNetAmount, 0)) AS DeliveryNetAmount, ISNULL(VM.TargetQty, 0) AS TargetQty 
FROM View_MioWiseInvoiceSummary AS VM 
GROUP BY VM.MIO, VM.Year, VM.MONTH, VM.ComUnitId, VM.MiaId, VM.CompanyId,VM.CompanyCode,VM.CompanyName, VM.ComUnitCode, VM.ComUnitName, VM.AreaName, VM.TerritoryName, VM.ProductId, VM.ProductCode, VM.ProductName, VM.PackSize, VM.TargetQty) 
AS VM ON TRGT.MiaId = VM.MiaId AND VM.CompanyId = TRGT.CompanyId 
AND TRGT.Year = VM.Year AND TRGT.Period = VM.MONTH AND TRGT.ProductId = VM.ProductId

WHERE TRGT.Year = DATENAME(year,'01-Oct-2024') AND TRGT.Period IN ('October') AND TRGT.MiaId = 1284

--UNION ALL

--SELECT VM.CompanyCode,VM.CompanyName,VM.ComUnitCode,VM.ComUnitName,VM.MIO,VM.AreaName, VM.TerritoryName,VM.ProductCode,VM.ProductName,VM.PackSize,
--'01-Oct-2024' AS FromDate, '20-Oct-2024' AS ToDate,SUM(ISNULL(VM.DeliveryTotalQuantity,0)) Qty,SUM(ISNULL(VM.DeliveryNetAmount,0)) Value,0 TargetQty,0 AS Achivment
--FROM View_MioWiseInvoiceSummary AS VM 
--WHERE VM.TargetQty = 0 AND VM.Year = '2024' AND VM.MONTH = 'October' AND VM.MiaId = 1284 
--GROUP BY VM.MIO, VM.Year, VM.MONTH, VM.ComUnitId, VM.CompanyId,VM.CompanyCode,VM.MiaId,VM.CompanyName, VM.ComUnitCode, VM.ComUnitName, VM.AreaName, VM.TerritoryName, VM.ProductId, VM.ProductCode, VM.ProductName, VM.PackSize, VM.TargetQty



END;
```

-- Create the stored procedure for updating an employee
CREATE PROCEDURE UpdateEmployee
    @EmployeeID INT,
    @FirstName NVARCHAR(50),
    @LastName NVARCHAR(50),
    @Department NVARCHAR(50),
    @Position NVARCHAR(50),

## spInsertTradeMaterialTransfer

**Parameters:**
- `@FromCompanyId INT`
- `@FromLocationId INT`
- `@FromProductId INT`
- `@FromProductCode NVARCHAR(100)`
- `@FromProductName NVARCHAR(300)`
- `@FromCurrentStockQty DECIMAL(18,3)`
- `@ToCompanyId INT`
- `@ToLocationId INT`
- `@ToItemDescriptionId INT`
- `@ToItemDescriptionCode NVARCHAR(100)`
- `@ToItemDescription NVARCHAR(300)`
- `@ToCurrentStockQty DECIMAL(18,3)`
- `@TransferQty DECIMAL(18,3)`
- `@Remarks NVARCHAR(500)`
- `@EntryBy NVARCHAR(100)`
- `@EntryDate DATETIME`
- `@TradeMaterialTransferId INT OUTPUT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblTradeMaterialTransfer
**Tables written:** INSERT → dbo.tblTradeMaterialTransfer

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Inserts a new inter-company/inter-location trade-material transfer record into `dbo.tblTradeMaterialTransfer`, generating a sequential `TransferCode` (prefix `TMT-YYMM` + next running number scoped to that prefix) and hardcoding the new row's `ApprovalStatus` to 'Posted'.

**Result set:** No result set; returns the new id via `@TradeMaterialTransferId OUTPUT` (`SCOPE_IDENTITY()`).

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/TradeMaterialTransferDal.cs — via `aInternalDal.SaveAction("spInsertTradeMaterialTransfer", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE   PROCEDURE dbo.spInsertTradeMaterialTransfer
    @FromCompanyId INT,
    @FromLocationId INT,
    @FromProductId INT,
    @FromProductCode NVARCHAR(100),
    @FromProductName NVARCHAR(300),
    @FromCurrentStockQty DECIMAL(18,3),
    @ToCompanyId INT,
    @ToLocationId INT,
    @ToItemDescriptionId INT,
    @ToItemDescriptionCode NVARCHAR(100),
    @ToItemDescription NVARCHAR(300),
    @ToCurrentStockQty DECIMAL(18,3),
    @TransferQty DECIMAL(18,3),
    @Remarks NVARCHAR(500),
    @EntryBy NVARCHAR(100),
    @EntryDate DATETIME,
    @TradeMaterialTransferId INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    -- DO NOT set SET NOCOUNT ON to allow row count messages back to Web Application
    DECLARE @Prefix NVARCHAR(10);
    DECLARE @NextSeq INT;
    DECLARE @GeneratedCode NVARCHAR(50);
    
    SET @Prefix = 'TMT-' + SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @EntryDate, 112), 3, 4);
    
    SELECT @NextSeq = ISNULL(MAX(CAST(RIGHT(TransferCode, 4) AS INT)), 1000) + 1 
    FROM dbo.tblTradeMaterialTransfer 
    WHERE TransferCode LIKE @Prefix + '%';
    
    SET @GeneratedCode = @Prefix + CAST(@NextSeq AS NVARCHAR(4));
    
    INSERT INTO dbo.tblTradeMaterialTransfer
    (
        TransferCode,
        FromCompanyId,
        FromLocationId,
        FromProductId,
        FromProductCode,
        FromProductName,
        FromCurrentStockQty,
        ToCompanyId,
        ToLocationId,
        ToItemDescriptionId,
        ToItemDescriptionCode,
        ToItemDescription,
        ToCurrentStockQty,
        TransferQty,
        Remarks,
        EntryBy,
        EntryDate,
        ApprovalStatus -- Auto-set to 'Posted' on Insert
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @GeneratedCode,
        @FromCompanyId,
        @FromLocationId,
        @FromProductId,
        @FromProductCode,
        @FromProductName,
        @FromCurrentStockQty,
        @ToCompanyId,
        @ToLocationId,
        @ToItemDescriptionId,
        @ToItemDescriptionCode,
        @ToItemDescription,
        @ToCurrentStockQty,
        @TransferQty,
        @Remarks,
        @EntryBy,
        @EntryDate,
        'Posted'
    );
    
    SET @TradeMaterialTransferId = SCOPE_IDENTITY();
END
```

## spUpdateTradeMaterialTransfer

**Parameters:**

- `@ToItemDescriptionCode NVARCHAR(100)`
- `@ToItemDescription NVARCHAR(300)`
