## sp_ADJ_VoucherMasterDetailPosting

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblCustPayDetail, dbo.tblCustomerPay, dbo.tblInvoice, tblCompanyUnit
**Tables written:** DELETE → ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Orchestrator with no parameters: cursors through every `ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster` row that has no matching detail rows yet, and — based on which id column is populated and keyword matches in the `Narration` text (e.g. '%Proforma Invoice (%', '%Delivery Confirmation Full -(%', '%Freeze Stock Release - (%') — dispatches to the matching `sp_ADJ_*` posting procedure to backfill the missing Debit/Credit detail rows for that voucher.

**Result set:** No result set (dispatch/orchestration only).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ADJ_VoucherMasterDetailPosting] 


AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT
	DECLARE @Narration NVARCHAR(MAX)
	DECLARE @ProformaId INT
	DECLARE @DcFreezeId INT
	DECLARE @DestroyId INT
	DECLARE @SalesReturnId INT
	DECLARE @CustPayDetailId INT
	        
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	---------------
	
	SELECT M.VoucherMasterId,M.Narration,M.ProformaId,M.DcFreezeId,M.DestroyId,M.SalesReturnId,M.CustPayDetailId FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS M
	LEFT JOIN ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS D ON D.VoucherMasterId = M.VoucherMasterId
    WHERE D.VoucherMasterId IS NULL 


	

	----------
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @VoucherMasterId
		,@Narration
		,@ProformaId
		,@DcFreezeId
		,@DestroyId
		,@SalesReturnId
		,@CustPayDetailId
	
	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN
	

	--DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	IF(@CustPayDetailId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_CustomerPaymentPosting 
		     @PayDetailID = @CustPayDetailId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END


	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Proforma Invoice (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_ProformaPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END
	

	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Proforma Invoice Return (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_ProformaReturnPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END


	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Confirmation Full -(%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Sales'

	END


	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Confirmation Full, Inventory Valuation - (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Inventory'

	END

	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Confirmation Partial -(%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Sales'

	END

	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Confirmation Partial, Inventory Valuation - (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Inventory'

	END

	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Confirmation Partial , Return Stock Valuation - (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Freeze'

	END


	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Proforma Full Reject%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_ProformaFullReturnPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @ProformaId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END

	IF(@DestroyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_ProductDestroyPosting 
		     @DestroyId = @DestroyId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END

	IF(@DcFreezeId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Freeze Stock  - (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_FreezeStockPosting 
		     @DcStockConditionId = @DcFreezeId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END
	
	IF(@DcFreezeId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Freeze Stock Release - (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_FreezeStockReleasePosting
		     @FreezeId = @DcFreezeId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	END

	
	IF(@SalesReturnId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Sales Return -(%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliverySalesReturnPosting
		     @ReturnId = @SalesReturnId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Sales'

	END

	IF(@ProformaId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Sales Return, Inventory Valuation - ( %')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliverySalesReturnPosting
		     @ReturnId = @SalesReturnId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Inventory'

	END


	IF(@SalesReturnId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Invoice Delete -(%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting
		     @InvoiceId = @SalesReturnId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Sales'

	END


	IF(@SalesReturnId IS NOT NULL AND @Narration LIKE '%Delivery Invoice Delete, Inventory Valuation - (%')
	BEGIN	

		EXEC sp_ADJ_DeliveryInvoiceDeletePosting
		     @InvoiceId = @SalesReturnId,@VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId,@Type = 'Inventory'

	END

	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @VoucherMasterId
		,@Narration
		,@ProformaId
		,@DcFreezeId
		,@DestroyId
		,@SalesReturnId
		,@CustPayDetailId
	
	END
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor

	
END





--SELECT CPD.PaymentAmount,CPY.CustomerMasterId,CPD.InvoiceId,CPD.CustPayDetailId,CPY.PaymentDate,UNT.CompanyId,CPD.Discount,InvoiceNo,PaymentStatus,CPD.BankId FROM dbo.tblCustPayDetail AS CPD 
--	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCustomerPay AS CPY ON CPD.CustPayId = CPY.CustPayId
--	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice ON tblInvoice.InvoiceId = CPD.InvoiceId
--	INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = tblInvoice.ComUnitId
```

## sp_Acc_ErrorPosting

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Diagnostic/reconciliation query with no parameters: three UNION ALL blocks compare (1) proforma invoice value vs posted accounting voucher value, (2) delivered/sales invoice value vs posted voucher value, and (3) customer payment amount vs posted voucher value, each restricted to `CompanyId = 16` and dates after 2024-07-01, surfacing rows where the ledger total doesn't match the source transaction (a hardcoded error-finder, not a general-purpose reusable report).

**Result set:** Three unioned result sets combined into one: entity id, code/no, expected value, posted VoucherMasterId/value/code, and a `VType` label ('Proforma'/'Sales'/'Payment') for rows that are missing or mismatched.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_ErrorPosting] 

	
AS
BEGIN


--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice AS INV
--LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS IVD ON INV.InvoiceId = IVD.InvoiceId
--WHERE INV.InvoiceId = 494768

--SELECT * FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS DVB
--LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS DVD ON DVB.VoucherMasterId = DVD.VoucherMasterId
--WHERE DVB.VoucherMasterId = 502138 

--AND Narration LIKE '%Proforma Invoice%' 
--GROUP BY DVB.ProformaId,DVB.VoucherMasterId




SELECT INV.InvoiceId,INV.InvoiceNo, SUM(INVD.TotalPrice) AS ProformaValue,VCR.ProformaId,VCR.PostingValue ,VoucherMasterId, 'Proforma' VType,VoucherCode
FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT DVB.VoucherMasterId,DVB.VoucherCode ,DVB.ProformaId, SUM(DVD.DebitAmountBDT) AS PostingValue 
    FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS DVB
    LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS DVD ON DVB.VoucherMasterId = DVD.VoucherMasterId
    WHERE PostingDate > '2024-07-01'  AND Narration LIKE '%Proforma Invoice%' GROUP BY DVB.ProformaId,DVB.VoucherMasterId, DVB.VoucherCode
) AS VCR ON INV.InvoiceId = VCR.ProformaId
WHERE InvoiceDate > '2024-07-01'  AND CompanyId = 16
GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,INV.InvoiceNo, VCR.ProformaId, VCR.PostingValue,VoucherMasterId, VoucherCode
HAVING VCR.ProformaId IS NULL OR SUM(INVD.TotalPrice) != VCR.PostingValue

UNION ALL

SELECT INV.InvoiceId,DelivaryInvoiceNo ,SUM(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice) AS ProformaValue,VCR.ProformaId,VCR.PostingValue,VoucherMasterId, 'Sales' VType,VoucherCode
FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
LEFT JOIN (
   SELECT DVB.VoucherMasterId,DVB.ProformaId,DVB.VoucherCode,SUM(DVD.DebitAmountBDT) AS PostingValue FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS DVB
LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS DVD ON DVB.VoucherMasterId = DVD.VoucherMasterId
WHERE PostingDate > '2024-07-01'  AND (Narration LIKE '%Delivery Sales Confirmation Full%' OR Narration LIKE '%Delivery Sales Confirmation Partial%')
GROUP BY DVB.ProformaId,DVB.TotalAmountBDT,DVB.VoucherMasterId, DVB.VoucherCode
) AS VCR ON INV.InvoiceId = VCR.ProformaId
WHERE InvoiceDate > '2024-07-01'  AND DelivaryInvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND CompanyId = 16
GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,DelivaryInvoiceNo, VCR.ProformaId,DelivaryInvoiceNo, VCR.PostingValue,VoucherMasterId,VoucherCode
HAVING SUM(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice) > 0 AND (VCR.ProformaId IS NULL OR SUM(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice) != ISNULL(VCR.PostingValue,0))

UNION ALL

SELECT CPD.CustPayDetailId,CP.PaymentCode,CPD.PaymentAmount+CPD.Discount+CPD.AIT AS PaymentAmount,VCR.CustPayDetailId,VCR.PostingValue,VoucherMasterId,  'Payment' VType, VoucherCode FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD 
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId

LEFT JOIN (SELECT DVB.VoucherMasterId,DVB.VoucherCode,DVB.CustPayDetailId,SUM(DVD.DebitAmountBDT) AS PostingValue FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS DVB
LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS DVD ON DVB.VoucherMasterId = DVD.VoucherMasterId
WHERE PostingDate > '2024-07-01'  AND Narration LIKE '%Customer Payment%' AND CompanyInfoId = 16
GROUP BY DVB.VoucherMasterId,DVB.CustPayDetailId,DVB.TotalAmountBDT,DVB.VoucherCode) AS VCR ON VCR.CustPayDetailId = CPD.CustPayDetailId

WHERE PaymentDate > '2024-07-01'  AND CompanyId = 16 
AND (VCR.CustPayDetailId IS NULL OR (CPD.PaymentAmount+CPD.Discount+CPD.AIT) != VCR.PostingValue)


	

	
END






--SELECT DVB.VoucherMasterId,SUM(DebitAmountBDT) DebitAmountBDT, SUM(CreditAmountBDT) CreditAmountBDT FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS DVB
--LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail AS DVD ON DVB.VoucherMasterId = DVD.VoucherMasterId
--WHERE PostingDate BETWEEN '2024-07-01' AND '2024-10-07' GROUP BY DVB.VoucherMasterId
--HAVING SUM(CreditAmountBDT) - SUM(DebitAmountBDT) != 0
```

## sp_Acc_MaterialConversionReceive

**Parameters:**
- `@ApprovalStatus NVARCHAR(200) = NULL`
- `@MasterId INT = 0`
- `@ApprovalBy NVARCHAR(200) = NULL`
- `@ApprovalDate DATETIME = NULL`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity, curMC, dbo.tblCentralStore, dbo.tblProduct, dbo.tblWHStockInDetail, dbo.tblWHStockInMaster, dbo.tblWearhouse, dbo.tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg, dbo.tbl_INV_Central_BatchWiseStock, tblWHStockInMaster
**Tables written:** INSERT → Batch, CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblCentralStore, dbo.tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg, dbo.tbl_INV_Central_BatchWiseStock, tblCentralStore, tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** On `@ApprovalStatus IN ('ACCEPTED','APPROVED')`, for a warehouse stock-in master (`@MasterId`): (1) if not already posted, creates a voucher header + Debit(Finished Goods 4156)/Credit(Material Conversion Clearing 5882) pair sized to the stock-in's total value, then (2) cursors through each stock-in detail line and inserts/updates the physical central-store stock (`tblCentralStore`), the weighted-average ledger (`tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg`), and per-batch stock (`tbl_INV_Central_BatchWiseStock`), computing opening/closing quantity and weighted-average rate for each product.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/WarehouseStockInApprovalDal.cs — via `aCommonInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_Acc_MaterialConversionReceive", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =======================================================================================
-- sp_Acc_MaterialConversionReceive 
-- Purpose : Material Conversion Approval, Voucher (DR 4156 / CR 5882) & Central Store Stock In
-- =======================================================================================
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_MaterialConversionReceive] 
	@ApprovalStatus NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@MasterId INT = 0,
	@ApprovalBy NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@ApprovalDate DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	IF(LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@ApprovalStatus))) IN ('ACCEPTED', 'APPROVED'))
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT, @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX), @CompanyId INT = 0, @FinancialYearId INT;
		DECLARE @CurrencyId INT = 1, @Conversionrate DECIMAL(18,4) = 1.00, @VoucherType NVARCHAR(200) ='Journal';
		DECLARE @ApproveDate DATETIME = ISNULL(@ApprovalDate, GETDATE());
		DECLARE @ApproveBy NVARCHAR(200) = ISNULL(@ApprovalBy, 'System');
		DECLARE @TotalValue DECIMAL(18,4) = 0.00, @WHStockInCode NVARCHAR(200), @StockInDate DATETIME;

		-- Fetch Master Info from tblWHStockInMaster and tblWearhouse
		SELECT 
			@WHStockInCode = WHSM.WHStockInCode,
			@StockInDate = WHSM.WHStockInDate,
			@CompanyId = ISNULL(WH.CompanyId, 16),
			@TotalValue = ISNULL(WHSM.TotalValue, 0)
		FROM dbo.tblWHStockInMaster AS WHSM WITH (NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblWearhouse AS WH WITH (NOLOCK) ON WHSM.WarehouseId = WH.WearhouseId
		WHERE WHSM.WHStockInMasterID = @MasterId;

		SELECT TOP 1 @FinancialYearId = FinancialYearId 
		FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND @StockInDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate;

		-- 1. Accounts Voucher Posting (DR 4156 Finished Goods / CR 5882 Material Conversion Clearing)
		IF (@TotalValue > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF NOT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH (NOLOCK)
				WHERE CommonPK_ID = @MasterId AND TableID = 1014 AND ActionStatus <> 'Cancelled'
			)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @year NVARCHAR(50) = SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), YEAR(@StockInDate)), 3, 2);
				DECLARE @month NVARCHAR(50) = RIGHT('0' + CAST(MONTH(@StockInDate) AS NVARCHAR(2)), 2);
				DECLARE @day NVARCHAR(50) = RIGHT('0' + CAST(DAY(@StockInDate) AS NVARCHAR(2)), 2);

				DECLARE @MaxVoucherMasterId INT;
				SELECT @MaxVoucherMasterId = ISNULL(MAX(VoucherMasterId), 0) + 1 FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH (NOLOCK);

				SET @VoucherCode = 'JVP-' + @year + @month + @day + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @MaxVoucherMasterId);

				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
				(
					VoucherCode, CompanyInfoId, FinancialYearId, PostingDate, VoucherCategoryID, CurrencyId, CurrencyCovRate,
					Narration, PaytoIsSupplier, IsInvoice, TotalAmountBDT, TotalAmount, Remarks, EntryBy, EntryDate, VoucherType,
					ActionStatus, IsCostCenter, IsBuyerCostCenter, IsOtherCostCenter, ApprovedBy, ApprovedDate, CommonPK_ID, TableID, IsInventoryValuation
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@VoucherCode, @CompanyId, @FinancialYearId, @StockInDate, 2, @CurrencyId, @Conversionrate,
					'Material Conversion Stock In for Reference No: ' + ISNULL(@WHStockInCode, ''), 0, 0, @TotalValue, @TotalValue,
					'Auto Posting', @ApproveBy, @ApproveDate, @VoucherType, 'Approved', 0, 0, 0, @ApproveBy, @ApproveDate, @MasterId, 1014, 1
				);

				SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

				-- DR: Finished Goods Inventory (GL 4156)
				DECLARE @DrGl INT = 0;
				SELECT @DrGl = GLId FROM CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity WHERE PageId = 1014 AND FlagId = 1014 AND IsInventory = 1;
				IF ISNULL(@DrGl, 0) = 0 SET @DrGl = 4156;

				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail (VoucherMasterId, AccountId, DebitorCredit, DebitAmount, CreditAmount, DebitAmountBDT, CreditAmountBDT, Remarks)
				VALUES (@VoucherMasterId, @DrGl, 'Debit', 0.00, 0.00, @TotalValue, 0.00, 'Finished Goods Inventory Stock In');

				-- CR: Material Conversion Clearing (GL 5882)
				DECLARE @CrGl INT = 0;
				SELECT @CrGl = GLId FROM CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity WHERE PageId = 1014 AND FlagId = 1021 AND IsInventory = 1;
				IF ISNULL(@CrGl, 0) = 0 SET @CrGl = 5882;

				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail (VoucherMasterId, AccountId, DebitorCredit, DebitAmountBDT, CreditAmountBDT, DebitAmount, CreditAmount, Remarks)
				VALUES (@VoucherMasterId, @CrGl, 'Credit', 0.00, @TotalValue, 0.00, 0.00, 'Material Conversion Clearing');
			END;
		END;

		-- 2. Physical Stock In into tblCentralStore & Weighted Average Ledgers
		DECLARE @ProductId INT, @ProductCode NVARCHAR(200), @ProductName NVARCHAR(MAX), @PackSize NVARCHAR(200);
		DECLARE @FinalOutputQuantity DECIMAL(18,4), @ChallanNo NVARCHAR(200), @ChallanDate DATETIME;
		DECLARE @CostPerUnit DECIMAL(18,4), @TotalPrice DECIMAL(18,4), @MaterialConversionMasterId INT;
		DECLARE @WarehouseId INT, @WHStockInDetailID INT, @Batch NVARCHAR(200), @MfgDate DATETIME, @ExpDate DATETIME;
		DECLARE @VATPerUnit DECIMAL(18,4) = 0.00, @TotalVAT DECIMAL(18,4) = 0.00, @StockCondition NVARCHAR(200) = 'Available', @ProductStockType NVARCHAR(200) = 'Sound';

		DECLARE curMC CURSOR LOCAL FAST_FORWARD FOR
		SELECT 
			WHSD.ProductId, P.ProductCode, P.ProductName, P.PackSize, WHSD.Qty,
			WHSM.ChallanNo, WHSM.ChallanDate, WHSD.Price, WHSD.TotalAmount,
			ISNULL(WHSD.MaterialConversionMasterId, 0), WHSM.WarehouseId,
			WHSD.WHStockInDetailID, WHSD.Batch, WHSD.MfgDate, WHSD.ExpDate,
			WHSD.VAT
		FROM dbo.tblWHStockInMaster WHSM WITH (NOLOCK)
		INNER JOIN dbo.tblWHStockInDetail WHSD WITH (NOLOCK) ON WHSM.WHStockInMasterID = WHSD.WHStockInMasterID
		INNER JOIN dbo.tblProduct P WITH (NOLOCK) ON WHSD.ProductId = P.ProductId
		WHERE WHSM.WHStockInMasterID = @MasterId;

		OPEN curMC;
		FETCH NEXT FROM curMC INTO 
			@ProductId, @ProductCode, @ProductName, @PackSize, @FinalOutputQuantity,
			@ChallanNo, @ChallanDate, @CostPerUnit, @TotalPrice,
			@MaterialConversionMasterId, @WarehouseId,
			@WHStockInDetailID, @Batch, @MfgDate, @ExpDate, @VATPerUnit;

		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
		BEGIN
			-- Calculate Central Store Opening and Weighted Average Rate
			DECLARE @Opening_Qty DECIMAL(18,3) = 0, @Opening_Amount DECIMAL(18,4) = 0, @Opening_Rate DECIMAL(18,4) = 0;
			DECLARE @Closing_Qty DECIMAL(18,3) = 0, @Closing_Amount DECIMAL(18,4) = 0, @Average_Rate DECIMAL(18,4) = 0;

			SELECT TOP 1 
				@Opening_Qty = ISNULL(Close_Qty, 0),
				@Opening_Amount = ISNULL(Close_Amt, 0)
			FROM dbo.tblCentralStore WITH (NOLOCK)
			WHERE Company_Id = @CompanyId AND ProductId = @ProductId
			ORDER BY Trans_Date DESC, ReceiveId DESC;

			SET @Opening_Rate = CASE WHEN @Opening_Qty > 0 THEN @Opening_Amount / @Opening_Qty ELSE 0 END;
			SET @Closing_Qty = @Opening_Qty + @FinalOutputQuantity;
			SET @Closing_Amount = @Opening_Amount + @TotalPrice;
			SET @Average_Rate = CASE WHEN @Closing_Qty > 0 THEN @Closing_Amount / @Closing_Qty ELSE 0 END;

			-- Insert into tblCentralStore
			IF NOT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tblCentralStore WITH (NOLOCK) 
				WHERE Mst_Trans_Id = @MasterId AND Trans_Id = @WHStockInDetailID AND ProductId = @ProductId
			)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO dbo.tblCentralStore
				(
					ProductId, ProductCode, ProductName, PackSize, BatchNo, Quantity,
					MfgDate, ExpDate, ReceiveDate, ChalanNo, ChalanDate, StockInQty,
					UnitPrice, TotalPrice, VATPerUnit, TotalVAT, TotalAmount, StockCondition,
					MigoDetailID, ProductStockType, InternalNoteNo, WarehouseId, Remarks, MaterialConversionMasterId,
					Company_Id, Mst_Trans_Id, Trans_Id, Ref_No, Trans_Date, Actual_Date, Entry_Date,
					Trans_Type_Id, Inv_Type_Id, Inv_Type, OP_Qty, OP_Rate, OP_Amt,
					Curr_Qty, Curr_Rate, Curr_Amt, Close_Qty, Avg_Rate, Close_Amt
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@ProductId, @ProductCode, @ProductName, @PackSize, @Batch, @FinalOutputQuantity,
					@MfgDate, @ExpDate, @StockInDate, @ChallanNo, @ChallanDate, @FinalOutputQuantity,
					@CostPerUnit, @TotalPrice, @VATPerUnit, (@VATPerUnit * @FinalOutputQuantity), @TotalPrice, @StockCondition,
					@WHStockInDetailID, @ProductStockType, @WHStockInCode, @WarehouseId, N'Material Conversion', @MaterialConversionMasterId,
					@CompanyId, @MasterId, @WHStockInDetailID, @WHStockInCode, GETDATE(), @StockInDate, @ApproveDate,
					1, 1, 'Finished Goods', @Opening_Qty, @Opening_Rate, @Opening_Amount,
					@FinalOutputQuantity, @CostPerUnit, @TotalPrice, @Closing_Qty, @Average_Rate, @Closing_Amount
				);
			END;

			-- Insert into tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg
			IF NOT EXISTS (
				SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg WITH (NOLOCK)
				WHERE Mst_Trans_Id = @MasterId AND Trans_Id = @WHStockInDetailID AND Company_Id = @CompanyId AND Item_Id = @ProductId
			)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO dbo.tbl_INV_CentralStore_WeightedAvg
				(
					Mst_Trans_Id, Trans_Id, Item_Code, Ref_No, Trans_Date, Actual_Date, Entry_Date,
					Trans_Type_Id, Inv_Type_Id, Inv_Type, Company_Id, Warehouse_Id, Item_Id,
					OP_Qty, OP_Rate, OP_Amt, Curr_Qty, Curr_Rate, Curr_Amt, Close_Qty, Avg_Rate, Close_Amt,
					Weighted_Avg_Id_Issue
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@MasterId, @WHStockInDetailID, @ProductCode, @WHStockInCode, GETDATE(), @StockInDate, @ApproveDate,
					1, 1, 'Finished Goods', @CompanyId, @WarehouseId, @ProductId,
					@Opening_Qty, @Opening_Rate, @Opening_Amount, @FinalOutputQuantity, @CostPerUnit, @TotalPrice,
					@Closing_Qty, @Average_Rate, @Closing_Amount, NULL
				);

				DECLARE @Weighted_Avg_Id_New INT = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

				-- Insert into Batch Stock
				IF (@Weighted_Avg_Id_New > 0)
				BEGIN
					IF NOT EXISTS (
						SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_INV_Central_BatchWiseStock WITH (NOLOCK)
						WHERE Weighted_Avg_Id = @Weighted_Avg_Id_New AND Batch_No = @Batch AND WHStockInDetailId = @WHStockInDetailID
					)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO dbo.tbl_INV_Central_BatchWiseStock
						(
							Weighted_Avg_Id, Batch_No, Mfg_Date, Exp_Date, Trans_Qty, Remarks, WHStockInDetailId
						)
						VALUES
						(
							@Weighted_Avg_Id_New, @Batch, @MfgDate, @ExpDate, @FinalOutputQuantity, N'WH Stock In', @WHStockInDetailID
						);
					END;
				END;
			END;

			FETCH NEXT FROM curMC INTO 
				@ProductId, @ProductCode, @ProductName, @PackSize, @FinalOutputQuantity,
				@ChallanNo, @ChallanDate, @CostPerUnit, @TotalPrice,
				@MaterialConversionMasterId, @WarehouseId,
				@WHStockInDetailID, @Batch, @MfgDate, @ExpDate, @VATPerUnit;
		END;

		CLOSE curMC;
		DEALLOCATE curMC;
	END;
END;
```

## sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnPayable

**Parameters:**
- `@ApprovalStatus NVARCHAR(200)`
- `@ApprovalBy NVARCHAR(200)`
- `@ApprovalDate DATETIME`
- `@MasterId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ACCDB..tblSubsidiaryInfo, CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblNonTranscationalInvoiceMaster
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** On `@ApprovalStatus IN ('ACCEPTED','APPROVED')`, for a non-transactional invoice return (`@MasterId`) with a positive total value and no existing 'Non-Transactional Invoice Return Payable' voucher, creates a voucher header + Debit(Non-Transactional Return Clearing 5893)/Credit(Accounts Payable 1053, tagged to the customer's subsidiary ledger) pair for the return value.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =========================================================================
-- 3. sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnPayable (CR Accounts Payable 1053)
-- =========================================================================
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnPayable] 
	@ApprovalStatus NVARCHAR(200),
	@ApprovalBy NVARCHAR(200),
	@ApprovalDate DATETIME,
	@MasterId INT
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@ApprovalStatus))) IN ('ACCEPTED', 'APPROVED'))
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @CompanyId INT, @ComUnitId INT, @FinancialYearId INT, @CustomerMasterId INT;
		DECLARE @ReturnCode NVARCHAR(200), @ReturnDate DATETIME, @TotalReturnValue DECIMAL(18,4);
		DECLARE @TableId INT = 2023; -- Non-Transactional Return TableID

		SELECT 
			@CompanyId = CU.CompanyId,
			@ComUnitId = NTM.ComUnitId,
			@CustomerMasterId = NTM.CustomerMasterId,
			@ReturnCode = NTM.ReturnCode,
			@ReturnDate = NTM.ReturnDate,
			@TotalReturnValue = ISNULL(NTM.TotalValue, 0)
		FROM dbo.tblNonTranscationalInvoiceMaster AS NTM WITH(NOLOCK)
		INNER JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS CU WITH(NOLOCK) ON CU.ComUnitId = NTM.ComUnitId
		WHERE NTM.InvReturnMasterID = @MasterId;

		IF (ISNULL(@TotalReturnValue, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			SELECT TOP 1 @FinancialYearId = FinancialYearId 
			FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear WITH(NOLOCK)
			WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND @ReturnDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate;

			IF NOT EXISTS
			(
				SELECT 1 FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE CommonPK_ID = @MasterId AND TableID = @TableId AND ActionStatus <> 'Cancelled'
				  AND Narration LIKE '%Non-Transactional Invoice Return Payable%'
			)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE	@DrGl INT = 0, @CrGl INT = 0, @SubsidiaryInfoId INT = 0;
				SELECT @DrGl = GLId FROM CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity WHERE PageId = 150 AND FlagId = 1031 AND IsInventory != 1;
				SELECT @CrGl = GLId FROM CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity WHERE PageId = 150 AND FlagId = 1 AND IsInventory != 1;

				IF ISNULL(@DrGl, 0) = 0 SET @DrGl = 5893; -- Non-Transactional Return Clearing
				IF ISNULL(@CrGl, 0) = 0 SET @CrGl = 1053; -- Accounts Payable

				SELECT TOP 1 @SubsidiaryInfoId = SB.SubsidiaryInfoId 
				FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblSubsidiaryInfo AS SB WITH(NOLOCK) 
				WHERE SB.SubsidiaryTypeId = 1 AND SB.S_Info_Id = @CustomerMasterId;

				DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(10) = SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), YEAR(@ApprovalDate)), 3, 2);
				DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(10) = RIGHT('0' + CAST(MONTH(@ApprovalDate) AS NVARCHAR(2)), 2);
				DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(10) = RIGHT('0' + CAST(DAY(@ApprovalDate) AS NVARCHAR(2)), 2);

				DECLARE @MaxNum INT = 10000;
				SELECT @MaxNum = ISNULL(MAX(VoucherMasterId), 10000) FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK);

				DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(50) = 'JVP-' + @yearText + @monthText + @dateText + CAST((@MaxNum % 89999) + 10002 AS NVARCHAR(10));

				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
				(
					VoucherCode, CompanyInfoId, FinancialYearId, PostingDate, VoucherCategoryID, CurrencyId, CurrencyCovRate,
					Narration, PaytoIsSupplier, IsInvoice, TotalAmountBDT, TotalAmount, Remarks, EntryBy, EntryDate, VoucherType,
					ActionStatus, IsCostCenter, IsBuyerCostCenter, IsOtherCostCenter, ApprovedBy, ApprovedDate, CommonPK_ID, TableID
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@VoucherCode, @CompanyId, @FinancialYearId, @ApprovalDate, 2, 1, 1.00,
					'An amount of BDT ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @TotalReturnValue) + ' has been created as payable to customer for Non-Transactional Return Under Return Code ' + @ReturnCode + '.',
					0, 0, @TotalReturnValue, @TotalReturnValue, 'Auto Posting', @ApprovalBy, @ApprovalDate, 'Journal',
					'Approved', 0, 0, 0, 'Auto Process', @ApprovalDate, @MasterId, @TableId
				);

				DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

				-- DR Non-Transactional Return Clearing (5893)
				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail (VoucherMasterId, AccountId, DebitorCredit, DebitAmount, CreditAmount, DebitAmountBDT, CreditAmountBDT, Remarks)
				VALUES (@VoucherMasterId, @DrGl, 'Debit', 0.00, 0.00, @TotalReturnValue, 0.00, 'Non-Transactional Invoice Return Payable');

				-- CR Accounts Payable (1053)
				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail (VoucherMasterId, AccountId, DebitorCredit, SubsidoryLedgerId, DebitAmountBDT, CreditAmountBDT, DebitAmount, CreditAmount, Remarks)
				VALUES (@VoucherMasterId, @CrGl, 'Credit', @SubsidiaryInfoId, 0.00, @TotalReturnValue, 0.00, 0.00, 'Non-Transactional Invoice Return Payable');
			END;
		END;
	END;
END;
```

## sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnReceive

**Parameters:**
- `@ApprovalStatus NVARCHAR(200)`
- `@ApprovalBy NVARCHAR(200)`
- `@ApprovalDate DATETIME`
- `@MasterId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblNonTranscationalInvoiceMaster
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Counterpart to `sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnPayable`: on approval, if no existing 'Non-Transactional Invoice Return Stock Receive' voucher exists for the master, creates a voucher header + Debit(Finished Goods 4156)/Credit(Non-Transactional Return Clearing 5893) pair for the return's stock value.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =========================================================================
-- 2. sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnReceive (DR Finished Goods FG 4156)
-- =========================================================================
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_NonTranscationalInvoiceReturnReceive] 
	@ApprovalStatus NVARCHAR(200),
	@ApprovalBy NVARCHAR(200),
	@ApprovalDate DATETIME,
	@MasterId INT
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@ApprovalStatus))) IN ('ACCEPTED', 'APPROVED'))
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @CompanyId INT, @ComUnitId INT, @FinancialYearId INT;
		DECLARE @ReturnCode NVARCHAR(200), @ReturnDate DATETIME, @TotalReturnValue DECIMAL(18,4);
		DECLARE @TableId INT = 2023; -- Non-Transactional Return TableID

		SELECT 
			@CompanyId = CU.CompanyId,
			@ComUnitId = NTM.ComUnitId,
			@ReturnCode = NTM.ReturnCode,
			@ReturnDate = NTM.ReturnDate,
			@TotalReturnValue = ISNULL(NTM.TotalValue, 0)
		FROM dbo.tblNonTranscationalInvoiceMaster AS NTM WITH(NOLOCK)
		INNER JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS CU WITH(NOLOCK) ON CU.ComUnitId = NTM.ComUnitId
		WHERE NTM.InvReturnMasterID = @MasterId;

		IF (ISNULL(@TotalReturnValue, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			SELECT TOP 1 @FinancialYearId = FinancialYearId 
			FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear WITH(NOLOCK)
			WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND @ReturnDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate;

			IF NOT EXISTS
			(
				SELECT 1 FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE CommonPK_ID = @MasterId AND TableID = @TableId AND ActionStatus <> 'Cancelled'
				  AND Narration LIKE '%Non-Transactional Invoice Return Stock Receive%'
			)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE	@DrGl INT = 0, @CrGl INT = 0;
				SELECT @DrGl = GLId FROM CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity WHERE PageId = 150 AND FlagId = 1014 AND IsInventory = 1;
				SELECT @CrGl = GLId FROM CSTL_ERPDB..Table_Integration_AccountsConnectivity WHERE PageId = 150 AND FlagId = 1031 AND IsInventory = 1;

				IF ISNULL(@DrGl, 0) = 0 SET @DrGl = 4156; -- Finished Goods Inventory FG
				IF ISNULL(@CrGl, 0) = 0 SET @CrGl = 5893; -- Non-Transactional Return Clearing

				DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(10) = SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), YEAR(@ApprovalDate)), 3, 2);
				DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(10) = RIGHT('0' + CAST(MONTH(@ApprovalDate) AS NVARCHAR(2)), 2);
				DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(10) = RIGHT('0' + CAST(DAY(@ApprovalDate) AS NVARCHAR(2)), 2);

				DECLARE @MaxNum INT = 10000;
				SELECT @MaxNum = ISNULL(MAX(VoucherMasterId), 10000) FROM CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK);

				DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(50) = 'JVP-' + @yearText + @monthText + @dateText + CAST((@MaxNum % 89999) + 10001 AS NVARCHAR(10));

				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
				(
					VoucherCode, CompanyInfoId, FinancialYearId, PostingDate, VoucherCategoryID, CurrencyId, CurrencyCovRate,
					Narration, PaytoIsSupplier, IsInvoice, TotalAmountBDT, TotalAmount, Remarks, EntryBy, EntryDate, VoucherType,
					ActionStatus, IsCostCenter, IsBuyerCostCenter, IsOtherCostCenter, ApprovedBy, ApprovedDate, CommonPK_ID, TableID
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@VoucherCode, @CompanyId, @FinancialYearId, @ApprovalDate, 2, 1, 1.00,
					'An amount of BDT ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @TotalReturnValue) + ' has been received into stock for Non-Transactional Invoice Return Under Return Code ' + @ReturnCode + '.',
					0, 0, @TotalReturnValue, @TotalReturnValue, 'Auto Posting', @ApprovalBy, @ApprovalDate, 'Journal',
					'Approved', 0, 0, 0, 'Auto Process', @ApprovalDate, @MasterId, @TableId
				);

				DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

				-- DR Finished Goods (4156)
				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail (VoucherMasterId, AccountId, DebitorCredit, DebitAmount, CreditAmount, DebitAmountBDT, CreditAmountBDT, Remarks)
				VALUES (@VoucherMasterId, @DrGl, 'Debit', 0.00, 0.00, @TotalReturnValue, 0.00, 'Non-Transactional Invoice Return Stock Receive');

				-- CR Non-Transactional Clearing (5893)
				INSERT INTO CSTL_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail (VoucherMasterId, AccountId, DebitorCredit, DebitAmountBDT, CreditAmountBDT, DebitAmount, CreditAmount, Remarks)
				VALUES (@VoucherMasterId, @CrGl, 'Credit', 0.00, @TotalReturnValue, 0.00, 0.00, 'Non-Transactional Invoice Return Stock Receive');
			END;
		END;
	END;
END;
```

## sp_AccountsIntegration_GetParticularByOperationId

**Parameters:**
- `@SuboperationId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Looks up the GL account mapping configured for a given sub-operation id, joining the (new) sales-integration-mapping table to chart-of-accounts, defaulting `BalanceTyp` to 'Dr' when null.

**Result set:** Single result set: SuboperationId, IntegrationId, Title, BalanceType, Remarks, AccountId, resolved BalanceTyp, and a formatted `AccountCode - AccountName`.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AccountsIntegration_GetParticularByOperationId]
	
   @SuboperationId INT

AS
BEGIN

	SELECT SuboperationId,IntegrationId,Title,BalanceType,Remarks,AccountId,CASE WHEN BalanceTyp IS NULL THEN 'Dr' ELSE BalanceTyp END BalanceTyp,AccountCode + ' - ' + AccountName AS AccountName FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew AS MP
	LEFT JOIN CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts AS GL ON MP.GlId = GL.AccountId
	WHERE SuboperationId = @SuboperationId
	
END
```

## sp_AccountsPosting_IntegratedOperations

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblSampleIssue, tblSampleIssueDetail
**Tables written:** DELETE → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** explicit transaction (BEGIN TRANSACTION/COMMIT/ROLLBACK); TRY/CATCH error handling.

**Business logic:** Batch/maintenance orchestrator (hardcoded to a `2024-07-01`–`2024-08-31` date range and `CompanyId = 16`): inside an explicit transaction, deletes existing voucher master/detail rows for that window tied to proformas or payments, then re-runs `sp_AccountsPosting_InvoiceOperations`, `sp_AccountsPosting_PaymentOperations`, and `sp_AccountsPosting_SampleInvoiceOperations` to regenerate them from scratch.

**Result set:** No result set (PRINT status messages only).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for integrated operations
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AccountsPosting_IntegratedOperations]
AS
BEGIN

    BEGIN TRY
        BEGIN TRANSACTION;

        -- DELETE Operations

        -- Master Delete

        DELETE FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
        WHERE VoucherMasterId IN (
            SELECT VoucherMasterId 
            FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS VM
            WHERE PostingDate BETWEEN '2024-07-01' AND '2024-08-31'
            AND CompanyInfoId = 16
            AND (ProformaId IS NOT NULL OR CustPayDetailId IS NOT NULL)
        );

        -- Details Delete

        DELETE FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
        WHERE VoucherMasterId IN (
            SELECT VoucherMasterId 
            FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster AS VM
            WHERE PostingDate BETWEEN '2024-07-01' AND '2024-08-31'
            AND CompanyInfoId = 16
            AND (ProformaId IS NOT NULL OR CustPayDetailId IS NOT NULL)
        );

        -- Execute Store Procedures

        EXEC sp_AccountsPosting_InvoiceOperations;
        EXEC sp_AccountsPosting_PaymentOperations;
        EXEC sp_AccountsPosting_SampleInvoiceOperations;

        COMMIT TRANSACTION;

        PRINT 'Integrated operations completed successfully.';
    END TRY
    BEGIN CATCH
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
        PRINT 'An error occurred during integrated operations.';
        PRINT ERROR_MESSAGE();
    END CATCH

END;



--SELECT * FROM tblSampleIssueDetail AS D
--LEFT JOIN tblSampleIssue AS M ON D.OrderId = M.OrderId
--WHERE SubmissionDate BETWEEN '2024-07-01' AND '2024-07-31' AND ManufacId = 16
```

## sp_AccountsPosting_InvoiceOperations

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblCompanyInfo, tblCompanyUnit, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Cursors through every invoice for `CompanyId = 16` in a hardcoded `2024-07-01`–`2024-08-31` window and, based on `DeliveryInvoiceStatus`, calls `sp_ProformaPosting` plus one of `sp_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting` / `sp_ProformaFullReturnPosting` / `sp_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting` for each — a bulk re-posting driver (none of the three referenced sub-procedures are in this batch, so they cannot be verified here).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Called internally by `sp_AccountsPosting_IntegratedOperations`; UNUSED — no direct application caller found.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for inserting a new employee
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AccountsPosting_InvoiceOperations]




AS
BEGIN
   


   	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	DECLARE @DeliveryInvoiceStatus NVARCHAR(200)

	
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT InvoiceId,DeliveryInvoiceStatus FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	WHERE CI.CompanyId = 16 AND InvoiceDate BETWEEN '2024-07-01' AND '2024-08-31' 


    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId,@DeliveryInvoiceStatus
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
	
		  
	-- Execute Proforma

	EXEC sp_ProformaPosting @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId; 


	-- Execute Delivery Full

	IF(LTRIM(RTRIM(@DeliveryInvoiceStatus)) = LTRIM(RTRIM('Full')))
	BEGIN

		EXEC sp_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId;

	END

	-- Execute Delivery Reject

	ELSE IF(LTRIM(RTRIM(@DeliveryInvoiceStatus)) = LTRIM(RTRIM('Reject')))
	BEGIN

		EXEC sp_ProformaFullReturnPosting @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId;

	END


	-- Execute Delivery Partial

	ELSE IF(LTRIM(RTRIM(@DeliveryInvoiceStatus)) = LTRIM(RTRIM('Partial')))
	BEGIN

		EXEC sp_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId;

	END


    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
    INTO @InvoiceId,@DeliveryInvoiceStatus
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor



END;
```

## sp_AccountsPosting_PaymentOperations

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblCompanyInfo, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Cursors through every customer-payment-to-invoice link for `CompanyId = 16` in the same hardcoded window and calls `sp_CustomerPaymentPosting` for each — a bulk re-posting driver for payments.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Called internally by `sp_AccountsPosting_IntegratedOperations`; UNUSED — no direct application caller found.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- Create the stored procedure for inserting a new employee
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AccountsPosting_PaymentOperations]




AS
BEGIN
   


   	DECLARE @InvoiceId1 INT
	DECLARE @CustPayId1 INT

	
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
    ---------------


	SELECT INV.InvoiceId,CPD.CustPayId FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD 
	LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	WHERE CI.CompanyId = 16 AND PaymentDate BETWEEN '2024-07-01' AND '2024-08-31' 



    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId1,@CustPayId1
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
	
		  
		-- Customer Payment Posting

		EXEC sp_CustomerPaymentPosting @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId1, @CustPayId = @CustPayId1; 


    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
    INTO @InvoiceId1,@CustPayId1
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor



END;
```

## sp_AuditLog_GetUserPermissionList

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @Parm nvarchar(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblMainMenu, tblMenuDistribution, tblUser
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and runs (via `sp_executesql`) a dynamic query listing which users have which menu permissions, appending a caller-supplied `@Parm` filter string directly into the WHERE clause.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: `UserName, ManuName`.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Tareq>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AuditLog_GetUserPermissionList]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@Parm nvarchar(max)
AS
BEGIN
   
   DECLARE @Q NVARCHAR(MAX)='SELECT USR.UserName,MN.ManuName FROM tblMenuDistribution AS MD
LEFT JOIN tblUser AS USR ON MD.UserId = USR.UserId
LEFT JOIN tblMainMenu AS MN ON MD.MenuSL = MN.SL
LEFT JOIN tblMainMenu AS MDLE ON MN.ParantId = MDLE.SL
WHERE USR.UserId IS NOT NULL AND MDLE.ManuName IS NOT NULL '+ @Parm +' ORDER BY USR.UserName'

EXEC sp_executesql @Q

END
```

## sp_CPM_BonusCampaignDetailInsert

**Parameters:**
- `@CampaignDetailId INT OUTPUT`
- `@CampgainMasterId INT = NULL`
- `@PointProductId INT = NULL`
- `@Quantity DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail, tblProduct, tblUnitPrice
**Tables written:** INSERT → [dbo].[Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail]

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Inserts one bonus-campaign product/quantity detail line into `Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail` for a given campaign master, but only if an identical (master, product, quantity) combination doesn't already exist.

**Result set:** No result set; returns the new id via `@CampaignDetailId OUTPUT` (0 if the row was a duplicate and skipped).

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/BonusCampaignDal.cs — via `aInternalDal.SaveAction("sp_CPM_BonusCampaignDetailInsert", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CPM_BonusCampaignDetailInsert]
     
	@CampaignDetailId INT OUTPUT,
	@CampgainMasterId INT = NULL,
    @PointProductId INT = NULL,
    @Quantity  DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL
   
AS
BEGIN


    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 
        FROM Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail
        WHERE 
            CampgainMasterId = @CampgainMasterId
            AND PointProductId = @PointProductId
            AND ISNULL(Quantity, 0) = ISNULL(@Quantity, 0)
    )
    BEGIN
        
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail]
           (CampgainMasterId
           ,PointProductId
           ,Quantity)
		VALUES
           (@CampgainMasterId
           ,@PointProductId
           ,@Quantity)

        SET @CampaignDetailId = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @CampaignDetailId = 0;
    END
END



--SELECT PD.ProductId, PD.ProductCode + ':' + PD.ProductName AS ProductName  FROM tblProduct AS PD
--LEFT JOIN tblUnitPrice AS UP ON PD.ProductId = UP.ProductId
--WHERE UP.IsActive = 1 AND CompanyId = 2 ORDER BY PD.ProductName
```

## sp_CPM_BonusCampaigntMasterInsert

**Parameters:**
- `@CampgainMasterId INT OUT`
- `@CompanyId INT = NULL`
- `@BonusProductId INT = NULL`
- `@BonusProductQuantity DECIMAL(18,2) = NULL`
- `@CampaignName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL`
- `@CampaignDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL`
- `@FromDate DATETIME`
- `@Todate DATETIME`
- `@IsActive BIT`
- `@EntryBy NVARCHAR(200) = NULL`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster, tblMoneyReceipt
**Tables written:** INSERT → [dbo].[Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster]

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Creates a new bonus-campaign master row in `Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster`, generating a `CampaignCode` in the form `BONUS-YYMMDD####` (sequence scoped to campaigns created that same day).

**Result set:** No result set; returns the new id via `@CampgainMasterId OUT` (`SCOPE_IDENTITY()`).

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/BonusCampaignDal.cs — via `aInternalDal.SaveAction("sp_CPM_BonusCampaigntMasterInsert", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,Nasa>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CPM_BonusCampaigntMasterInsert] 

	 @CampgainMasterId INT OUT
    ,@CompanyId INT = NULL
	,@BonusProductId INT = NULL
	,@BonusProductQuantity DECIMAL(18,2) = NULL
    ,@CampaignName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
    ,@CampaignDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
    ,@FromDate DATETIME
    ,@Todate DATETIME
    ,@IsActive BIT
    ,@EntryBy NVARCHAR(200) = NULL

AS
BEGIN

	

	--IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tblMoneyReceipt
	--WHERE CreateBy = @CreateBy AND Pay = @PaymentDate AND DATEDIFF(SECOND,CreateDate, GETDATE()) < 30 )
		  
	--BEGIN

		-- Declare variables

		DECLARE @CampaignCode NVARCHAR(500);
		DECLARE @ProCount INT;
		
		-- Calculate the sequence for the current date

		SELECT @ProCount = COUNT(CampgainMasterId) + 1 
		FROM Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster 
		WHERE CAST(EntryDate AS DATE) = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);
		
		-- Generate the ProformaCode in the desired format (YYMMDD + sequence starting at 1000)

		SET @CampaignCode = 'BONUS-' + RIGHT(YEAR(GETDATE()), 2) 
							+ FORMAT(GETDATE(), 'MMdd') 
							+ CAST(1000 + @ProCount AS NVARCHAR);

		--PRINT @SpotPurchaseCode

		-- Insert

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster]
           (CampaignCode
           ,CompanyId
           ,CampaignName
           ,CampaignDesc
           ,FromDate
           ,Todate
           ,IsActive
           ,EntryBy
           ,EntryDate,
		   BonusProductId,
		   BonusProductQuantity)
     VALUES
           (@CampaignCode
           ,@CompanyId
           ,@CampaignName
           ,@CampaignDesc
           ,@FromDate
           ,@Todate
           ,@IsActive
           ,@EntryBy
           ,GETDATE(),
		   @BonusProductId,
		   @BonusProductQuantity)


		SET @CampgainMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

	--END


END
```

## sp_CPM_BonusCampaigntMasterUpdate

**Parameters:**
- `@CampgainMasterId INT`
- `@CompanyId INT = NULL`
- `@BonusProductId INT = NULL`
- `@BonusProductQuantity DECIMAL(18,2) = NULL`
- `@CampaignName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL`
- `@CampaignDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL`
- `@FromDate DATETIME`
- `@Todate DATETIME`
- `@IsActive BIT`
- `@EntryBy NVARCHAR(200) = NULL`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblMoneyReceipt
**Tables written:** UPDATE → [dbo].[Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster]

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Updates an existing bonus-campaign master row's editable fields, then deletes all of its existing detail rows from `Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail` (the caller is expected to re-insert fresh detail rows afterward via `sp_CPM_BonusCampaignDetailInsert`).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/BonusCampaignDal.cs — via `aInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_CPM_BonusCampaigntMasterUpdate", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,Nasa>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CPM_BonusCampaigntMasterUpdate] 

	 @CampgainMasterId INT
    ,@CompanyId INT = NULL
	,@BonusProductId INT = NULL
	,@BonusProductQuantity DECIMAL(18,2) = NULL
    ,@CampaignName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
    ,@CampaignDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
    ,@FromDate DATETIME
    ,@Todate DATETIME
    ,@IsActive BIT
    ,@EntryBy NVARCHAR(200) = NULL

AS
BEGIN

	

	--IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tblMoneyReceipt
	--WHERE CreateBy = @CreateBy AND Pay = @PaymentDate AND DATEDIFF(SECOND,CreateDate, GETDATE()) < 30 )
		  
	--BEGIN


		-- Insert



		UPDATE [dbo].[Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster]
		SET
		    CompanyId = @CompanyId,
		    CampaignName = @CampaignName,
		    CampaignDesc = @CampaignDesc,
		    FromDate = @FromDate,
		    Todate = @Todate,
		    IsActive = @IsActive,
		    UpdateBy = @EntryBy,
		    UpdateDate = GETDATE(),
		    BonusProductId = @BonusProductId,
		    BonusProductQuantity = @BonusProductQuantity
		WHERE
		    CampgainMasterId = @CampgainMasterId;

		--Delete Details

		DELETE Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail WHERE CampgainMasterId = @CampgainMasterId

	--END


END
```

## sp_CPM_DeleteBonusCampaign

**Parameters:**
- `@CampaignMasterId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail, Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster
**Tables written:** DELETE → Table_CPM_BonusCampaignDetail, Table_CPM_BonusCampaignMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

## sp_CRRR_WrongPayment

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblPaymentTranscationDetail, tblPaymentTranscationMaster
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblInvoice; DELETE → ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblPaymentTranscationDetail, tblPaymentTranscationMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** One-off manual data-correction script (not parameterized): hardcodes a specific `InvoiceNo` and a set of `CustPayId`/`VoucherMasterId` values to inspect, then flip the invoice's payment status back to 'Partial' and delete the erroneous payment/transaction/voucher rows tied to a specific wrongly-recorded payment.

**Result set:** Several ad-hoc SELECTs for inspection, interleaved with UPDATE/DELETE statements against hardcoded ids.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found (one-off DBA/support script, hardcoded ids, not designed for repeat execution).

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CRRR_WrongPayment] 

 
AS
BEGIN
	
	
SELECT * FROM tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceNo IN ('INV-BD57-000000575')
SELECT * FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE InvoiceId = 88027


SELECT * FROM tblPaymentTranscationDetail WHERE  InvoiceId = 88027

UPDATE tblInvoice SET PaymentStatus = 'Partial' WHERE InvoiceNo IN ('INV-BD57-000000575')

DELETE FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE CustPayDetailId = 90182
DELETE FROM tblCustomerPay WHERE CustPayId = 90185

DELETE FROM tblPaymentTranscationMaster WHERE CustPayId = 52088
DELETE FROM tblPaymentTranscationDetail WHERE CustPayDetailId = 52076

DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = 206158
DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = 206158


SELECT * FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE CustPayDetailId = 88027
	
END
```

## sp_CSOpeningBalanceProcess

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** db_cursor, dbo.tblCentralStore
**Tables written:** INSERT → dbo.tblCentralStore_OpeninigBalance

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Snapshot job with no parameters: cursors through every `tblCentralStore` row with `Quantity > 0` and copies it into `tblCentralStore_OpeninigBalance`, stamped with the current date as the opening-balance date — used to freeze a periodic central-store opening balance.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:	<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CSOpeningBalanceProcess] 

AS
BEGIN


declare @CSOpeningBalanceDate datetime

set @CSOpeningBalanceDate = Getdate()





DECLARE @ReceiveId INT
DECLARE @StorageLocation nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @ProductId INT
DECLARE @ProductCode nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @ProductName nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @PackSize nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @BatchNo nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @Quantity decimal(18,0)
DECLARE @MfgDate datetime
DECLARE @ExpDate datetime
DECLARE @ReceiveDate datetime
DECLARE @ChalanNo nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @ChalanDate datetime
DECLARE @StockInQty decimal(18,0)
DECLARE @UnitPrice decimal(18,2)
DECLARE @TotalPrice decimal(18,2)
DECLARE @VATPerUnit decimal(18,2)
DECLARE @TotalVAT decimal(18,2)
DECLARE @TotalAmount decimal(18,2)
DECLARE @StockCondition nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @MigoDetailID INT
DECLARE @DeveloperRemarks nvarchar(500)
DECLARE @ProductStockType nvarchar(250)
DECLARE @InternalNoteNo nvarchar(250)






DECLARE db_cursor CURSOR FOR 

SELECT ReceiveId ,
StorageLocation ,
ProductId ,
ProductCode ,
ProductName ,
PackSize ,
BatchNo ,
Quantity ,
MfgDate ,
ExpDate ,
ReceiveDate ,
ChalanNo ,
ChalanDate ,
StockInQty ,
UnitPrice ,
TotalPrice ,
VATPerUnit ,
TotalVAT ,
TotalAmount ,
StockCondition ,
MigoDetailID ,
DeveloperRemarks ,
ProductStockType ,
InternalNoteNo FROM dbo.tblCentralStore where Quantity>0

OPEN db_cursor 
FETCH NEXT FROM db_cursor INTO @ReceiveId ,
@StorageLocation ,
@ProductId ,
@ProductCode ,
@ProductName ,
@PackSize ,
@BatchNo ,
@Quantity ,
@MfgDate ,
@ExpDate ,
@ReceiveDate ,
@ChalanNo ,
@ChalanDate ,
@StockInQty ,
@UnitPrice ,
@TotalPrice ,
@VATPerUnit ,
@TotalVAT ,
@TotalAmount ,
@StockCondition ,
@MigoDetailID ,
@DeveloperRemarks ,
@ProductStockType ,
@InternalNoteNo 

WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 
BEGIN 
INSERT INTO dbo.tblCentralStore_OpeninigBalance
( CSOpeninigBalanceDate ,
ReceiveId ,
StorageLocation ,
ProductId ,
ProductCode ,
ProductName ,
PackSize ,
BatchNo ,
Quantity ,
MfgDate ,
ExpDate ,
ReceiveDate ,
ChalanNo ,
ChalanDate ,
StockInQty ,
UnitPrice ,
TotalPrice ,
VATPerUnit ,
TotalVAT ,
TotalAmount ,
StockCondition ,
MigoDetailID ,
DeveloperRemarks ,
ProductStockType ,
InternalNoteNo
)
VALUES ( CONVERT(nvarchar(11),@CSOpeningBalanceDate,106) , -- CSOpeninigBalanceDate - datetime
@ReceiveId ,
@StorageLocation ,
@ProductId ,
@ProductCode ,
@ProductName ,
@PackSize ,
@BatchNo ,
@Quantity ,
@MfgDate ,
@ExpDate ,
@ReceiveDate ,
@ChalanNo ,
@ChalanDate ,
@StockInQty ,
@UnitPrice ,
@TotalPrice ,
@VATPerUnit ,
@TotalVAT ,
@TotalAmount ,
@StockCondition ,
@MigoDetailID ,
@DeveloperRemarks ,
@ProductStockType ,
@InternalNoteNo 
)

FETCH NEXT FROM db_cursor INTO @ReceiveId ,
@StorageLocation ,
@ProductId ,
@ProductCode ,
@ProductName ,
@PackSize ,
@BatchNo ,
@Quantity ,
@MfgDate ,
@ExpDate ,
@ReceiveDate ,
@ChalanNo ,
@ChalanDate ,
@StockInQty ,
@UnitPrice ,
@TotalPrice ,
@VATPerUnit ,
@TotalVAT ,
@TotalAmount ,
@StockCondition ,
@MigoDetailID ,
@DeveloperRemarks ,
@ProductStockType ,
@InternalNoteNo 
END 

CLOSE db_cursor 
DEALLOCATE db_cursor 




END
```

## sp_CS_GetArea__ByEmpId

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @EMPId int`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblArea, tblArea, tblCompanyUnit, tblDcWiseAreaInfo, tblRsmInfo
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes (`EXEC`) a dynamic query returning the areas visible to a given employee: resolves the employee's company via `tblRsmInfo`, builds a comma-list of region ids reachable by that company via `tblDcWiseAreaInfo`/`tblCompanyUnit`/`tblArea`, then selects areas under those regions, marking inactive ones.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: `AreaId, AreaName` for areas under the employee's accessible regions, ordered by area code.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CS_GetArea__ByEmpId]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@EMPId int
AS
BEGIN

 




declare @ComId int=0
select @ComId=CompanyId from tblRsmInfo where    EmployeeId=@EMPId and isActive=1

 
   DECLARE @Query NVARCHAR(MAX)=null;

   DECLARE @Parrameter NVARCHAR(MAX)='';

 
 		 select  @Parrameter=	STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',', ar.RegionId , '') FROM tblDcWiseAreaInfo dc (NOLOCK)  inner join tblCompanyUnit dpto on dc.DCId=dpto.ComUnitId  
		   inner join tblArea ar on dc.AreaId=ar.AreaId 
		  where dpto.CompanyId=@ComId  FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'') 

	


			SET @Query = '	SELECT AreaId, case when IsActive = 1 then   AreaCode+'' : ''+AreaName else  AreaCode+'' : ''+AreaName +'' [Inactive]'' end  AreaName FROM dbo.tblArea  WITH (NOLOCK)   WHERE    RegionId in (	 '+@Parrameter+' )

		order by AreaCode asc'
			
	
			EXEC (@Query)


END
```

## sp_CS_GetCom_ByGroupId

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @id int=null`
- `@EmpInfoId int=null`
- `@UserId int=null`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblCompanyInfo, tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblUser
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Dropdown-source lookup for companies under a given group id, with access scoped by user: 'Admin' users see all companies in the group; other users are further scoped by `@EmpInfoId` (if provided) joined through `tblEmpGeneralInfo`, or otherwise just by user id.

**Result set:** Single result set: `CompanyId, CompanyName` (short-name-prefixed where available), scoped by group and user role.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CS_GetCom_ByGroupId]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@id int=null,

	@EmpInfoId int=null,
	@UserId int=null

AS
BEGIN
		declare @UserType nvarchar(max)=null
		select @UserType=UserType from tblUser  where UserId=@UserId

		if(@UserType='Admin')

		begin 
		select distinct c.CompanyId, ISNULL(c.CmpShortName+' : ', '')+ c.CompanyName CompanyName from tblCompanyInfo c 
			 
			where c.GroupId=@id  
			order by CompanyName
		end

		else
		begin
			 if(@EmpInfoId is null)
			 begin
			 select distinct c.CompanyId, ISNULL(c.CmpShortName+' : ', '')+  c.CompanyName CompanyName from tblCompanyInfo c 
			inner join tblUser  us on us.UserId=@UserId

			where c.GroupId=@id -- and us.IsHead=1 
			order by CompanyName
			 end

			 else
			 begin
			select distinct c.CompanyId,  ISNULL(c.CmpShortName+' : ', '')+  c.CompanyName CompanyName from tblCompanyInfo c 
			inner join tblEmpGeneralInfo  emp on c.[CompanyId]=emp.CompanyId
			inner join tblUser  us on us.EmpInfoId=emp.EmpInfoId

			where c.GroupId=@id  and emp.EmpInfoId=@EmpInfoId --and us.IsHead=1
			order by CompanyName
			end
			end
END
```

## sp_CS_GetEmpGeneralInfo_All

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblDistrictCoordinator, tblUpazilaCoordinator, tblUser
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Lists all app-enabled employees (`tblUser.IsAppsUser = 1`) with a non-blank master code, cross-checked against the `ZASHRIS` HR system, labeling the display name with '(status)' when the HR employee status isn't 'Active'.

**Result set:** Single result set: `EmpMasterCode, EmpName (with status suffix), EmpInfoId`, ordered by master code.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CS_GetEmpGeneralInfo_All]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here

AS
BEGIN
		
				SELECT    distinct   emp.EmpMasterCode, case when hris.EmployeeStatus='Active' then    emp.EmpMasterCode+' : '+ emp.EmpName else   emp.EmpMasterCode+' : '+ emp.EmpName + ' ('+ hris.EmployeeStatus +')' end  EmpName, emp.EmpInfoId	FROM dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo  emp with (nolock) --where EmployeeStatus='Active'
				inner join ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo hris on hris.EmpMasterCode=emp.EmpMasterCode
				 inner join tblUser ss on ss.EmpInfoId=emp.EmpInfoId  and ss.IsAppsUser=1
				where   emp.EmpMasterCode <>''  
				order by emp.EmpMasterCode




				--SELECT EmpMasterCode+' : '+ EmpName AS EmpName, *	FROM dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo   E
				--LEFT JOIN tblDistrictCoordinator DC ON DC.EmpInfoId=  E.EmpInfoId
				--LEFT JOIn tblUpazilaCoordinator UC ON UC.EmpInfoId = E.EmpInfoId
				
			

END
```

## sp_CS_GetEmpGeneralInfo_ByUserHead

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @EmpInfoId int=null`
- `@UserId int=null`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblDistrictCoordinator, tblUpazilaCoordinator
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@EmpInfoId`/`@UserId`, first resolves the company id(s) of that one employee (via the HR system, only if HR status is 'Active'), then lists all active employees belonging to those same company id(s) — effectively "employees under the same company as this user's head".

**Result set:** Single result set: `EmpName, EmpInfoId` for active employees in the resolved company set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CS_GetEmpGeneralInfo_ByUserHead]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here


	@EmpInfoId int=null,
	@UserId int=null
AS
BEGIN

declare @TRID nvarchar(max)=null
		select  @TRID=	STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',', emp.CompanyId , '') FROM   dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo  emp with (nolock) inner join ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo hris on hris.EmpMasterCode=emp.EmpMasterCode
				where hris.EmployeeStatus='Active'  and emp.[EmpInfoId] =@EmpInfoId    FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'')
				SELECT        emp.EmpMasterCode+' : '+ emp.EmpName  EmpName, emp.EmpInfoId	FROM dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo  emp with (nolock) --where EmployeeStatus='Active'
				inner join ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo hris on hris.EmpMasterCode=emp.EmpMasterCode
				where hris.EmployeeStatus='Active' and emp.EmpMasterCode <>''  and  emp.CompanyId in (@TRID)
				order by emp.EmpMasterCode






				--SELECT EmpMasterCode+' : '+ EmpName AS EmpName, *	FROM dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo   E
				--LEFT JOIN tblDistrictCoordinator DC ON DC.EmpInfoId=  E.EmpInfoId
				--LEFT JOIn tblUpazilaCoordinator UC ON UC.EmpInfoId = E.EmpInfoId
				
			

END
```

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

```sql
AS
BEGIN

END
```

## sp_Common_GetUnitWiseLocation_FromErp

**Parameters:**
- `@CompanyId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERPDB..tblCompanyUnit
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Dropdown-source lookup of company units for a given company id, sourced from the cross-database `CSTL_ERPDB` ERP company-unit table rather than the local distribution DB.

**Result set:** Single result set: `UnitId, UnitName FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblCompanyUnit WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY UnitName`.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/TradeMaterialTransferDal.cs — `LoadToLocation(...)`, via `ClsCommonInternalDAL.LoadDropDownListData(...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE   PROCEDURE dbo.sp_Common_GetUnitWiseLocation_FromErp
    @CompanyId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    SELECT 
        UnitId, 
        UnitName 
    FROM CSTL_ERPDB..tblCompanyUnit 
    WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId 
    ORDER BY UnitName;
END
```
