## sp_CustomerPaymentPosting

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceId INT`
- `-- 485057 @CustPayId INT -- 476422`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, dbo.tblCustPayDetail, dbo.tblCustomerPay, dbo.tblInvoice, tblCompanyUnit, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster; UPDATE → dbo.tblCustPayDetail, tblCustPayDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@InvoiceId`/`@CustPayId`, restricted to `CompanyId = 16`: cursors through the matching `tblCustPayDetail` row(s), generates a voucher code, creates a voucher header, then posts Debit (cash/bank GL) + optional Debit (discount GL, if `@Discount > 0`) + optional Debit (AIT GL, if `@AIT > 0`) + Credit (customer-payment GL) rows, and finally flags the payment detail row `IsPosting = 1`/`0` depending on whether a voucher now exists for it.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Referenced only from commented-out code in Library.DAL (BatchWiseCollectionReportDal.cs, CustPaymentDAL.cs, PaymentTranscationDal.cs all have the call lines commented out) — effectively UNUSED in the active application, though also called internally (live) by `sp_AccountsPosting_PaymentOperations`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_CustomerPaymentPosting]
    @InvoiceId INT, -- 485057
    @CustPayId INT -- 476422
AS
BEGIN
    
	
	DECLARE @TempCompanyId INT = 0
    
    SELECT @TempCompanyId = CompanyId 
    FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
    LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS CU ON INV.ComUnitId = CU.ComUnitId
    WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId

    IF(@TempCompanyId = 16)
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @CustomerMasterId INT
        DECLARE @CustPayMId INT
        DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME
        DECLARE @CompanyId INT
        DECLARE @BankId INT
        DECLARE @CashId INT
        DECLARE @PaymentAmount DECIMAL(18,2)
        DECLARE @Discount DECIMAL(18,2)
        DECLARE @AIT DECIMAL(18,2)
        DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(500)
        DECLARE @PaymentStatus NVARCHAR(500)
        DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(500)
        
        DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT
        DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
        SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
        FOR
        SELECT 
            CPD.PaymentAmount,
            CPY.CustomerMasterId,
            CPD.CustPayDetailId,
            CPY.PaymentDate,
            UNT.CompanyId,
            ISNULL(CPD.Discount, 0) AS Discount,
            ISNULL(CPD.AIT, 0) AS AIT,
            InvoiceNo,
            PaymentStatus,
            CPD.BankAccId,
            CPD.CashAccId 
        FROM dbo.tblCustPayDetail AS CPD 
        LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCustomerPay AS CPY ON CPD.CustPayId = CPY.CustPayId
        LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice ON tblInvoice.InvoiceId = CPD.InvoiceId
        INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = tblInvoice.ComUnitId
        WHERE CPD.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId AND CPD.CustPayId = @CustPayId

        OPEN @MyCursor
        FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
        INTO @PaymentAmount, @CustomerMasterId, @CustPayId, @InvDate, @CompanyId, @Discount, @AIT, @InvoiceNo, @PaymentStatus, @BankId, @CashId
        
        WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
        BEGIN
            DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)
            DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX) = SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)
            DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX) = (CASE WHEN LEN(MONTH(@InvDate)) = 1 THEN '0' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
            DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX) = (CASE WHEN LEN(DAY(@InvDate)) = 1 THEN '0' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), DAY(@InvDate)) END)

            SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText + @monthText + @dateText) + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX), CONVERT(INT, ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode, 11, 6)), 10000)) + 1)
            FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
            WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate

            DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
            SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId 
            FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
            WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND @InvDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

            SET @VoucherType = CASE 
                WHEN @BankId IS NOT NULL AND @BankId != 0 THEN 'CreditVoucherBank'
                WHEN @CashId != 0 AND @CashId IS NOT NULL THEN 'CreditVoucherCash'
            END

            IF(@VoucherType = 'DebitVoucherBank')
            BEGIN
                SET @VoucherCode = 'DVB-' + @VoucherCode
            END
            IF(@VoucherType = 'DebitVoucherCash')
            BEGIN
                SET @VoucherCode = 'DVC-' + @VoucherCode
            END
            IF(@VoucherType = 'CreditVoucherBank')
            BEGIN
                SET @VoucherCode = 'CVB-' + @VoucherCode
            END
            IF(@VoucherType = 'CreditVoucherCash')
            BEGIN
                SET @VoucherCode = 'CVC-' + @VoucherCode
            END
            IF(@VoucherType = 'FundTransfer')
            BEGIN
                SET @VoucherCode = 'FUT-' + @VoucherCode
            END
            IF(@VoucherType = 'Journal')
            BEGIN
                SET @VoucherCode = 'JVP-' + @VoucherCode
            END

            INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
            (
                VoucherCode,
                CompanyInfoId,
                FinancialYearId,
                PostingDate,
                VoucherCategoryID,
                CurrencyId,
                CurrencyCovRate,
                Narration,
                TotalAmount,
                TotalAmountBDT,
                EntryBy,
                EntryDate,
                VoucherType, 
                JournalType,
                CustPayDetailId,
                IsCostCenter,
                IsInvoice,
                isManualVCRNo,
                IsExportInvoice,
                IsImportInvoice
            )
            VALUES
            (
                @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
                @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
                @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
                @InvDate,           -- PostingDate - datetime
                2,                  -- VoucherCategoryID - int
                1,                  -- CurrencyId - int
                1.00,               -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
                'Customer Payment - ( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Payment Date:' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Payment Amount:' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @PaymentAmount) + ', Discount Amount:' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @Discount) + ', AIT Amount:' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @AIT) + ')',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
                0.00,               -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                @PaymentAmount + @Discount + @AIT,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                'Auto Process',     -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
                CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,  -- EntryDate - datetime
                @VoucherType,       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
                'Receiveable',      -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
                @CustPayId,         -- ProformaId - int
                0, 0, 0, 0, 0       -- Other flags
            )

            SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

            DECLARE @DrGl1 INT
            IF (@BankId IS NOT NULL AND @BankId != 0)
            BEGIN 
                SELECT @DrGl1 = AccountId 
                FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts 
                WHERE BankAccountId = @BankId
            END

            IF(@CashId IS NOT NULL AND @CashId != 0)
            BEGIN 
                SELECT @DrGl1 = AccountId 
                FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts 
                WHERE CashAccountId = @CashId
            END

            INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
            (
                VoucherMasterId,
                AccountId,
                DebitorCredit,
                DebitAmount,
                CreditAmount,
                DebitAmountBDT,
                CreditAmountBDT
            )
            VALUES
            (
                @VoucherMasterId,   -- VoucherMasterId - int
                @DrGl1,             -- AccountId - int
                'Debit',            -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
                @PaymentAmount,     -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                0.00,               -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                @PaymentAmount,     -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                0.00                -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
            )

            IF(@Discount > 0)
            BEGIN
                DECLARE @DrGl INT 
                SELECT @DrGl = GlId 
                FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew 
                WHERE Particular IN ('CustomerPayment') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr2')

                INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
                (
                    VoucherMasterId,
                    AccountId,
                    DebitorCredit,
                    DebitAmount,
                    CreditAmount,
                    DebitAmountBDT,
                    CreditAmountBDT
                )
                VALUES
                (
                    @VoucherMasterId,   -- VoucherMasterId - int
                    @DrGl,              -- AccountId - int
                    'Debit',            -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
                    @Discount,          -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                    0.00,               -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                    @Discount,          -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                    0.00                -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                )
            END

            IF (@AIT > 0)    
            BEGIN
                DECLARE @AITGl INT 
                SELECT @AITGl = GlId 
                FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew 
                WHERE Particular IN ('CustomerPayment') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr3')

                IF (@AITGl > 0)    
                BEGIN
                    INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
                    (
                        VoucherMasterId,
                        AccountId,
                        DebitorCredit,
                        DebitAmount,
                        CreditAmount,
                        DebitAmountBDT,
                        CreditAmountBDT
                    )
                    VALUES
                    (
                        @VoucherMasterId,   -- VoucherMasterId - int
                        @AITGl,             -- AccountId - int
                        'Debit',            -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
                        @AIT,               -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                        0.00,               -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                        @AIT,               -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                        0.00                -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                    )
                END
            END

            DECLARE @CrGl INT 
            SELECT @CrGl = GlId 
            FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew 
            WHERE Particular IN ('CustomerPayment') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')

            INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
            (
                VoucherMasterId,
                AccountId,
                DebitorCredit,
                DebitAmount,
                CreditAmount,
                DebitAmountBDT,
                CreditAmountBDT
            )
            VALUES
            (
                @VoucherMasterId,                   -- VoucherMasterId - int
                ISNULL(@CrGl, 0),                   -- AccountId - int
                'Credit',                           -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
                0.00,                               -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                @PaymentAmount + @Discount + @AIT,  -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
                0.00,                               -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
                @PaymentAmount + @Discount + @AIT   -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
            )

            DECLARE @Count INT = 0 
            SELECT @Count = COUNT(CustPayDetailId) 
            FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
            WHERE CustPayDetailId = @CustPayId

            IF (@Count > 0)
            BEGIN
                UPDATE dbo.tblCustPayDetail 
                SET IsPosting = 1 
                WHERE CustPayDetailId = @CustPayId
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                UPDATE tblCustPayDetail 
                SET IsPosting = 0 
                WHERE CustPayDetailId = @CustPayId
            END

            FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
            INTO @PaymentAmount, @CustomerMasterId, @CustPayId, @InvDate, @CompanyId, @Discount, @AIT, @InvoiceNo, @PaymentStatus, @BankId, @CashId
        END

        CLOSE @MyCursor
        DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
    END
END



--SELECT CPD.PaymentAmount,CPY.CustomerMasterId,CPD.InvoiceId,CPD.CustPayDetailId,CPY.PaymentDate,UNT.CompanyId,CPD.Discount,InvoiceNo,PaymentStatus,CPD.BankId FROM dbo.tblCustPayDetail AS CPD 
--	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCustomerPay AS CPY ON CPD.CustPayId = CPY.CustPayId
--	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice ON tblInvoice.InvoiceId = CPD.InvoiceId
--	INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = tblInvoice.ComUnitId
```

## sp_DCBinCard

**Parameters:**
- `@FromDate DATETIME`
- `@ToDate DATETIME`
- `@DcId INT`
- `@DCName NVARCHAR(MAX)`
- `@CompanyId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** Bonus, ChallanIssue, ChallanReceive, ChallanTransit, FinalData, FreezeBlocked, FreezeRestricted, NonTransReceive, OB, ProductBase, ProductBonus, RawData, Sales, SalesInTransit, SampleIssue, StoReceive, StockReceive, dbo.tblCentralStore, dbo.tblChalanDetail, dbo.tblChalanInfo, dbo.tblDCStore, dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze, dbo.tblDCStore_OpeningBalance, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail, dbo.tblNonTranscationalInvoiceDetail, dbo.tblProduct, dbo.tblSampleIssue, dbo.tblSampleIssueDetail, dbo.tblSampleIssueTranscation
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Builds a per-DC (distribution centre), per-product "bin card" style stock-movement report between `@FromDate` and `@ToDate`: assembles opening balance, receipts (from central warehouse, non-transactional returns, inter-DC challan, direct stock-in), and issues (sales, product bonus, general bonus, sales-in-transit, inter-DC challan out, freeze/blocked stock, sample issue), then computes total received, total issued, closing stock, and closing stock value, filtering out all-zero rows.

**Result set:** Single result set: one row per product with opening/received/issued/closing quantity and value columns, restricted to rows with any non-zero movement.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/AreaWiseMonthlyInventoryReportDal.cs — via `aCommonInternalDal.GetDataTableAction("sp_DCBinCard", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,0@CompanyId/@CompanyId5/20@CompanyId6,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DCBinCard] 

	@FromDate DATETIME,
	@ToDate DATETIME,
	@DcId INT,
	@DCName NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@CompanyId INT

	
AS
BEGIN
	
	;WITH ProductBase AS
(
    SELECT 
        P.ProductCode,
        P.ProductName,
        P.PackSize
    FROM dbo.tblProduct P WITH (NOLOCK)
    WHERE P.CompanyId = @CompanyId
),

OB AS
(
    SELECT 
        OB.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(OB.StockQty, 0)) AS Quantity,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(OB.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(OB.StockQty, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStore_OpeningBalance OB WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON OB.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        OB.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND OB.DCOpeningBalanceDate = @FromDate
    GROUP BY 
        OB.ProductCode
),

StoReceive AS
(
    SELECT 
        DCS.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0)) AS TotalStockReceiveQty,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.StockQty, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        DCS.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND DCS.ChalanDetailsId IS NULL
        AND DCS.StockRcvDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        DCS.ProductCode
),

NonTransReceive AS
(
    SELECT 
        DCS.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0)) AS TotalStockReceiveQty,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(NTD.Price, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(NTD.VAT, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblNonTranscationalInvoiceDetail NTD WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON NTD.InvReturnDetailID = DCS.NonTranscationalInvReturnDetailID
    WHERE 
        DCS.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND ISNULL(DCS.NonTranscationalInvReturnDetailID, 0) > 0
        AND ISNULL(DCS.ReceiveId, 0) = 0
        AND ISNULL(DCS.TempReceiveId, 0) = 0
        AND DCS.StockRcvDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        DCS.ProductCode
),

ChallanReceive AS
(
    SELECT 
        DCS.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0)) AS TotalStockReceiveQty,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.StockQty, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        DCS.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND DCS.ChalanDetailsId IS NOT NULL
        AND DCS.StockRcvDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        DCS.ProductCode
),

StockReceive AS
(
    SELECT 
        DCS.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0)) AS TotalStockReceiveQty,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(DCS.TotalQuantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        DCS.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND DCS.StockRcvDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        DCS.ProductCode
),

Sales AS
(
    SELECT 
        ID.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(ID.DeliveryQuantity, 0)) AS Sales,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(ID.DeliveryQuantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(ID.DeliveryQuantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblInvoiceDetail ID WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblInvoice I WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON ID.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = ID.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        I.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND ID.DeliveryStatus IN ('Full', 'Partial')
        AND I.InvoiceDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        ID.ProductCode
),

Bonus AS
(
    SELECT 
        ID.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(ID.BonusQuantity, 0)) AS BonusQty,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(ID.BonusQuantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(ID.BonusQuantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblInvoiceDetail ID WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblInvoice I WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON ID.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = ID.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        I.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND (ID.DeliveryStatus IS NULL OR ID.DeliveryStatus IN ('Full', 'Partial'))
        AND I.InvoiceDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        ID.ProductCode
),

ProductBonus AS
(
    SELECT 
        ID.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(ID.DeliveryQuantity, 0)) AS Sales,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(ID.DeliveryQuantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(ID.DeliveryQuantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblInvoiceDetail ID WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblInvoice I WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON ID.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = ID.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        I.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND ID.OrderDetailsId = 0
        AND I.InvoiceDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        ID.ProductCode
),

SalesInTransit AS
(
    SELECT 
        ID.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(ID.TotalQuantity, 0)) AS Sales,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(ID.TotalQuantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(ID.TotalQuantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblInvoiceDetail ID WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblInvoice I WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON ID.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = ID.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        I.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND ID.DeliveryStatus IS NULL
        AND I.InvoiceDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        ID.ProductCode
),

ChallanIssue AS
(
    SELECT 
        CD.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(CD.Quantity, 0)) AS Challan,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(CD.Quantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(CD.Quantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblChalanDetail CD WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblChalanInfo CI WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON CD.ChalanId = CI.ChalanId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = CD.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        CI.FromComUnitId = @DcId
        AND CI.ChalanDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
        AND CI.IsDeliver = 'True'
    GROUP BY 
        CD.ProductCode
),

ChallanTransit AS
(
    SELECT 
        CD.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(CD.Quantity, 0)) AS Challan,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(CD.Quantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(CD.Quantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblChalanDetail CD WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblChalanInfo CI WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON CD.ChalanId = CI.ChalanId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = CD.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        CI.FromComUnitId = @DcId
        AND CI.ChalanDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
        AND CI.IsDeliver = 'False'
    GROUP BY 
        CD.ProductCode
),

FreezeRestricted AS
(
    SELECT 
        F.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(F.StockQty, 0) + ISNULL(F.DamageQty, 0)) AS Freeze,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(F.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(F.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(F.DamageQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(F.DamageQty, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze F WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = F.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        F.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND F.StockCondition = 'Restricted'
        AND F.StockRcvDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        F.ProductCode
),

FreezeBlocked AS
(
    SELECT 
        F.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(F.StockQty, 0) + ISNULL(F.DamageQty, 0)) AS Freeze,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(F.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(F.StockQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(F.DamageQty, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(F.DamageQty, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze F WITH (NOLOCK)
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = F.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        F.ComUnitId = @DcId
        AND F.StockCondition = 'Blocked'
        AND F.StockRcvDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
    GROUP BY 
        F.ProductCode
),

SampleIssue AS
(
    SELECT 
        SMPD.ProductCode,
        SUM(ISNULL(ST.Quantity, 0) * -1) AS SampleIssue,
        SUM(
            (ISNULL(CTRS.UnitPrice, 0) * ISNULL(SMPD.Quantity, 0))
            + (ISNULL(CTRS.VATPerUnit, 0) * ISNULL(SMPD.Quantity, 0))
        ) AS Value
    FROM dbo.tblSampleIssue SMPI WITH (NOLOCK)
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblSampleIssueDetail SMPD WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON SMPI.OrderId = SMPD.OrderId
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tblSampleIssueTranscation ST WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON ST.IssueDetailId = SMPD.OrderDetailId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStore DCS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.DCStoreId = ST.DCStoreId
    INNER JOIN dbo.tblCentralStore CTRS WITH (NOLOCK)
        ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    WHERE 
        SMPI.ActionStatus = 'Accepted'
        AND SMPI.ApprovedDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate
        AND SMPI.ComUnitId = @DcId
    GROUP BY 
        SMPD.ProductCode
),

RawData AS
(
    SELECT 
        PB.ProductCode,
        PB.ProductName,
        PB.PackSize AS BaseUnit,

        ISNULL(OB.Quantity, 0) AS OpeningStock,
        ISNULL(SR.TotalStockReceiveQty, 0) AS ReceiveFromCentralWarehouse,
        ISNULL(NTR.TotalStockReceiveQty, 0) AS ReturnQty,
        ISNULL(CR.TotalStockReceiveQty, 0) AS ReceiveFromAreaOfficeInterTransfer,
        ISNULL(STR.TotalStockReceiveQty, 0) AS BaseTotalReceived,

        ISNULL(CT.Challan, 0) AS IntransitWHB2B,
        ISNULL(SIT.Sales, 0) AS SalesInTransit,
        ISNULL(SL.Sales, 0) AS IssuedToSales,
        ISNULL(BN.BonusQty, 0) AS IssuedToBonus,
        ISNULL(PBNS.Sales, 0) AS IssuedToProductBonus,
        ISNULL(CI.Challan, 0) AS IssuedToAreaOfficeInterTransfer,
        ISNULL(FR.Freeze, 0) AS IssuedToDamageAndOthers,
        ISNULL(FB.Freeze, 0) AS Blocked,
        ISNULL(SI.SampleIssue, 0) AS SampleIssue,

        ISNULL(OB.Value, 0) AS OBValue,
        ISNULL(SR.Value, 0) AS ReceiveValue,
        ISNULL(NTR.Value, 0) AS NonTransReceiveValue,
        ISNULL(CR.Value, 0) AS ChallanReceiveValue,
        ISNULL(CT.Value, 0) AS ChallanTransitValue,

        ISNULL(SL.Value, 0) AS SalesValue,
        ISNULL(PBNS.Value, 0) AS ProductBonusValue,
        ISNULL(CI.Value, 0) AS ChallanIssueValue,
        ISNULL(SI.Value, 0) AS SampleIssueValue,
        ISNULL(BN.Value, 0) AS BonusValue,
        ISNULL(SIT.Value, 0) AS SalesTransitValue
    FROM ProductBase PB
    LEFT JOIN OB 
        ON OB.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN StoReceive SR 
        ON SR.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN NonTransReceive NTR 
        ON NTR.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN ChallanReceive CR 
        ON CR.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN StockReceive STR 
        ON STR.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN Sales SL 
        ON SL.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN Bonus BN 
        ON BN.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN ProductBonus PBNS 
        ON PBNS.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN SalesInTransit SIT 
        ON SIT.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN ChallanIssue CI 
        ON CI.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN ChallanTransit CT 
        ON CT.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN FreezeRestricted FR 
        ON FR.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN FreezeBlocked FB 
        ON FB.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
    LEFT JOIN SampleIssue SI 
        ON SI.ProductCode = PB.ProductCode
),

FinalData AS
(
    SELECT 
        ProductCode,
        ProductName,
        @FromDate AS FromDate,
        @ToDate AS ToDate,
        @DCName AS DC,
        BaseUnit,

        OpeningStock,
        ReceiveFromCentralWarehouse,
        ReturnQty,
        ReceiveFromAreaOfficeInterTransfer,

        BaseTotalReceived + ReturnQty AS TotalReceived,

        IntransitWHB2B,
        SalesInTransit,
        IssuedToSales,
        IssuedToBonus,
        IssuedToProductBonus,
        IssuedToAreaOfficeInterTransfer,
        IssuedToDamageAndOthers,
        Blocked,
        SampleIssue,

        (
            OpeningStock
            + ReceiveFromCentralWarehouse
            + ReturnQty
            + ReceiveFromAreaOfficeInterTransfer
            + IntransitWHB2B
        ) AS TotalStock,

        (
            IssuedToSales
            + IssuedToProductBonus
            + IssuedToAreaOfficeInterTransfer
            + SampleIssue
            + IssuedToBonus
            + SalesInTransit
        ) AS TotalIssue,

        (
            OpeningStock
            + ReceiveFromCentralWarehouse
            + ReturnQty
            + ReceiveFromAreaOfficeInterTransfer
            + IntransitWHB2B
        )
        -
        (
            IssuedToSales
            + IssuedToProductBonus
            + IssuedToAreaOfficeInterTransfer
            + SampleIssue
            + IssuedToBonus
            + SalesInTransit
        ) AS ClosingStock,

        (
            SalesValue
            + ProductBonusValue
            + ChallanIssueValue
            + SampleIssueValue
            + BonusValue
            + SalesTransitValue
        ) AS IssuedValue,

        (
            OBValue
            + ReceiveValue
            + NonTransReceiveValue
            + ChallanReceiveValue
            + ChallanTransitValue
        ) AS TotalRcvValue,

        ABS
        (
            (
                OBValue
                + ReceiveValue
                + NonTransReceiveValue
                + ChallanReceiveValue
                + ChallanTransitValue
            )
            -
            (
                SalesValue
                + ProductBonusValue
                + ChallanIssueValue
                + SampleIssueValue
                + BonusValue
                + SalesTransitValue
            )
        ) AS ClosingStockValue
    FROM RawData
)

SELECT *
FROM FinalData
WHERE 
    OpeningStock > 0
    OR ReceiveFromCentralWarehouse > 0
    OR ReturnQty > 0
    OR ReceiveFromAreaOfficeInterTransfer > 0
    OR TotalReceived > 0
    OR IntransitWHB2B > 0
    OR SalesInTransit > 0
    OR IssuedToSales > 0
    OR IssuedToBonus > 0
    OR IssuedToProductBonus > 0
    OR IssuedToAreaOfficeInterTransfer > 0
    OR IssuedToDamageAndOthers > 0
    OR Blocked > 0
    OR SampleIssue > 0
    OR TotalStock > 0
    OR TotalIssue > 0
    OR ClosingStock > 0
    OR IssuedValue > 0
    OR TotalRcvValue > 0
    OR ClosingStockValue > 0
ORDER BY 
    ProductCode;

END
```

## sp_DCStoreOpeningBalanceProcess

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** db_cursor, dbo.tblDCStore
**Tables written:** INSERT → dbo.tblDCStore_OpeningBalance

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Snapshot job with no parameters: cursors through every `tblDCStore` row with `StockQty > 0` and copies it into `tblDCStore_OpeningBalance`, stamped with the current date — used to freeze a periodic DC-level opening balance (DC-level counterpart to `sp_CSOpeningBalanceProcess`).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DCStoreOpeningBalanceProcess] 
	
AS
BEGIN
	

	declare @DCStoreOpeningBalanceDate datetime

	set @DCStoreOpeningBalanceDate = Getdate()



	

	   Declare @DCStoreId INT
       Declare @StorageLocation nvarchar(250)
       Declare @ProductCode nvarchar(250)
       Declare @ProductName nvarchar(500)
       Declare @PackSize nvarchar(250)
       Declare @BatchNo nvarchar(250)
       Declare @TotalQuantity decimal(18,0)
       Declare @ExpDate datetime
       Declare @ReceiveDate datetime
       Declare @ChalanNo nvarchar(500)
       Declare @ChalanDate datetime
       Declare @ComUnitId int 
       Declare @StockQty decimal(18,0)
       Declare @DamageQty decimal(18,0)
       Declare @StockRcvDate datetime
       Declare @ReqId int
       Declare @ReqChildId INT
       Declare @StockInTransfarId INT
       Declare @StockCondition nvarchar(250)
       Declare @ChalanDetailsId INT
       Declare @MfgDate datetime
	   Declare @TempReceiveId INT





DECLARE db_cursor CURSOR FOR 

  SELECT 
       DCStoreId ,
       StorageLocation ,
       ProductCode ,
       ProductName ,
       PackSize ,
       BatchNo ,
       TotalQuantity ,
       ExpDate ,
       ReceiveDate ,
       ChalanNo ,
       ChalanDate ,
       ComUnitId ,
       StockQty ,
       DamageQty ,
       StockRcvDate ,
       ReqId ,
       ReqChildId ,
       StockInTransfarId ,
       StockCondition ,
       ChalanDetailsId ,
       MfgDate,TempReceiveId FROM dbo.tblDCStore where StockQty>0

OPEN db_cursor  
FETCH NEXT FROM db_cursor INTO @DCStoreId ,
       @StorageLocation ,
       @ProductCode ,
       @ProductName ,
       @PackSize ,
       @BatchNo ,
       @TotalQuantity ,
       @ExpDate ,
       @ReceiveDate ,
       @ChalanNo ,
       @ChalanDate ,
       @ComUnitId ,
       @StockQty ,
       @DamageQty ,
       @StockRcvDate ,
       @ReqId ,
       @ReqChildId ,
       @StockInTransfarId ,
       @StockCondition ,
       @ChalanDetailsId ,
       @MfgDate,
	   @TempReceiveId 

WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0  
BEGIN  
      INSERT INTO dbo.tblDCStore_OpeningBalance
        (DCOpeningBalanceDate ,
          DCStoreId ,
          StorageLocation ,
          ProductCode ,
          ProductName ,
          PackSize ,
          BatchNo ,
          TotalQuantity ,
          ExpDate ,
          ReceiveDate ,
          ChalanNo ,
          ChalanDate ,
          ComUnitId ,
          StockQty ,
          DamageQty ,
          StockRcvDate ,
          ReqId ,
          ReqChildId ,
          StockInTransfarId ,
          StockCondition ,
          ChalanDetailsId ,
          MfgDate,
		  TempReceiveId
        )
VALUES  (convert(nvarchar(11),@DCStoreOpeningBalanceDate,106) , -- DCOpeningBalanceDate - datetime
       @DCStoreId ,
       @StorageLocation ,
       @ProductCode ,
       @ProductName ,
       @PackSize ,
       @BatchNo ,
       @TotalQuantity ,
       @ExpDate ,
       @ReceiveDate ,
       @ChalanNo ,
       @ChalanDate ,
       @ComUnitId ,
       @StockQty ,
       @DamageQty ,
       @StockRcvDate ,
       @ReqId ,
       @ReqChildId ,
       @StockInTransfarId ,
       @StockCondition ,
       @ChalanDetailsId ,
       @MfgDate,
	   @TempReceiveId  
        )
	  
      FETCH NEXT FROM db_cursor INTO @DCStoreId ,
       @StorageLocation ,
       @ProductCode ,
       @ProductName ,
       @PackSize ,
       @BatchNo ,
       @TotalQuantity ,
       @ExpDate ,
       @ReceiveDate ,
       @ChalanNo ,
       @ChalanDate ,
       @ComUnitId ,
       @StockQty ,
       @DamageQty ,
       @StockRcvDate ,
       @ReqId ,
       @ReqChildId ,
       @StockInTransfarId ,
       @StockCondition ,
       @ChalanDetailsId ,
       @MfgDate,
	   @TempReceiveId  

END 

CLOSE db_cursor  
DEALLOCATE db_cursor 




END
```

## sp_DEL_DuplicatePayment

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblCustomerPay, tblCustPayDetail, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** DELETE → ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblCustomerPay, tblCustPayDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given an `@InvoiceNo`, finds duplicate payment-detail rows for that invoice (rows sharing a `TransctionDetailId` that occurs more than once), keeps the first one, and for every other duplicate deletes its posted accounting vouchers (`ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster/Detail`) plus the payment/payment-detail rows themselves.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/DuplicatePaymentCorrectionDal.cs — `DeleteDuplicatePayment(string Invoice)`, via `_aCommonInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_DEL_DuplicatePayment", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DEL_DuplicatePayment] 
	
	@InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(MAX)
	
AS
BEGIN


	DECLARE @CustPayDetailId INT
	DECLARE @CustPayId INT
	    
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	---------------
	

	SELECT CPD.CustPayDetailId,CustPayId FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD 
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	WHERE INV.InvoiceNo = @InvoiceNo AND TransctionDetailId IN (SELECT TransctionDetailId FROM tblCustPayDetail
    GROUP BY TransctionDetailId HAVING COUNT(TransctionDetailId) > 1)
	AND CustPayDetailId NOT IN 
	(
		SELECT  TOP 1 CustPayDetailId FROM tblCustPayDetail CP
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CP.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId WHERE INV.InvoiceNo = @InvoiceNo AND TransctionDetailId IN (SELECT TransctionDetailId FROM tblCustPayDetail
		GROUP BY TransctionDetailId HAVING COUNT(TransctionDetailId) > 1)
	)
		
	----------
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @CustPayDetailId,@CustPayId
	
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
		BEGIN
	
	     -- INNER Courser Start
			
			DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT
			

			DECLARE @inner_cursor CURSOR
			SET @inner_cursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
			
			FOR
			---------------

			SELECT VoucherMasterId FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE CustPayDetailId = @CustPayDetailId
				
			----------
			OPEN @inner_cursor
			FETCH NEXT FROM @inner_cursor
			INTO @VoucherMasterId
			
				WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
				BEGIN

					-- DELETE Voucher

					DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId
					DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

						
				FETCH NEXT FROM @inner_cursor
				INTO @VoucherMasterId
			
			CLOSE @inner_cursor
			DEALLOCATE @inner_cursor

		END
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @CustPayDetailId,@CustPayId

		-- DELETE Payment

		DELETE FROM dbo.tblCustomerPay WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayId
		DELETE FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE CustPayDetailId = @CustPayDetailId

	END
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor

END
```

## sp_DEL_DuplicatePaymentProcess

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** PaymentBatchDetails, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblCustPayDetail, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustMaster, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblPaymentTranscationDetail, tblPaymentTranscationMaster
**Tables written:** UPDATE → dbo.tblInvoice, tblPaymentTranscationMaster; DELETE → ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Multi-part daily data-fix job with no parameters, run as several independent cursor blocks: (1) for invoices with more than one payment recorded today sharing the same amount/AIT/discount, deletes all but one duplicate payment (and its posted vouchers); (2) deletes payments that were later marked rejected (`IsReject = 1`) but never removed from `tblCustPayDetail`; (3) fixes payment-transaction rows whose recorded `PaymentDate` differs from their `CreateDate` for the current month; (4) for invoices whose stored `PaymentAmount`/`AIT`/`DiscountOnPayment` doesn't match the sum of `tblCustPayDetail`, recomputes them and also sets `PaymentStatus` to 'Full' or 'Partial'; (5) marks stale unprocessed/unbatched payment-transaction rows from before today as rejected.

**Result set:** No result set (all five blocks are cursor-driven DELETE/UPDATE only).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DEL_DuplicatePaymentProcess] 
	

	
AS
BEGIN


	--First Cursor Start

	DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(200)
	DECLARE @PaymentAmount DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @NoOfPayment INT
	DECLARE @AIT INT
	DECLARE @Discount INT
	DECLARE @PaymentDate DATETIME

	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
    ---------------
	
	SELECT  InvoiceNo,PaymentDate,PD.AIT,PD.Discount,PD.PaymentAmount,Count(CustPayDetailId) NoOfPayment FROM dbo.tblCustPayDetail AS PD WITH(NOLOCK)
    LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay  AS PM  WITH(NOLOCK) ON PD.CustPayId = PM.CustPayId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV  WITH(NOLOCK) ON PD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
    WHERE CONVERT(DATE,PaymentDate) = CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE()) 
	GROUP BY InvoiceNo,PaymentDate,PD.AIT,PD.Discount,PD.PaymentAmount
	HAVING Count(CustPayDetailId) > 1 ORDER BY InvoiceNo DESC

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceNo,@PaymentDate,@AIT,@Discount,@PaymentAmount,@NoOfPayment
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
		-- Second Cursor Start
		
		DECLARE @CustPayDetailId INT
		DECLARE @CustPayId INT
		    
		--------------------------------------------------------
		DECLARE @MyCursor2 CURSOR
		SET @MyCursor2 = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
		FOR
		---------------	

		SELECT TOP 1 CD.CustPayDetailId,CD.CustPayId FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CD
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceNo = @InvoiceNo
		AND CD.AIT = @AIT AND CD.PaymentAmount = @PaymentAmount AND CD.Discount = @Discount
		AND CONVERT(DATE,PaymentDate) = CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE()) 

			
		----------
		OPEN @MyCursor2
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor2
		INTO @CustPayDetailId,@CustPayId
		
			WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
			BEGIN
		
		     -- INNER Courser Start
				
				DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT
				

				DECLARE @inner_cursor2 CURSOR
				SET @inner_cursor2 = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
				
				FOR
				---------------

				SELECT VoucherMasterId FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE CustPayDetailId = @CustPayDetailId
					
				----------
				OPEN @inner_cursor2
				FETCH NEXT FROM @inner_cursor2
				INTO @VoucherMasterId
				
					WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
					BEGIN

						-- DELETE Voucher

						DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId
						DELETE FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

							
					FETCH NEXT FROM @inner_cursor2
					INTO @VoucherMasterId
				
				CLOSE @inner_cursor2
				DEALLOCATE @inner_cursor2

			END
			FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor2
			INTO @CustPayDetailId,@CustPayId

			-- DELETE Payment

			    

			DELETE FROM tblCustomerPay WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayId
			DELETE FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayId

		END
		CLOSE @MyCursor2
		DEALLOCATE @MyCursor2	  
		
		-- Second Cursor End 
		       
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
    INTO @InvoiceNo,@PaymentDate,@AIT,@Discount,@PaymentAmount,@NoOfPayment
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor

	-- First Cursor End


	-- New Cursor for Reject payment but Show Collection


	DECLARE @CustPayId4 INT
	        
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor4 CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor4 = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT CustPayId FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE TransctionDetailId IN (SELECT CustPayDetailId FROM tblPaymentTranscationMaster AS PM 
	LEFT JOIN tblPaymentTranscationDetail AS PD ON PM.CustPayId = PD.CustPayId
	WHERE PaymentDate >= '2024-05-21' AND IsReject = 1)

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor4
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor4
    INTO @CustPayId4
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
	
	DELETE FROM tblCustomerPay WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayId4
	DELETE FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayId4
	

	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor4
    INTO @CustPayId4 
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor4
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor4



	-- New Cursor for Reject payment but Show Collection


	DECLARE @CustPayDetailId5 INT
	DECLARE @CreateDate DATE
	        
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor5 CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor5 = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT PD.CustPayId,CAST(CreateDate AS DATE) CreateDate FROM tblPaymentTranscationDetail AS D 
	LEFT JOIN tblPaymentTranscationMaster AS PD ON D.CustPayId = PD.CustPayId
	WHERE  CAST(PaymentDate AS DATE) != CAST(CreateDate AS DATE) AND YEAR(PaymentDate) = YEAR(GEtDATE()) AND MONTH(PaymentDate) = MONTH(GEtDATE())

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor5
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor5
    INTO @CustPayDetailId5,@CreateDate
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
	
	UPDATE tblPaymentTranscationMaster SET PaymentDate = @CreateDate WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayDetailId5
	

	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor5
    INTO @CustPayDetailId5,@CreateDate
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor5
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor5


	--New Cursor Payent mismatch


	Declare @Status nvarchar(250)  
    Declare @PaymentAmount1 DECIMAL(18,2)
	Declare @DeliveryTpGrandTotal DECIMAL(18,2)
	Declare @AIT1 DECIMAL(18,2)
	Declare @Discount1 DECIMAL(18,2)
    Declare @InvoicePaymentAmount DECIMAL(18,2)
	Declare @DiscountOnPayment DECIMAL(18,2)
	Declare @InvAIT DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	        
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor3 CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor3 = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal,0) DeliveryTpGrandTotal,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount, SUM(Isnull(CPD.AIT,0)) AIT, SUM(Isnull(CPD.Discount,0)) Discount,INV.PaymentAmount,CPD.InvoiceId,INV.DiscountOnPayment,INV.AIT AS InvAIT FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId 
	--WHERE INV.InvoiceId = 468202
    GROUP BY INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal,INV.PaymentAmount,CPD.InvoiceId,INV.DiscountOnPayment,INV.AIT HAVING (ISNULL(INV.PaymentAmount,0) != SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) 
	OR ISNULL(INV.AIT,0) != SUM(CPD.AIT) OR ISNULL(INV.DiscountOnPayment,0) != SUM(CPD.Discount))
	AND ISNULL(INV.DeliveryTpGrandTotal,0) > 0

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor3
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor3
    INTO @DeliveryTpGrandTotal,@PaymentAmount1, @AIT1, @Discount1,@InvoicePaymentAmount,@InvoiceId,@DiscountOnPayment,@InvAIT
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
	

	IF(@DeliveryTpGrandTotal = (@PaymentAmount1 + @AIT1 + @Discount1))
	BEGIN
		
	    SET @Status = 'Full'
		                               
	END

	ELSE 
	BEGIN
		
	    SET @Status = 'Partial'
		                               
	END


	UPDATE dbo.tblInvoice SET PaymentAmount = @PaymentAmount1,AIT = @AIT1, DiscountOnPayment = @Discount1, PaymentStatus = @Status WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId

	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor3
    INTO @DeliveryTpGrandTotal,@PaymentAmount1, @AIT1, @Discount1,@InvoicePaymentAmount,@InvoiceId,@DiscountOnPayment,@InvAIT 
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor3
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor3


	-- New 

		DECLARE @CustPayId6 INT

	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor6 CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor6 = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT  M.CustPayId FROM tblPaymentTranscationDetail AS D
	LEFT JOIN tblPaymentTranscationMaster AS M ON D.CustPayId = M.CustPayId
	LEFT JOIN PaymentBatchDetails AS BCD ON D.CustPayDetailId = BCD.TranscationDetailId
	WHERE IsProcess = 0 AND IsReject = 0 AND BCD.TranscationDetailId IS NULL AND PaymentDate < CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE())


    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor6
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor6
    INTO @CustPayId6
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
	
		   UPDATE tblPaymentTranscationMaster SET IsReject = 1 WHERE CustPayId = @CustPayId6
		  
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor6 
    INTO @CustPayId6
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor6
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor6


END



--SELECT ComUnitName,InvoiceNo,InvoiceDate,D.PaymentAmount,D.Discount,D.AIT,PaymentDate FROM tblCustPayDetail AS D 
--LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON D.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
--LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS CI ON INV.ComUnitId = CI.ComUnitId
--LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON D.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
--WHERE TransctionDetailId IN (SELECT CustPayDetailId FROM tblPaymentTranscationMaster AS PM 
--LEFT JOIN tblPaymentTranscationDetail AS PD ON PM.CustPayId = PD.CustPayId

--SELECT GETDATE()



--DELETE FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE CustPayId = 461569
--DELETE FROM tblCustomerPay WHERE CustPayId = 461569
```
