## sp_DEL_EmpLatLong

**Parameters:**
- `( @EmpInfoId INT , @ShiftId INT )`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** [dbo].[tblEmpLatLong]
**Tables written:** DELETE → [dbo].[tblEmpLatLong]

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Deletes an employee's recorded lat/long row(s) for a specific shift, by `@EmpInfoId`/`@ShiftId`.

**Result set:** No result set (pure DELETE).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DEL_EmpLatLong]
(
     
   
    @EmpInfoId INT  ,
    @ShiftId INT  

)
AS
BEGIN
    

    delete from  [dbo].[tblEmpLatLong] where
        [EmpInfoId] =
        @EmpInfoId  and  ShiftId=@ShiftId
END
```

## sp_DEL_SampleStockIssue

**Parameters:**
- `@IssueCode NVARCHAR(MAX)`
- `@userid NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblDCStore, tblSampleIssue, tblSampleIssueTranscation
**Tables written:** INSERT → [dbo].[tblInvoiceDeleteLog], log; UPDATE → tblDCStore, tblSampleIssue; DELETE → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@IssueCode`/`@userid`, reverses a sample-stock issue: cursors through its transaction lines, adds the issued quantity back onto `tblDCStore.StockQty`, marks the sample issue `ActionStatus = 'Deleted'`, logs the deletion into `tblInvoiceDeleteLog`, and (per an inline comment block, effectively dead/commented-out code in this body) would also delete any matching posted sales voucher.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/SampleIssueDal.cs — `DeleteSampleInvoice(string invoiceNo, string userid)`, via `aInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_DEL_SampleStockIssue", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DEL_SampleStockIssue] 

  @IssueCode NVARCHAR(MAX),
  @userid NVARCHAR(MAX)

AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @IssueId INT
	DECLARE @DCStoreId INT
    Declare @Quantity DECIMAL(18,2) 

            
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT IssueId,ST.DCStoreId,ST.Quantity FROM tblSampleIssue AS SI 
	LEFT JOIN tblSampleIssueTranscation AS ST ON SI.OrderId = ST.IssueId
	WHERE SI.OrderCode = @IssueCode 

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @IssueId ,
       @DCStoreId ,
       @Quantity
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
	

	DECLARE @Stock INT
	SET @Stock = 0
	SELECT @Stock = StockQty FROM tblDCStore WHERE DCStoreId = @DCStoreId

	UPDATE tblDCStore SET StockQty = @Stock + ISNULL((@Quantity*-1),0) WHERE DCStoreId = @DCStoreId
	UPDATE tblSampleIssue SET ActionStatus = 'Deleted' WHERE OrderId = @IssueId
	
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @IssueId ,
       @DCStoreId ,
       @Quantity

	--Insert into log

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[tblInvoiceDeleteLog]
           (
            [DeleteDateTime]
           ,[SampleId]
           ,[SampleInvoiceNo]
           ,[DeleteBy])
     VALUES
           (GETDATE(),(SELECT OrderId FROM tblSampleIssue WHERE OrderCode = @IssueCode),@IssueCode,@userid)

	-- Accounts Voucher Delete

	DECLARE @SalesVoucherMasterId INT = 0
	SELECT @SalesVoucherMasterId = VoucherMasterId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE Narration LIKE '%Sample Invoice%' 
	AND SampleInvoiceId = (SELECT OrderId FROM tblSampleIssue WHERE OrderCode = @IssueCode) 
	--PRINT(@SalesVoucherMasterId)

	IF(@SalesVoucherMasterId > 0)
	BEGIN

		DELETE FROM	CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @SalesVoucherMasterId
		DELETE FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = @SalesVoucherMasterId

	END

    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
END



--SELECT DCStoreId,StockQty FROM tblDCStore WHERE DCStoreId IN (6458,6452)
```

## sp_DSB_AglingReport

**Parameters:**
- `@Parameter NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.View_CustomerMaster, tblCompanyInfo, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblRegion
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes (`EXEC`) a dynamic ageing (aged-receivables) report by region, bucketing outstanding invoice due (`DeliveryTpGrandTotal` minus payments/AIT/discount) into 0-30/31-60/61-90/91-120/121-150/151-180/180+ day buckets, with a caller-supplied `@Parameter` filter string spliced into every sub-query's WHERE clause.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: `RegionName` plus one due-amount column per ageing bucket (D10/D20/D30/D40/D50/D60/D61), restricted to regions with a non-zero due somewhere.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/SalesDashboardDal.cs — `GetAglingReport(string parameter)`, via `_aCommonInternalDal.GetDataTableAction("sp_DSB_AglingReport", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,0@CompanyId/@CompanyId5/20@CompanyId6,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DSB_AglingReport] 

	@Parameter NVARCHAR(MAX)

	
AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @Query NVARCHAR(MAX)


	SET @Query = 'SELECT RGN.RegionName,ISNULL(SUM(D9.DueAmount),0) AS D10,ISNULL(SUM(D20.DueAmount),0) AS D20, ISNULL(SUM(D30.DueAmount),0) AS D30,
	ISNULL(SUM(D40.DueAmount),0) AS D40,ISNULL(SUM(D50.DueAmount),0) AS D50,ISNULL(SUM(D60.DueAmount),0) AS D60,ISNULL(SUM(D61.DueAmount),0) D61 FROM tblRegion AS RGN
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + ' AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 180)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D61 ON RGN.RegionName = D61.RegionName   

	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'')AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + '  AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 0 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) < 31)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D9 ON RGN.RegionName = D9.RegionName 

	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + ' AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 30 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) < 61)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D20 ON RGN.RegionName = D20.RegionName 

	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + '  AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 60 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) < 91)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D30 ON RGN.RegionName = D30.RegionName 

	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + ' AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 90 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) < 121)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D40 ON RGN.RegionName = D40.RegionName 

	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + '  AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 120 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) < 151)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D50 ON RGN.RegionName = D50.RegionName

	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) DueAmount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= GETDATE() GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter + '  AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) > 150 AND DATEDIFF(DAY, InvoiceDate, GETDATE()) < 181)
	GROUP BY RegionName
	HAVING SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) > 0
	) AS D60 ON RGN.RegionName = D60.RegionName GROUP BY RGN.RegionName
	HAVING ISNULL(SUM(D9.DueAmount),0) > 0 OR ISNULL(SUM(D20.DueAmount),0) >0 OR ISNULL(SUM(D30.DueAmount),0) > 0 
	OR ISNULL(SUM(D40.DueAmount),0) > 0 OR ISNULL(SUM(D50.DueAmount),0) > 0 OR ISNULL(SUM(D60.DueAmount),0) > 0 OR ISNULL(SUM(D61.DueAmount),0) > 0'


	EXEC(@Query)

END
```

## sp_DSB_BusinessSummery

**Parameters:**
- `@Parameter1 NVARCHAR(MAX)`
- `@Parameter2 NVARCHAR(MAX)`
- `@Parameter3 NVARCHAR(MAX)`
- `@Parameter4 NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.View_CustomerMaster, tblCompanyInfo, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblRegion, tblinvoice
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes (`EXEC`) a dynamic dashboard summary by region: previous-period due, today's due, total due, previous-due collection, today's collection, and total collection, with four separate caller-supplied filter strings (`@Parameter1..4`) spliced into the four underlying sub-queries.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: `RegionName, PreviousDue, TodaysDue, TotalDue, PrevousDueCollection, TodaysCollection, TotalCollection`, restricted to regions with any non-zero figure.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/SalesDashboardDal.cs — same dashboard family as `GetAglingReport`, via `_aCommonInternalDal.GetDataTableAction("sp_DSB_BusinessSummery", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,0@CompanyId/@CompanyId5/20@CompanyId6,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DSB_BusinessSummery] 

	@Parameter1 NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@Parameter2 NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@Parameter3 NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@Parameter4 NVARCHAR(MAX)
	
AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @Query NVARCHAR(MAX)


	SET @Query = 'SELECT RGN.RegionName,ISNULL(DD.Amount,0) PreviousDue,ISNULL(DDP.Amount,0) TodaysDue,(ISNULL(DD.Amount,0) + ISNULL(DDP.Amount,0)) AS TotalDue,
	ISNULL(CTPD.PrevousDueCollection,0) PrevousDueCollection,ISNULL(CTD.TodaysCollection,0) TodaysCollection,
	(ISNULL(CTPD.PrevousDueCollection,0) + ISNULL(CTD.TodaysCollection,0)) AS TotalCollection  FROM tblRegion AS RGN
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) AS Amount FROM tblinvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter1 + '
	GROUP BY RegionName) AS DD ON RGN.RegionName = DD.RegionName 
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT RegionName,SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal - (ISNULL(PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(AIT,0)  + ISNULL(DiscountOnPayment,0))) AS Amount FROM tblinvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN (''Full'',''Partial'') AND RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter2 + '
	GROUP BY RegionName) AS DDP ON RGN.RegionName = DDP.RegionName	
	LEFT JOIN (	SELECT RegionName,ISNULL(SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount),0) AS TodaysCollection FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
	LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	WHERE RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter3 + ' GROUP BY RegionName) AS CTD ON RGN.RegionName = CTD.RegionName
	LEFT JOIN (	SELECT RegionName,ISNULL(SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount),0) AS PrevousDueCollection FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
	LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
	LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId
	WHERE RegionName IS NOT NULL ' + @Parameter4 + ' GROUP BY RegionName) AS CTPD ON RGN.RegionName = CTPD.RegionName
	GROUP BY RGN.RegionName,ISNULL(DD.Amount,0) ,ISNULL(DDP.Amount,0),ISNULL(CTD.TodaysCollection,0),ISNULL(CTPD.PrevousDueCollection,0) 
	HAVING ISNULL(DD.Amount,0) > 0 OR ISNULL(DDP.Amount,0) > 0 OR ISNULL(CTD.TodaysCollection,0) > 0 OR ISNULL(CTPD.PrevousDueCollection,0) > 0'


	EXEC(@Query)

END
```

## sp_Dashboard_Top50Customer

**Parameters:**
- `@fromDate DATETIME`
- `@toDate DATETIME`
- `@CompanyId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblCompanyInfo, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, tblCustMaster, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** For a given company and date range, ranks the top 50 customers by current-period actual sales, also computing the equivalent previous-month figures (sales, paid, due) for comparison, joining invoice/invoice-detail/payment tables.

**Result set:** Single result set: top 50 customers with previous-month and current-month ActualSales/TotalPaid/Due, ordered by current ActualSales descending.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/SalesDashboardDal.cs — via `_aCommonInternalDal.GetDataTableAction("sp_Dashboard_Top50Customer", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Dashboard_Top50Customer] 


	@fromDate DATETIME,
	@toDate DATETIME,
	@CompanyId INT


AS
BEGIN
	
	SELECT TOP 50 CSTMR.CustomerMasterId,CSTMR.CustomerCode,CSTMR.CustomerName,ISNULL(PrevActualSales,0) PrevActualSales,
		ISNULL(PrevTotalPaid,0) PrevTotalPaid,ISNULL(PrevMonthDue,0) PrevMonthDue ,ISNULL(ActualSales,0) ActualSales,ISNULL(TotalPaid,0) TotalPaid,ISNULL(MonthDue,0) AS Due FROM tblCustMaster AS CSTMR
	
	-- Previous Month Calculation

		LEFT JOIN (SELECT INV.CustomerMasterId,SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) PrevActualSales,
		PPAY.PrevTotalPaid,SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) - ISNULL(PPAY.PrevTotalPaid,0) AS PrevMonthDue FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId

		LEFT JOIN (SELECT INV.CustomerMasterId,
		SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) + SUM(ISNULL(CPD.AIT,0)) + SUM(ISNULL(CPD.Discount,0)) AS PrevTotalPaid FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE (INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus != 'Reject') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId 
		AND INV.InvoiceDate BETWEEN DATEADD(mm, DATEDIFF(mm, 0, @toDate) - 1, 0) AND DATEADD(DAY, -(DAY(@toDate)), GETDATE())
		GROUP BY INV.CustomerMasterId) AS PPAY ON INV.CustomerMasterId = PPAY.CustomerMasterId

		WHERE (INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus != 'Reject') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId AND INV.InvoiceDate 
		BETWEEN DATEADD(mm, DATEDIFF(mm, 0, @toDate) - 1, 0) AND DATEADD(DAY, -(DAY(@toDate)), GETDATE())
		GROUP BY INV.CustomerMasterId,PPAY.PrevTotalPaid) AS PSALES ON CSTMR.CustomerMasterId = PSALES.CustomerMasterId


		-- Current Month Calculation

		LEFT JOIN (SELECT INV.CustomerMasterId,SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) ActualSales,
		PAY.TotalPaid,SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) - ISNULL(PAY.TotalPaid,0) AS MonthDue FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId

		LEFT JOIN (SELECT INV.CustomerMasterId,
		SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) + SUM(ISNULL(CPD.AIT,0)) + SUM(ISNULL(CPD.Discount,0)) AS TotalPaid FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE (INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus != 'Reject') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId 
		AND INV.InvoiceDate BETWEEN @fromDate AND @toDate
		GROUP BY INV.CustomerMasterId) AS PAY ON INV.CustomerMasterId = PAY.CustomerMasterId

		WHERE (INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus != 'Reject') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId AND INV.InvoiceDate 
		BETWEEN @fromDate AND @toDate
		GROUP BY INV.CustomerMasterId,PAY.TotalPaid) AS SALES ON CSTMR.CustomerMasterId = SALES.CustomerMasterId

		ORDER BY ActualSales DESC
	
END
```

## sp_DataUpload_ProductStock

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblProduct, tblProductUpload, tblWHStockInMaster, tblWearhouse
**Tables written:** INSERT → Master, [dbo].[tblWHStockInDetail], [dbo].[tblWHStockInMaster], tblWHStockInDetail, tblWHStockInMaster; UPDATE → tblWHStockInMaster, the, total

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Bulk-upload helper with no parameters: cursors through a `tblProductUpload` staging table joined to `tblProduct`, creates one `tblWHStockInMaster` header (generating a sequential `WHS-#######` code, hardcoded challan/reference/supplier values) the first iteration only, then inserts one `tblWHStockInDetail` line per uploaded product row, finally updating the master's total quantity/value.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DataUpload_ProductStock]
  
AS
BEGIN

	

	DECLARE @ProductId INT
	DECLARE @CompanyId INT
	DECLARE @Batch NVARCHAR(200)
	DECLARE @ExpDate DATETIME
	DECLARE @MfgDate DATETIME
	DECLARE @StockQuantity DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @UnitPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @VAT DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @TotalValue DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @MasterInsertCount INT = 0
	DECLARE @WHStockInMasterID INT = 0
	
	DECLARE @TotalQuantity DECIMAL(18,2) = 0
	DECLARE @GrandTotalValue DECIMAL(18,2) = 0
	
	--------------------------------------------------------
	
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	
	SELECT PD.ProductId,
	       'A1234' AS Batch,
	       GETDATE() AS ExpDate,
	       DATEADD(YEAR, 2, GETDATE()) AS MfgDate,  -- Adding 2 years to current date
	       PUD.StockQuantity,
	       PUD.PurchaseRate AS Price,
	       0 AS VAT,
	       PUD.TotalValue, 
	       PUD.CompanyId  
	FROM tblProductUpload AS PUD
	INNER JOIN tblProduct AS PD ON PUD.ProductCode = PD.ProductCode;
	
	---------- Cursor Operations ----------
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @ProductId, @Batch, @ExpDate, @MfgDate, @StockQuantity, @UnitPrice, @VAT, @TotalValue, @CompanyId
	
	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN

	    -- Insert into Master Table (First Iteration Only)

	    IF (@MasterInsertCount = 0)
	    BEGIN

	        DECLARE @NextWHStockInCode NVARCHAR(50);
	        DECLARE @Prefix NVARCHAR(10) = 'WHS-';
	        DECLARE @NextNumber INT;
	
	        -- Fetch last WHStockInCode and generate the next one
	        SELECT @NextNumber = ISNULL(MAX(CAST(SUBSTRING(WHStockInCode, 5, LEN(WHStockInCode) - 4) AS INT)), 0) + 1
	        FROM tblWHStockInMaster;
	        
	        -- Generate next sequential code
	        SET @NextWHStockInCode = @Prefix + FORMAT(@NextNumber, '0000000');
	
	        -- Generate next WHStockInMasterID
	        SELECT @WHStockInMasterID = ISNULL(MAX(WHStockInMasterID), 0) + 1 FROM tblWHStockInMaster;
	        
	        -- Insert into tblWHStockInMaster
	        INSERT INTO [dbo].[tblWHStockInMaster]
	               (WHStockInMasterID
	               ,WHStockInCode
	               ,WarehouseId
	               ,WHStockInDate
	               ,TotalQuantity
	               ,TotalVat
	               ,TotalValue
	               ,ChallanNo
	               ,ChallanDate
	               ,ReferenceNo
	               ,ReferenceDate
	               ,Remarks
	               ,Status
	               ,EntryBy
	               ,EntryDate
	               ,SupplierId
	               ,IsPosting)
	        VALUES
	               (@WHStockInMasterID,
	                @NextWHStockInCode,  -- Generated Code
	               (SELECT TOP 1 WearhouseId FROM tblWearhouse WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId),
	               GETDATE(),
	               0,   -- Default TotalQuantity
	               0,   -- Default TotalVat
	               0,   -- Default TotalValue
	               'CHL-12345',  -- Example ChallanNo
	               GETDATE(),
	               'REF-12345',  -- Example ReferenceNo
	               GETDATE(),
	               'Purchase',
	               'Posted',  -- Example Status (e.g., Active)
	               'Auto Process',  -- Example EntryBy
	               GETDATE(),
	               5,  -- Example SupplierId
	               0   -- Example IsPosting
	               );
	
	        -- Flag to prevent duplicate master insert
	        SET @MasterInsertCount = 1
	    END
	
	    -- Update total quantity and value
	    SET @TotalQuantity = @TotalQuantity + @StockQuantity
	    SET @GrandTotalValue = @GrandTotalValue + @TotalValue
	
	    -- Insert into tblWHStockInDetail
	    INSERT INTO [dbo].[tblWHStockInDetail]
	           (WHStockInMasterID
	           ,ProductId
	           ,Batch
	           ,ExpDate
	           ,MfgDate
	           ,Qty
	           ,Price
	           ,VAT
	           ,TotalAmount)
	    VALUES
	           (@WHStockInMasterID
	           ,@ProductId
	           ,@Batch
	           ,@ExpDate
	           ,@MfgDate
	           ,@StockQuantity
	           ,@UnitPrice
	           ,@VAT
	           ,@TotalValue);
	
	    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
	    INTO @ProductId, @Batch, @ExpDate, @MfgDate, @StockQuantity, @UnitPrice, @VAT, @TotalValue, @CompanyId
	END
	
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
	-- Update the master record with the total quantity and value
	UPDATE tblWHStockInMaster 
	SET TotalQuantity = @TotalQuantity, 
	    TotalValue = @GrandTotalValue 
	WHERE WHStockInMasterID = @WHStockInMasterID;

  
 
END
 
```

## sp_DataUpload_ProductandPrice

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblProductUpload, tblUnitPrice
**Tables written:** INSERT → [dbo].[tblProduct], [dbo].[tblUnitPrice]

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Bulk-upload helper with no parameters: cursors through every row of the `tblProductUpload` staging table and, for each, inserts a new `tblProduct` row (hardcoded flags: active, trade/recipe both off) and a corresponding `tblUnitPrice` row (using a `MAX(UnitPriceId)+1` pattern for the new price id, which is unsafe under concurrent execution).

**Result set:** No result set (a `PRINT @ProductId` per row is debug output only).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DataUpload_ProductandPrice]
  
AS
BEGIN

	

	DECLARE @CompanyId INT
    DECLARE @ProductCode NVARCHAR(200)
    DECLARE @ProductName NVARCHAR(MAX)
    DECLARE @Description NVARCHAR(MAX)
    DECLARE @PackSize NVARCHAR(200)
    DECLARE @BrandId INT
    DECLARE @PackSizeId INT
    DECLARE @TypeId INT
    DECLARE @CategoryId INT
    DECLARE @ManufacturerId INT
    DECLARE @UOMId INT
    DECLARE @CaseId INT
	DECLARE @UnitPrice decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @MRPPrice decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @CostPrice decimal(18,2)
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT CompanyId
           ,ProductCode
           ,ProductName
           ,Description
           ,PackSize
           ,BrandId
           ,PackSizeId
           ,TypeId
           ,CategoryId
           ,ManufacturerId
           ,UOMId
           ,CaseId, UnitPrice, MRPPrice, CostPrice FROM tblProductUpload

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @CompanyId
           ,@ProductCode
           ,@ProductName
           ,@Description
           ,@PackSize
           ,@BrandId
           ,@PackSizeId
           ,@TypeId
           ,@CategoryId
           ,@ManufacturerId
           ,@UOMId
           ,@CaseId, @UnitPrice, @MRPPrice,@CostPrice
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
	

	DECLARE @ProductId int = 0

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[tblProduct]
           (CompanyId
           ,ProductCode
           ,ProductName
           ,Description
           ,PackSize
           ,ProductBrandId
           ,PackSizeId
           ,ProTypeId
           ,CategoryId
           ,ManufacId
           ,StockUOMId
           ,CaseId
           ,IsActiveProduct
           ,ActiveInactiveBy
           ,ActiveInactiveDate
           ,IsTrade
           ,IsRecipe)
     VALUES
           (@CompanyId
           ,@ProductCode
           ,@ProductName
           ,@Description
           ,@PackSize
           ,@BrandId
           ,@PackSizeId
           ,@TypeId
           ,@CategoryId
           ,@ManufacturerId
           ,@UOMId
           ,@CaseId
		   ,1
		   ,1
		   , GETDATE()
           ,0
           ,0)


		  SET @ProductId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		  print @ProductId

		  INSERT INTO [dbo].[tblUnitPrice]
           (UnitPriceId
           ,ProductId
           ,ProductCode
           ,ProductName
           ,PackSize
           ,CostPrice
           ,UnitPrice
           ,MRPPrice
           ,VATPercentage
           ,VATAmountPerUnit
           ,MusakVATPercentage
           ,MusakVATAmountPerUnit
           ,TPVat
           ,MusakVat
           ,IsActive
           ,ActiveDate
           ,ActionStatus
           ,EntryBy
           ,EntryDate)
     VALUES
           ((SELECT MAX(UnitPriceId) + 1 FROM tblUnitPrice) -- Corrected subquery
           ,@ProductId
           ,@ProductCode
           ,@ProductName
           ,@PackSize
           ,@CostPrice 
           ,@UnitPrice
           ,@MRPPrice
           ,0
           ,0
           ,0
           ,0
           ,0
           ,0
           ,1
           ,GETDATE()
           ,'Posted'
           ,'Auto Process'
           ,GETDATE());


    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
    INTO @CompanyId
           ,@ProductCode
           ,@ProductName
           ,@Description
           ,@PackSize
           ,@BrandId
           ,@PackSizeId
           ,@TypeId
           ,@CategoryId
           ,@ManufacturerId
           ,@UOMId
           ,@CaseId,@UnitPrice,@MRPPrice,@CostPrice
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
  
 
END
 
```

## sp_DeleteDeliveryInvoice

**Parameters:**
- `@DelivaryInvoiceNo nvarchar(500)`
- `@userid nvarchar(500)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblDCStore, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail, tblCustPayDetail, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** INSERT → tblInvoiceDeleteLog; UPDATE → dbo.tblInvoiceDetail, tblDCStore, tblInvoice; DELETE → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@DelivaryInvoiceNo`/`@userid`, and only if no customer payment has ever been recorded against it: reverses the delivery by adding the delivered-but-not-shipped quantity difference back onto DC stock and clearing any related freeze rows, blanks out all the delivery-related columns on the invoice detail/header, logs the deletion in `tblInvoiceDeleteLog`, and (per inline comment blocks, effectively dead/commented-out) would also delete matching posted sales/inventory vouchers.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/ProformaOrInvoiceReturn.cs — via `aCommonInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_DeleteDeliveryInvoice", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:	<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeleteDeliveryInvoice]

	@DelivaryInvoiceNo nvarchar(500),
    @userid nvarchar(500)

AS
BEGIN


--SELECT * FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CP
--LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS I ON CP.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
--WHERE DelivaryInvoiceNo='DEL-INV-BD159-000000384'


	IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT DelivaryInvoiceNo FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CP
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS I ON CP.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId WHERE DelivaryInvoiceNo=@DelivaryInvoiceNo)	
	BEGIN
	DECLARE @OrderIdForUpdate int
	
	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	DECLARE @InvoiceDetailId int
	DECLARE @DCStoreId int
	DECLARE @OrderId int
	DECLARE @OrderDetailsId int
	
	DECLARE @TotalQuantity DECIMAL(18,0)=0
	DECLARE @DeliveryTotalQuantity DECIMAL(18,0)=0
	
	DECLARE @TempQuantity DECIMAL(18,0)=0
	
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	
	SELECT I.InvoiceId,ID.InvoiceDetailId,ISNULL(ID.DCStoreId,0)DCStoreId,I.OrderId,ID.OrderDetailsId,ID.TotalQuantity,ISNULL(ID.DeliveryTotalQuantity,0)DeliveryTotalQuantity FROM dbo.tblInvoice I INNER JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail ID ON ID.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
	WHERE I.DelivaryInvoiceNo=@DelivaryInvoiceNo ORDER BY ID.InvoiceDetailId
	
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @InvoiceId,@InvoiceDetailId,@DCStoreId,@OrderId,@OrderDetailsId,@TotalQuantity,@DeliveryTotalQuantity
	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN
	
	SELECT @TempQuantity=StockQty FROM dbo.tblDCStore WHERE DCStoreId=@DCStoreId
	SET @TempQuantity=@TempQuantity-(@TotalQuantity-@DeliveryTotalQuantity)
	UPDATE tblDCStore SET StockQty=@TempQuantity WHERE DCStoreId=@DCStoreId
	delete tblDCStoreFreeze where InvoiceDetailId=@InvoiceDetailId
	
	
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @InvoiceId,@InvoiceDetailId,@DCStoreId,@OrderId,@OrderDetailsId,@TotalQuantity,@DeliveryTotalQuantity
	END
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
	UPDATE dbo.tblInvoiceDetail SET 
	
	DeliveryQuantity =NULL,
	DeliveryBonusQuantity =NULL,
	DeliveryTotalQuantity =NULL,
	DeliveryTotalPrice =NULL,
	DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount =NULL,
	DeliveryDiscountPercentage =NULL,
	DeliveryDiscountAmount =NULL,
	DeliveryNetAmount =NULL,
	DeliveryStatus =NULL,
	
	
	DelivarySpecialAmount =NULL,
	ReturnReason =NULL WHERE InvoiceId=(SELECT InvoiceId FROM tblInvoice WHERE DelivaryInvoiceNo=@DelivaryInvoiceNo)
	
	
	update tblInvoice set DelivaryInvoiceNo=NULL , DeliveryInvoiceStatus=NULL WHERE DelivaryInvoiceNo=@DelivaryInvoiceNo
	
	DECLARE @InvoiceIdForLog INT
	DECLARE @InvoiceNoForLog NVARCHAR(500)
	SELECT @InvoiceIdForLog=InvoiceId,@InvoiceNoForLog=InvoiceNo FROM tblInvoice WHERE DelivaryInvoiceNo=@DelivaryInvoiceNo
	
	INSERT INTO tblInvoiceDeleteLog (InvoiceId,InvoiceNo,DelInvoiceNo,DeleteDateTime,DeleteBy)
	VALUES (@InvoiceIdForLog,@InvoiceNoForLog,@DelivaryInvoiceNo,GETDATE(),@userid)
	

	    --Accounts Voucher Delete

		DECLARE @SalesVoucherMasterId INT = 0
		SELECT @SalesVoucherMasterId = VoucherMasterId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE Narration LIKE '%Delivery Sales Confirmation%' AND ProformaId = @InvoiceIdForLog 
		--PRINT(@SalesVoucherMasterId)

		IF(@SalesVoucherMasterId > 0)
		BEGIN

			DELETE FROM	CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @SalesVoucherMasterId
			DELETE FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = @SalesVoucherMasterId

		END

		DECLARE @InventoryVoucherMasterId INT = 0
		SELECT @InventoryVoucherMasterId = VoucherMasterId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE Narration LIKE '%Delivery Confirmation Full, Inventory Valuation%' AND ProformaId = @InvoiceIdForLog 
		--PRINT(@InventoryVoucherMasterId)

		IF(@InventoryVoucherMasterId > 0)
		BEGIN

			DELETE FROM	CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @InventoryVoucherMasterId
			DELETE FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = @InventoryVoucherMasterId

		END
	END

END
```

## sp_DeleteOrder

**Parameters:**
- `@OrdID INT`
- `@LoginName NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblOrder, dbo.tblOrderDetail
**Tables written:** INSERT → dbo.tblOrderDel, dbo.tblOrderDetailDel; DELETE → dbo.tblOrder, dbo.tblOrderDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@OrdID`/`@LoginName`, and only if the order hasn't yet been invoiced (`IsInvoice = 0`): archives the order header and its detail lines into `tblOrderDel`/`tblOrderDetailDel` (audit/soft-delete tables) stamped with who deleted it and when, then hard-deletes the live order and its detail rows.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/CustomerMasterDAL.cs — `DeleteRequisition(int OrdID)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.DeleteAction("sp_DeleteOrder", ...)` (an earlier, differently-named overload of the same method is commented out in the same file).

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeleteOrder]
	@OrdID INT,
	@LoginName NVARCHAR(MAX)
AS
BEGIN

DECLARE @OrderCode NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @ISInvoice bit

SELECT @OrderCode=OrderCode FROM dbo.tblOrder WHERE OrderId=@OrdID 
SELECT @ISInvoice=IsInvoice FROM dbo.tblOrder WHERE OrderId=@OrdID 
if(@ISInvoice =0)
BEGIN
INSERT INTO dbo.tblOrderDel
        ( OrderId ,
          OrderCode ,
          ComUnitId ,
          ComUnitCode ,
          ComUnitName ,
          MIOCode ,
          MIOName ,
          ManufacId ,
          CustomerCode ,
          CustomerName ,
          GrossValue ,
          SubmissionDate ,
          IsInvoice ,
          IsManual,
          DeleteBy,
          DeleteDate
        )
SELECT  OrderId ,
        OrderCode ,
        ComUnitId ,
        ComUnitCode ,
        ComUnitName ,
        MIOCode ,
        MIOName ,
        ManufacId ,
        CustomerCode ,
        CustomerName ,
        GrossValue ,
        SubmissionDate ,
        IsInvoice ,
        IsManual,@LoginName,GETDATE() FROM dbo.tblOrder WHERE OrderId=@OrdID


INSERT INTO dbo.tblOrderDetailDel
        ( OrderDetailId ,
          ProductId ,
          ProductCode ,
          ProductName ,
          Quantity ,
          TradePrice ,
          TotalTradePrice ,
          OrderId ,
          OrderListDetailId ,
          Status
        )
SELECT	 OrderDetailId ,
        ProductId ,
        ProductCode ,
        ProductName ,
        Quantity ,
        TradePrice ,
        TotalTradePrice ,
        OrderId ,
        OrderListDetailId ,
        Status FROM dbo.tblOrderDetail WHERE OrderId=@OrdID

DELETE  FROM dbo.tblOrder WHERE OrderId=@OrdID
DELETE  FROM dbo.tblOrderDetail WHERE OrderId=@OrdID

END

END
```

## sp_Delete_ProformaInvoice

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceCode NVARCHAR(500)`
- `@userid nvarchar(500)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, ZAS_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, dbo.tblCompanyInfo, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblDCStore, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail, tblProInvoiceReturnTrack
**Tables written:** INSERT → [dbo].[tblInvoiceDetail_DeleterRecord], tblInvoiceDeleteLog, tblProInvoiceReturnTrack; UPDATE → dbo.tblOrder, dbo.tblOrderDetail, tblDCStore; DELETE → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, dbo.tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@InvoiceCode`/`@userid`, and only if the proforma invoice exists: cursors its detail lines, for any line already received into a DC store adds the quantity back to `tblDCStore.StockQty`, archives each detail row into `tblInvoiceDetail_DeleterRecord` and records the reversal in `tblProInvoiceReturnTrack`, deletes the live detail rows, clears the parent order's invoiced flag, logs the deletion, then hard-deletes the invoice header itself; the final accounting-voucher cleanup block is commented out (dead code).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/ProformaOrInvoiceReturn.cs — `DeleteProformaDal(string Invoice, string userId)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_Delete_ProformaInvoice", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE   [dbo].[sp_Delete_ProformaInvoice] 
	
	@InvoiceCode NVARCHAR(500),
    @userid nvarchar(500)

AS
BEGIN


	--DECLARE @InvoiceCode NVARCHAR(500) = 'INV-BD202-2502130006';




	
	IF EXISTS(SELECT * FROM dbo.tblInvoice WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(InvoiceNo)))= LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@InvoiceCode))))
	BEGIN


	DECLARE @OrderIdForUpdate int

	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	DECLARE @InvoiceDetailId int
	DECLARE @DCStoreId int
	DECLARE @OrderId int
	DECLARE @OrderDetailsId int
	
	DECLARE @TotalQuantity DECIMAL(18,0)=0
	
	DECLARE @TempQuantity DECIMAL(18,0)=0
	
	---
	DECLARE @ProductCode nvarchar(max)
	DECLARE @ProductName nvarchar(max)
	DECLARE @PackSize nvarchar(max)
	DECLARE @BatchNo nvarchar(max)
	DECLARE @ReceiveDate datetime
	DECLARE @ExpDate datetime
	DECLARE @CostPrice decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @UnitPrice decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @UnitVatAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @Quantity decimal(18,0)
	DECLARE @BonusQuantity decimal(18,0)
	--DECLARE @TotalQuantity decimal(18,0)
	DECLARE @TotalPrice decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @TotalPriceVatAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DiscountPercentage decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DiscountAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @NetAmount decimal(18,2)
	--DECLARE @InvoiceId int
	--DECLARE @DCStoreId int
	DECLARE @DeliveryQuantity decimal(18,0)
	DECLARE @DeliveryBonusQuantity decimal(18,0)
	DECLARE @DeliveryTotalQuantity decimal(18,0)
	DECLARE @DeliveryTotalPrice decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DeliveryDiscountPercentage decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DeliveryDiscountAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DeliveryNetAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DeliveryStatus nvarchar(50)
	--DECLARE @OrderDetailsId int
	DECLARE @SpecialAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @DelivarySpecialAmount decimal(18,2)
	DECLARE @ReturnReason nvarchar(500)
	---

	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	
	SELECT I.InvoiceId,ID.InvoiceDetailId,ISNULL(ID.DCStoreId,0)DCStoreId,I.OrderId,ID.OrderDetailsId,ID.TotalQuantity,
	ProductCode ,ProductName ,PackSize ,BatchNo ,ReceiveDate ,ExpDate ,CostPrice ,UnitPrice ,UnitVatAmount ,Quantity ,BonusQuantity ,TotalPrice ,TotalPriceVatAmount ,DiscountPercentage ,DiscountAmount ,
	NetAmount ,DeliveryQuantity ,DeliveryBonusQuantity ,DeliveryTotalQuantity ,DeliveryTotalPrice ,DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount ,DeliveryDiscountPercentage ,DeliveryDiscountAmount ,DeliveryNetAmount ,
	DeliveryStatus ,SpecialAmount ,I.DelivarySpecialAmount ,ReturnReason 
	FROM dbo.tblInvoice I 
	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail ID ON ID.InvoiceId = I.InvoiceId
	WHERE I.DelivaryInvoiceNo IS NULL AND I.InvoiceNo= @InvoiceCode 
	ORDER BY ID.InvoiceDetailId
	
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @InvoiceId,@InvoiceDetailId,@DCStoreId,@OrderId,@OrderDetailsId,@TotalQuantity,
				@ProductCode ,
				@ProductName ,
				@PackSize ,
				@BatchNo ,
				@ReceiveDate ,
				@ExpDate ,
				@CostPrice ,
				@UnitPrice ,
				@UnitVatAmount ,
				@Quantity ,
				@BonusQuantity ,
				@TotalPrice ,
				@TotalPriceVatAmount ,
				@DiscountPercentage ,
				@DiscountAmount ,----------
				@NetAmount ,
				@DeliveryQuantity ,
				@DeliveryBonusQuantity ,
				@DeliveryTotalQuantity ,
				@DeliveryTotalPrice ,
				@DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount ,
				@DeliveryDiscountPercentage ,
				@DeliveryDiscountAmount ,
				@DeliveryNetAmount ,-----
				@DeliveryStatus ,
				@SpecialAmount ,
				@DelivarySpecialAmount ,
				@ReturnReason 

	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN


	--SELECT * FROM tblProInvoiceReturnTrack


	IF(@DCStoreId > 0)
	BEGIN

		SELECT @TempQuantity=StockQty FROM dbo.tblDCStore WHERE DCStoreId=@DCStoreId
		SET @TempQuantity=@TempQuantity+@TotalQuantity
		UPDATE tblDCStore SET StockQty=@TempQuantity  WHERE DCStoreId=@DCStoreId

		---

		insert into tblProInvoiceReturnTrack (ReturnTotalQuantity,DCStoreId,ReturnDate,ReturnExecutionDateTime,InvoiceDetailId,InvoiceId)
		values (@TotalQuantity,@DCStoreId,convert(nvarchar(11),getdate(),106),getdate(),@InvoiceDetailId,@InvoiceId)
		
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[tblInvoiceDetail_DeleterRecord]
	           ([InvoiceDetailId]
	           ,[ProductCode]
	           ,[ProductName]
	           ,[PackSize]
	           ,[BatchNo]
	           ,[ReceiveDate]
	           ,[ExpDate]
	           ,[CostPrice]
	           ,[UnitPrice]
	           ,[UnitVatAmount]
	           ,[Quantity]
	           ,[BonusQuantity]
	           ,[TotalQuantity]
	           ,[TotalPrice]
	           ,[TotalPriceVatAmount]
	           ,[DiscountPercentage]
	           ,[DiscountAmount]
	           ,[NetAmount]
	           ,[InvoiceId]
	           ,[DCStoreId]
	           ,[DeliveryQuantity]
	           ,[DeliveryBonusQuantity]
	           ,[DeliveryTotalQuantity]
	           ,[DeliveryTotalPrice]
	           ,[DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount]
	           ,[DeliveryDiscountPercentage]
	           ,[DeliveryDiscountAmount]
	           ,[DeliveryNetAmount]
	           ,[DeliveryStatus]
	           ,[OrderDetailsId]
	           ,[SpecialAmount]
	           ,[DelivarySpecialAmount]
	           ,[ReturnReason])
			VALUES
	           (    @InvoiceDetailId,
					@ProductCode ,
					@ProductName ,
					@PackSize ,
					@BatchNo ,
					@ReceiveDate ,
					@ExpDate ,
					@CostPrice ,
					@UnitPrice ,
					@UnitVatAmount ,
					@Quantity ,
					@BonusQuantity ,
					@TotalQuantity ,
					@TotalPrice ,
					@TotalPriceVatAmount ,
					@DiscountPercentage ,
					@DiscountAmount ,
					@NetAmount ,
					@InvoiceId ,
					@DCStoreId ,
					@DeliveryQuantity ,
					@DeliveryBonusQuantity ,
					@DeliveryTotalQuantity ,
					@DeliveryTotalPrice ,
					@DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount ,
					@DeliveryDiscountPercentage ,
					@DeliveryDiscountAmount ,
					@DeliveryNetAmount ,
					@DeliveryStatus ,
					@OrderDetailsId ,
					@SpecialAmount ,
					@DelivarySpecialAmount ,
					@ReturnReason 
			   )
	 
	
		DELETE FROM  tblInvoiceDetail WHERE InvoiceDetailId=@InvoiceDetailId

	END
	
	
	
	 
	SET @OrderIdForUpdate=@OrderId
	 
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @InvoiceId,@InvoiceDetailId,@DCStoreId,@OrderId,@OrderDetailsId,@TotalQuantity,
	
	
	@ProductCode ,
					@ProductName ,
					@PackSize ,
					@BatchNo ,
					@ReceiveDate ,
					@ExpDate ,
					@CostPrice ,
					@UnitPrice ,
					@UnitVatAmount ,
					@Quantity ,
					@BonusQuantity ,
					
					@TotalPrice ,
					@TotalPriceVatAmount ,
					@DiscountPercentage ,
					@DiscountAmount ,----------
					@NetAmount ,
					
					
					@DeliveryQuantity ,
					@DeliveryBonusQuantity ,
					@DeliveryTotalQuantity ,
					@DeliveryTotalPrice ,
					@DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount ,
					@DeliveryDiscountPercentage ,
					@DeliveryDiscountAmount ,
					@DeliveryNetAmount ,-----
					@DeliveryStatus ,
					
					@SpecialAmount ,
					@DelivarySpecialAmount ,
					@ReturnReason 
	END
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor


	-- Invoice Detail Log
	
	DECLARE @InvoiceIdForLog INT
	DECLARE @InvoiceNoForLog NVARCHAR(500)
	SELECT @InvoiceIdForLog=InvoiceId,@InvoiceNoForLog=InvoiceNo FROM tblInvoice WHERE  InvoiceNo=@InvoiceCode
	
	UPDATE dbo.tblOrderDetail SET [Status]=NULL WHERE OrderId=@OrderIdForUpdate
	UPDATE dbo.tblOrder SET IsInvoice=0 WHERE OrderId=@OrderIdForUpdate 

	INSERT INTO tblInvoiceDeleteLog (InvoiceId,InvoiceNo,DeleteDateTime,DeleteBy)
	VALUES (@InvoiceIdForLog,@InvoiceNoForLog,GETDATE(),@userid)

	DELETE FROM dbo.tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceNo=@InvoiceCode

	--	--Accounts Voucher Delete

	--	DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT = 0
	--	SELECT @VoucherMasterId = VoucherMasterId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE Narration LIKE '%Proforma Invoice%' AND ProformaId = @InvoiceIdForLog 
		
	--	IF(@VoucherMasterId > 0)
	--	BEGIN

	--		DELETE FROM	CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId
	--		DELETE FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail WHERE VoucherMasterId = @VoucherMasterId

	--	END
	
	END
END


--SELECT DISTINCT FinancialYearId FROM dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT
--                             INNER JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON CI.CompanyId = UNT.CompanyId
--                             INNER JOIN ZAS_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear ON tblFinancialYear.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
--                             WHERE UNT.ComUnitId = 1 AND Status IN ('Active') AND '22-Sep-2020' BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate
```

## sp_Deletenvoice

**Parameters:**
- `@OrderID int`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblDCStore, tblOrder; DELETE → tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@OrderID`, reverses and deletes the invoice tied to that order: adds each invoice-detail line's `TotalQuantity` back onto the corresponding DC store's stock, then hard-deletes the invoice header/detail rows, and finally resets the parent order's `IsInvoice` flag back to 0 so it can be re-invoiced.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/InvoiceDAL.cs — `DeleteInvoice(string Invoice)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_Deletenvoice", ...)` (a second call site further down the same file is commented out).

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Deletenvoice]

	@OrderID int

AS
BEGIN

	-- Stock Adjustment

	DECLARE @DCStoreId INT
	DECLARE @TotalQuantity DECIMAL(18,2)

    --------------------------------------------------------

    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR

    ---------------

	SELECT DCStoreId,TotalQuantity FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS IVD ON INV.InvoiceId = IVD.InvoiceId
	WHERE INV.OrderId = @OrderID -- @OrderID

    ---------------

    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @DCStoreId,@TotalQuantity
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
		UPDATE tblDCStore SET StockQty = ISNULL(StockQty,0) + ISNULL(@TotalQuantity,0) WHERE DCStoreId = @DCStoreId
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @DCStoreId,@TotalQuantity
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor 	-- End of Stock Adjustment
	
	
	-- Delete Operation

	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT  = 0
	SELECT @InvoiceId = ISNULL(InvoiceId,0) FROM tblInvoice WHERE OrderId = @OrderID

	-- Delete Invoice

	IF(@InvoiceId > 0)
	BEGIN

		DELETE FROM tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		DELETE FROM tblInvoiceDetail WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId

	END

	-- Active Order to Invoice Mode

	UPDATE tblOrder SET IsInvoice = 0 WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
	

END
```

## sp_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblCentralStore, tblCompanyUnit, tblDCStore, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster; UPDATE → tblInvoice

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Restricted to `CompanyId = 16`; given `@InvoiceId`, computes the invoice's net sales/discount and stock cost, generates two voucher codes, and would post a 'Delivery Sales Confirmation Full' Debit(customer GL)/Debit(discount GL, if any)/Credit voucher plus a separate 'Delivery Confirmation Full, Inventory Valuation' Debit/Credit voucher into `CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster/Detail`, finally flagging the invoice `IsPosting` accordingly — the self-contained (creates-its-own-voucher-header) counterpart to `sp_ADJ_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting`, which only appends detail rows to a voucher created elsewhere.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Referenced only from commented-out code — Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/InvoiceDAL.cs `PostDeliveryConfFullJournal(int invoiceId)` and Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/RequisitionDAL.cs `ProcessWeightedAvg(int stockInTransferId)` both have the call line commented out. Effectively UNUSED in the active application.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting] 

	@InvoiceId INT
	
AS
BEGIN
	

	DECLARE @TempCompanyId INT = 0
	
	SELECT @TempCompanyId = CompanyId FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS CU ON INV.ComUnitId = CU.ComUnitId
	WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId


	IF(@TempCompanyId = 16)
	BEGIN

		DECLARE @ProformaInvId INT
		DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME
		DECLARE @SalesDate DATETIME
		DECLARE @CompanyId INT
		DECLARE @CustomerMasterId INT
		DECLARE @CostPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
		DECLARE @DiscontPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
		DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(500)
		
		
		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT 
		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId2 INT       
		--------------------------------------------------------
		DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
		SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
		FOR
		---------------
		
		SELECT INV.InvoiceId,INV.UpdateDate,UNT.CompanyId,SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice,0))-SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryDiscountAmount,0) + ISNULL(INVD.DelivarySpecialAmount,0)) AS TotalPrice,
		SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryDiscountAmount,0) + ISNULL(INVD.DelivarySpecialAmount,0)) AS Discount,INV.CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo,INV.InvoiceDate FROM tblInvoice AS INV
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INVD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD.DCStoreId
		INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
		INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.UpdateDate,CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo,DeliveryTpDiscount,INV.InvoiceDate


		----------
		OPEN @MyCursor
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @ProformaInvId,@SalesDate,@CompanyId,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo,@InvDate
		
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
		BEGIN
		

		-- Master Variable Info
		-------------------------------------

		DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(MAX) ='Journal'
		DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

		DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@SalesDate)), 3, 3)   
		DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@SalesDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@SalesDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@SalesDate)) END)
		DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@SalesDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@SalesDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@SalesDate)) END)
		SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText+@monthText+@dateText)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
		WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
		
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='DVB-'+@VoucherCode	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='DVC-'+@VoucherCode
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='CVB-'+@VoucherCode	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='CVC-'+@VoucherCode
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='FUT-'+@VoucherCode
			END

			IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='JVP-'+@VoucherCode
			END


		DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''

		SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
		WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND @InvDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

		----------------------------------

		-- Master Information Posting

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
		(
		    VoucherCode,
		    CompanyInfoId,
		    FinancialYearId,
		    PostingDate,
		    VoucherCategoryID,
		    CurrencyId,
		    CurrencyCovRate,
		    Narration,
		    TotalAmount,
		    TotalAmountBDT,
		    EntryBy,
		    EntryDate,
		    VoucherType, 
		    JournalType,
		    ProformaId,
			IsCostCenter,
			IsInvoice,
			isManualVCRNo,
			IsExportInvoice,
			IsImportInvoice

		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
		    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
		    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
		    @SalesDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
		    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
		    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
		    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Delivery Sales Confirmation Full - ( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Invoice Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Sales Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @CostPrice) + ', Discount: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @DiscontPrice) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
		    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
		    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
		    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
		    'Receivable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
		    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
			0,0,0,0,0
		)


		-- Detail Information Posting

		SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		--SELECT @VoucherMasterId = VoucherMasterId FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE VoucherCode = @VoucherCode

		DECLARE	@DrGl1 INT 
		SELECT @DrGl1 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfirmationFull') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr1')

		-- Debit 1 (Customer GL)

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGl1,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)

		IF(@DiscontPrice > 0)
		BEGIN


			DECLARE	@DrGl INT 
			SELECT @DrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfirmationFull') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr2')


			-- Debit 2

			INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
			(
			    VoucherMasterId,
			    AccountId,
			    DebitorCredit,
			    DebitAmount,
			    CreditAmount,
			    DebitAmountBDT,
			    CreditAmountBDT
			)
			VALUES
			(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
			    @DrGl,    -- AccountId - int
			    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
			    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    @DiscontPrice, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
			    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

			)


		END


		
		--- Credit

		DECLARE	@CrGl INT 
		SELECT @CrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfirmationFull') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')

		
		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)


		-- Inventory Valuation

		DECLARE @VoucherCode2 NVARCHAR(MAX)
		

		DECLARE @yearText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
		DECLARE @monthText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
		DECLARE @dateText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
		SELECT @VoucherCode2 = (@yearText2+@monthText2+@dateText2)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
		WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
		
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='DVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='DVC-'+@VoucherCode2
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='CVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='CVC-'+@VoucherCode2
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='FUT-'+@VoucherCode2
			END

			IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='JVP-'+@VoucherCode2
			END

		DECLARE	@StockValue DECIMAL(18,2)

		SELECT @StockValue = SUM(ISNULL(INVD1.DeliveryTotalPrice,0)) + SUM(ISNULL(INVD1.DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount,0)) FROM tblInvoice AS INV
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD1 ON INVD1.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD1.DCStoreId
		INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS1 ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS1.ReceiveId
		INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.InvoiceDate

		-- Master Information Posting

		INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
		(
		    VoucherCode,
		    CompanyInfoId,
		    FinancialYearId,
		    PostingDate,
		    VoucherCategoryID,
		    CurrencyId,
		    CurrencyCovRate,
		    Narration,
		    TotalAmount,
		    TotalAmountBDT,
		    EntryBy,
		    EntryDate,
		    VoucherType, 
		    JournalType,
		    ProformaId,
			IsCostCenter,
			IsInvoice,
			isManualVCRNo,
			IsExportInvoice,
			IsImportInvoice

		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherCode2,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
		    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
		    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
		    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
		    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
		    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
		    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Delivery Confirmation Full, Inventory Valuation - ( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Invoice Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @StockValue) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
		    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @StockValue,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
		    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
		    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
		    'Adjustable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
		    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
			0,0,0,0,0
		)


		SET @VoucherMasterId2 = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		DECLARE	@DrGlInv1 INT 
		SELECT @DrGlInv1 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfFullInventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

		-- Debit 2

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId2,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGlInv1,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @StockValue, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

		)

		--- Credit

		DECLARE	@DrGlInv2 INT 
		SELECT @DrGlInv2 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfFullInventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
		
		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherMasterId2,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGlInv2,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    @StockValue  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)


		DECLARE @Count INT = 0 

		SELECT @Count = COUNT(ProformaId) FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE ProformaId = @ProformaInvId

		IF (@Count > 0)
			BEGIN
			   
			   UPDATE tblInvoice SET IsPosting = 1 WHERE InvoiceId = @ProformaInvId

			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN

			UPDATE tblInvoice SET IsPosting = 0 WHERE InvoiceId = @ProformaInvId

			END
		
		
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @ProformaInvId,@SalesDate,@CompanyId,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo,@InvDate
		
		END
		CLOSE @MyCursor
		DEALLOCATE @MyCursor

	END

	

	
END
```

## sp_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, tblCentralStore, tblCompanyUnit, tblDCStore, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster; UPDATE → tblInvoice

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Same shape as `sp_DeliveryConfirmationFullPosting` but for partially-delivered invoices; posts three vouchers ('Delivery Sales Confirmation Partial', its inventory valuation, and a 'Return Stock Valuation' for the un-delivered remainder) into `CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster/Detail`.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Referenced only from commented-out code — Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/InvoiceDAL.cs `PostDeliveryConfPartialJournal(int invoiceId)`. Effectively UNUSED in the active application.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeliveryConfirmationPartiallPosting] 

	@InvoiceId INT
	
AS
BEGIN
	

	DECLARE @TempCompanyId INT = 0
	
	SELECT @TempCompanyId = CompanyId FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS CU ON INV.ComUnitId = CU.ComUnitId
	WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId


	IF(@TempCompanyId = 16)
	BEGIN


		DECLARE @ProformaInvId INT
		DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME
		DECLARE @SalesDate DATETIME
		DECLARE @CompanyId INT
		DECLARE @CustomerMasterId INT
		DECLARE @CostPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
		DECLARE @DiscontPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
		DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(500)
		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT 
		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId1 INT 
		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId3 INT       
		--------------------------------------------------------
		DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
		SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
		FOR
		---------------
		
		SELECT INV.InvoiceId,INV.UpdateDate,UNT.CompanyId,SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice,0))-SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryDiscountAmount,0) + ISNULL(INVD.DelivarySpecialAmount,0)) AS TotalPrice,
		SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryDiscountAmount,0) + ISNULL(INVD.DelivarySpecialAmount,0)) AS Discount,INV.CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo,INV.InvoiceDate FROM tblInvoice AS INV
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INVD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD.DCStoreId
		INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
		INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.UpdateDate,CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo,DeliveryTpDiscount,INV.InvoiceDate

			
		----------
		OPEN @MyCursor
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @ProformaInvId,@SalesDate,@CompanyId,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo,@InvDate
		
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
		BEGIN
		

		-- Master Variable Info
		-------------------------------------

		DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(MAX) ='Journal'
		DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

		DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@SalesDate)), 3, 3)   
		DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@SalesDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@SalesDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@SalesDate)) END)
		DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@SalesDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@SalesDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@SalesDate)) END)
		SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText+@monthText+@dateText)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
		WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
		
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='DVB-'+@VoucherCode	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='DVC-'+@VoucherCode
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='CVB-'+@VoucherCode	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='CVC-'+@VoucherCode
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='FUT-'+@VoucherCode
			END

			IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='JVP-'+@VoucherCode
			END

		DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''

		SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
		WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND @InvDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

		----------------------------------

		-- Master Information Posting

		INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
		(
		    VoucherCode,
		    CompanyInfoId,
		    FinancialYearId,
		    PostingDate,
		    VoucherCategoryID,
		    CurrencyId,
		    CurrencyCovRate,
		    Narration,
		    TotalAmount,
		    TotalAmountBDT,
		    EntryBy,
		    EntryDate,
		    VoucherType, 
		    JournalType,
		    ProformaId,
			IsCostCenter,
			IsInvoice,
			isManualVCRNo,
			IsExportInvoice,
			IsImportInvoice

		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
		    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
		    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
		    @SalesDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
		    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
		    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
		    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Delivery Sales Confirmation Partial -( Invoice No: ' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Invoice Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Gross Sales: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @CostPrice ) + ', Discount: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @DiscontPrice) +  ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
		    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
		    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
		    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
		    'Receivable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
		    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
			0,0,0,0,0
		)


		-- Detail Information Posting

		SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		DECLARE	@DrGl1 INT 
		SELECT @DrGl1 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfirmationPartial') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr1')

		-- Debit 1 (Customer GL)

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    ISNULL(@DrGl1,0),    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)


		DECLARE	@DrGl INT 
		SELECT @DrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfirmationPartial') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr2')

		-- Debit 2

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGl,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @DiscontPrice, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

		)

		--- Credit

		DECLARE	@CrGl INT 
		SELECT @CrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfirmationPartial') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
		
		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)


		-- Inventory Valuation

		DECLARE @VoucherCode2 NVARCHAR(MAX)
		
		DECLARE @yearText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
		DECLARE @monthText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
		DECLARE @dateText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
		SELECT @VoucherCode2 = (@yearText2+@monthText2+@dateText2)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
		WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
		
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='DVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='DVC-'+@VoucherCode2
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='CVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='CVC-'+@VoucherCode2
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='FUT-'+@VoucherCode2
			END

			IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode2='JVP-'+@VoucherCode2
			END


		DECLARE	@StockValue DECIMAL(18,2)

		SELECT @StockValue = SUM(INVD1.DeliveryTotalPrice) + SUM(ISNULL(INVD1.DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount,0)) FROM tblInvoice AS INV
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD1 ON INVD1.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD1.DCStoreId
		INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS1 ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS1.ReceiveId
		INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.InvoiceDate

		-- Master Information Posting

		INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
		(
		    VoucherCode,
		    CompanyInfoId,
		    FinancialYearId,
		    PostingDate,
		    VoucherCategoryID,
		    CurrencyId,
		    CurrencyCovRate,
		    Narration,
		    TotalAmount,
		    TotalAmountBDT,
		    EntryBy,
		    EntryDate,
		    VoucherType, 
		    JournalType,
		    ProformaId,
			IsCostCenter,
			IsInvoice,
			isManualVCRNo,
			IsExportInvoice,
			IsImportInvoice

		)
		VALUES
		(   
		   @VoucherCode2,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
		    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
```
