```sql
		    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
		    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
		    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
		    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
		    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Delivery Confirmation Partial, Inventory Valuation - ( Invoice No: ' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Invoice Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @StockValue) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
		    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @StockValue,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
		    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
		    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
		    'Adjustable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
		    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
			0,0,0,0,0
		)


		SET @VoucherMasterId1 = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		DECLARE	@DrGlInv1 INT 
		SELECT @DrGlInv1 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfPartialnventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

		-- Debit 2

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId1,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGlInv1,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @StockValue, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

		)

		--- Credit

		DECLARE	@DrGlInv2 INT 
		SELECT @DrGlInv2 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfPartialnventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
		
		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherMasterId1,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGlInv2,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    @StockValue  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)





		-- Freeze Stock 

		DECLARE @VoucherCode3 NVARCHAR(MAX)
		
		DECLARE @yearText3 NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
		DECLARE @monthText3 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
		DECLARE @dateText3 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
		SELECT @VoucherCode3 = (@yearText3+@monthText3+@dateText3)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
		WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
		
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode3='DVB-'+@VoucherCode3	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode3='DVC-'+@VoucherCode3
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode3='CVB-'+@VoucherCode3	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode3='CVC-'+@VoucherCode3
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode3='FUT-'+@VoucherCode3
			END

			IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode3='JVP-'+@VoucherCode3
			END


		DECLARE	@ReturnStockValue DECIMAL(18,2)

		SELECT @ReturnStockValue = SUM(INVD2.TotalPrice) - SUM(INVD2.DeliveryTotalPrice) FROM tblInvoice AS INV2
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD2 ON INVD2.InvoiceId = INV2.InvoiceId
		WHERE INV2.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
		GROUP BY INV2.InvoiceId

		--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice AS IV
		--LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS IVD ON IV.InvoiceId = IVD.InvoiceId
		--WHERE IV.InvoiceId = 319613

		--print (@ReturnStockValue)

		-- Master Information Posting

		INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
		(
		    VoucherCode,
		    CompanyInfoId,
		    FinancialYearId,
		    PostingDate,
		    VoucherCategoryID,
		    CurrencyId,
		    CurrencyCovRate,
		    Narration,
		    TotalAmount,
		    TotalAmountBDT,
		    EntryBy,
		    EntryDate,
		    VoucherType, 
		    JournalType,
		    ProformaId,
			IsCostCenter,
			IsInvoice,
			isManualVCRNo,
			IsExportInvoice,
			IsImportInvoice

		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherCode3,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
		    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
		    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
		    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
		    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
		    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
		    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Delivery Confirmation Partial , Return Stock Valuation - ( Invoice No: ' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Invoice Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @ReturnStockValue) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
		    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @ReturnStockValue,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
		    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
		    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
		    'Adjustable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
		    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
			0,0,0,0,0
		)


		SET @VoucherMasterId3 = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		DECLARE	@DrGlInv3 INT 
		SELECT @DrGlInv3 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfPartialFreeze') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

		-- Debit 3

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId3,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGlInv3,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @ReturnStockValue, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

		)

		--- Credit

		DECLARE	@DrGlInv4 INT 
		SELECT  @DrGlInv4 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliveryConfPartialFreeze') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
		
		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherMasterId3,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGlInv4,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    @ReturnStockValue  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)

		DECLARE @Count INT = 0 

		SELECT @Count = COUNT(ProformaId) FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE ProformaId = @ProformaInvId

		IF (@Count > 0)
			BEGIN
			   
			   UPDATE tblInvoice SET IsPosting = 1 WHERE InvoiceId = @ProformaInvId

			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN

			UPDATE tblInvoice SET IsPosting = 0 WHERE InvoiceId = @ProformaInvId

			END
		
		
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @ProformaInvId,@SalesDate,@CompanyId,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo,@InvDate
		
		END
		CLOSE @MyCursor
		DEALLOCATE @MyCursor


	END

	

	
END
```

## sp_DeliveryConformationFull

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(250)`
- `@UpdateBy NVARCHAR(250)`
- `@UpdateDate DATETIME`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** MY_data, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@InvoiceNo`/`@UpdateBy`/`@UpdateDate`, and only if the invoice hasn't already been delivery-confirmed: copies every invoice-detail line's ordered quantity/price/discount into the matching `Delivery*` columns as a full (100%) delivery, marks each line `DeliveryStatus = 'Full'`, then stamps the invoice header with the delivery totals, `DeliveryInvoiceStatus = 'Full'`, and a generated `DEL-<InvoiceNo>` delivery invoice number.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/InvoiceDAL.cs — `SaveFullInvoice(...)` and `SaveFullProformaInvoice(...)`; Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/MaterialConversionReceiveDal.cs — all via `aCommonInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_DeliveryConformationFull", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeliveryConformationFull] ---- sp_DeliveryConformationFull 'INV-BD32-00001394','Test','29-Oct-2018'
	@InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(250),
	@UpdateBy NVARCHAR(250),
	@UpdateDate DATETIME 
AS
BEGIN
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceNo=@InvoiceNo AND DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NULL)
	BEGIN

	   DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	   DECLARE @TpTotal decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TpDiscount decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TpVat decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TpGrandTotal decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TotalSpecialAmount decimal(18,2)

	    SELECT @InvoiceId=InvoiceId ,
               @TpTotal=TpTotal ,
               @TpDiscount=TpDiscount ,
               @TpVat=TpVat ,
               @TpGrandTotal=TpGrandTotal ,
			   @TotalSpecialAmount=TotalSpecialAmount
                FROM dbo.tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceNo=@InvoiceNo

	   DECLARE @InvoiceDetailId INT
       DECLARE @Quantity decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @BonusQuantity decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @TotalQuantity decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @TotalPrice decimal(18,2)
       DECLARE @TotalPriceVatAmount decimal(18,2)
       DECLARE @DiscountPercentage decimal(18,2)
       DECLARE @DiscountAmount decimal(18,2)
       DECLARE @NetAmount decimal(18,2)
       DECLARE @DCStoreId INT
	   DECLARE @SpecialAmount decimal(18,2)

       DECLARE MY_data CURSOR FOR

				SELECT D.InvoiceDetailId ,
					   D.Quantity ,
					   D.BonusQuantity ,
					   D.TotalQuantity ,
					   D.TotalPrice ,
					   D.TotalPriceVatAmount ,
					   D.DiscountPercentage ,
					   D.DiscountAmount ,
					   D.NetAmount ,
					   D.DCStoreId ,
					   D.SpecialAmount 
						FROM dbo.tblInvoiceDetail D INNER JOIN
				dbo.tblInvoice I ON I.InvoiceId = D.InvoiceId WHERE D.InvoiceId=@InvoiceId

			    OPEN MY_data
				FETCH NEXT FROM MY_data INTO @InvoiceDetailId ,
				   @Quantity ,
				   @BonusQuantity ,
				   @TotalQuantity ,
				   @TotalPrice ,
				   @TotalPriceVatAmount ,
				   @DiscountPercentage ,
				   @DiscountAmount ,
				   @NetAmount ,
				   @DCStoreId ,
				   @SpecialAmount
					WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
					BEGIN

				UPDATE tblInvoiceDetail SET DeliveryQuantity=@Quantity ,
				   DeliveryBonusQuantity=@BonusQuantity ,
				   DeliveryTotalQuantity=@TotalQuantity ,
				   DeliveryTotalPrice=@TotalPrice ,
				   DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount=@TotalPriceVatAmount ,
				   DeliveryDiscountPercentage=@DiscountPercentage ,
				   DeliveryDiscountAmount=@DiscountAmount ,
				   DeliveryNetAmount=@NetAmount ,
				   DeliveryStatus ='Full', 
				   DelivarySpecialAmount=@SpecialAmount WHERE InvoiceDetailId=@InvoiceDetailId

					FETCH NEXT FROM MY_data INTO @InvoiceDetailId ,
				   @Quantity ,
				   @BonusQuantity ,
				   @TotalQuantity ,
				   @TotalPrice ,
				   @TotalPriceVatAmount ,
				   @DiscountPercentage ,
				   @DiscountAmount ,
				   @NetAmount ,
				   @DCStoreId ,
				   @SpecialAmount
					END
				CLOSE MY_data
			DEALLOCATE MY_data

			UPDATE tblInvoice SET DeliveryTpTotal=@TpTotal,DeliveryTpDiscount=@TpDiscount,DeliveryTpVat=@TpVat,
			DeliveryTpGrandTotal=@TpGrandTotal,DeliveryInvoiceStatus='Full',
			DelivaryInvoiceNo='DEL-'+@InvoiceNo,DelivarySpecialAmount=@TotalSpecialAmount,
			UpdateBy=@UpdateBy,UpdateDate=CONVERT(NVARCHAR(11),@UpdateDate,106) WHERE InvoiceId=@InvoiceId
	END
    


END
```

## sp_DeliveryConformationReject

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(250)`
- `@UpdateBy NVARCHAR(250)`
- `@ReturnReason NVARCHAR(250)`
- `@UpdateDate DATETIME`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** MY_data, dbo.tblDCStore, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail, tblDCStoreFreeze
**Tables written:** INSERT → dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze; UPDATE → tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@InvoiceNo`/`@UpdateBy`/`@ReturnReason`/`@UpdateDate`, and only if not already delivery-confirmed: zeroes out every invoice-detail line's `Delivery*` columns, marks each `DeliveryStatus = 'Reject'` with the return reason, and — if no freeze record exists yet for that detail line — inserts one into `tblDCStoreFreeze` (copying storage/product/batch metadata from `tblDCStore`) to hold the rejected stock as "frozen" pending disposition; finally stamps the invoice header as rejected with zeroed totals and a generated `DEL-<InvoiceNo>` number.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/InvoiceDAL.cs — via `aCommonInternalDal.RunStoreProcedure("sp_DeliveryConformationReject", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeliveryConformationReject] ---- sp_DeliveryConformationFull 'INV-BD32-00001394','Test','29-Oct-2018'
	@InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(250),
	@UpdateBy NVARCHAR(250),
	@ReturnReason NVARCHAR(250),
	@UpdateDate DATETIME 
AS
BEGIN
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceNo=@InvoiceNo AND DeliveryInvoiceStatus IS NULL)
	BEGIN

	   DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	   DECLARE @TpTotal decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TpDiscount decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TpVat decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TpGrandTotal decimal(18,2)
	   DECLARE @TotalSpecialAmount decimal(18,2)

	    SELECT @InvoiceId=InvoiceId ,
               @TpTotal=TpTotal ,
               @TpDiscount=TpDiscount ,
               @TpVat=TpVat ,
               @TpGrandTotal=TpGrandTotal ,
			   @TotalSpecialAmount=TotalSpecialAmount
                FROM dbo.tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceNo=@InvoiceNo

	   DECLARE @InvoiceDetailId INT
       DECLARE @Quantity decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @BonusQuantity decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @TotalQuantity decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @TotalPrice decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @TotalPriceVatAmount decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @DiscountPercentage decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @DiscountAmount decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @NetAmount decimal(18,0)
       DECLARE @DCStoreId INT
	   DECLARE @SpecialAmount decimal(18,0)

       DECLARE MY_data CURSOR FOR

				SELECT D.InvoiceDetailId ,
					   D.Quantity ,
					   D.BonusQuantity ,
					   D.TotalQuantity ,
					   D.TotalPrice ,
					   D.TotalPriceVatAmount ,
					   D.DiscountPercentage ,
					   D.DiscountAmount ,
					   D.NetAmount ,
					   D.DCStoreId ,
					   D.SpecialAmount 
						FROM dbo.tblInvoiceDetail D INNER JOIN
				dbo.tblInvoice I ON I.InvoiceId = D.InvoiceId WHERE D.InvoiceId=@InvoiceId

			    OPEN MY_data
				FETCH NEXT FROM MY_data INTO @InvoiceDetailId ,
				   @Quantity ,
				   @BonusQuantity ,
				   @TotalQuantity ,
				   @TotalPrice ,
				   @TotalPriceVatAmount ,
				   @DiscountPercentage ,
				   @DiscountAmount ,
				   @NetAmount ,
				   @DCStoreId ,
				   @SpecialAmount
					WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
					BEGIN

				UPDATE tblInvoiceDetail SET DeliveryQuantity=0 ,
				   DeliveryBonusQuantity=0 ,
				   DeliveryTotalQuantity=0 ,
				   DeliveryTotalPrice=0 ,
				   DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount=0 ,
				   DeliveryDiscountPercentage=0 ,
				   DeliveryDiscountAmount=0 ,
				   DeliveryNetAmount=0 ,
				   DeliveryStatus ='Reject', 
				   DelivarySpecialAmount=0, 
				   ReturnReason=@ReturnReason 
				   WHERE InvoiceDetailId=@InvoiceDetailId


				   declare @CountDetail int
				   select @CountDetail=COUNT(*) from tblDCStoreFreeze where InvoiceDetailId=@InvoiceDetailId

				   if(@CountDetail=0)
				   begin
				   -----------------
				     declare @DcStoreFreezeId int=null
					 declare @StorageLocation nvarchar(max)=null
					 declare @ProductCode nvarchar(max) =null
					 declare @ProductName nvarchar(max) =null
					 declare @PackSize nvarchar(max)=null
					 declare @BatchNo nvarchar(max) =null
					 declare @ExpDate datetime=null
					 declare @ReceiveDate datetime=null
					 declare @ChalanNo nvarchar(max)=null
					 declare @ChalanDate datetime=null
					 declare @ComUnitId int=null
					 declare @StockQty decimal(18,0)=@TotalQuantity
					 declare @DamageQty decimal(18,0)=0
					 declare @StockRcvDate datetime=null
					 declare @ReqId int=null
					 declare @ReqChildId int=null
					 declare @StockInTransfarId int=null
					 declare @StockCondition int=null
					 declare @ChalanDetailsId int=null

				   SELECT @DcStoreFreezeId=(isnull(MAX(DCStoreFreezeId),0)+1)  FROM tblDCStoreFreeze

				   SELECT @StorageLocation=StorageLocation,@ProductCode=ProductCode,@ProductName=ProductName,@PackSize=PackSize,@BatchNo=BatchNo
				   ,@ExpDate=ExpDate,@ReceiveDate=ReceiveDate,@ChalanNo=ChalanNo,@ChalanDate=ChalanDate,@ComUnitId=ComUnitId,@StockRcvDate=StockRcvDate
				   ,@ReqId=ReqId,@ReqChildId=ReqChildId,@StockInTransfarId=StockInTransfarId,@ChalanDetailsId=ChalanDetailsId FROM dbo.tblDCStore WHERE DCStoreId=@DCStoreId

				   INSERT INTO dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze 
         ( DCStoreId , 
             DCStoreFreezeId, 
             InvoiceDetailId, 
             StorageLocation , 
             ProductCode , 
             ProductName , 
             PackSize , 
             BatchNo , 
             TotalQuantity , 
             ExpDate , 
             ReceiveDate , 
             ChalanNo , 
             ChalanDate , 
             ComUnitId , 
             StockQty , 
             DamageQty , 
             StockRcvDate , 
             ReqId ,
             ReqChildId , 
             StockInTransfarId, 
             StockCondition,ChalanDetailsId 
          ) 
		  values (@DCStoreId , 
             @DCStoreFreezeId, 
             @InvoiceDetailId, 
             @StorageLocation , 
             @ProductCode , 
             @ProductName , 
             @PackSize , 
             @BatchNo , 
             @TotalQuantity , 
             @ExpDate , 
             @ReceiveDate , 
             @ChalanNo , 
             @ChalanDate , 
             @ComUnitId , 
             @StockQty , 
             @DamageQty , 
             @StockRcvDate , 
             @ReqId ,
             @ReqChildId , 
             @StockInTransfarId, 
             @StockCondition,@ChalanDetailsId )
        

				   ----------------

		end


					FETCH NEXT FROM MY_data INTO @InvoiceDetailId ,
				   @Quantity ,
				   @BonusQuantity ,
				   @TotalQuantity ,
				   @TotalPrice ,
				   @TotalPriceVatAmount ,
				   @DiscountPercentage ,
				   @DiscountAmount ,
				   @NetAmount ,
				   @DCStoreId ,
				   @SpecialAmount
					END
				CLOSE MY_data
			DEALLOCATE MY_data

			UPDATE tblInvoice SET DeliveryTpTotal=0,DeliveryTpDiscount=0,DeliveryTpVat=0,
			DeliveryTpGrandTotal=0,DeliveryInvoiceStatus='Reject',
			DelivaryInvoiceNo='DEL-'+@InvoiceNo,
			DelivarySpecialAmount=0,
			UpdateBy=@UpdateBy,UpdateDate=CONVERT(NVARCHAR(11),@UpdateDate,106)
			WHERE InvoiceId=@InvoiceId
	END
    


END
```

## sp_DeliveryInvoiceDeletePosting

**Parameters:**
- `@InvoiceId NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMaping, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, tblCentralStore, tblCompanyUnit, tblDCStore, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail, tblStockInTransfar
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster; UPDATE → dbo.tblCustomerReturnMaster, tblCustomerReturnMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@InvoiceId` (actually matched against `tblInvoice.DelivaryInvoiceNo`, despite the INT-typed parameter name/usage suggesting otherwise — it's declared NVARCHAR), posts a 'Delivery Invoice Delete' reversal voucher (credit customer GL / credit+debit sales-return-mapped GLs) plus a separate 'Delivery Invoice Delete, Inventory Valuation' voucher reversing the stock value, into `CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster/Detail` — the self-contained counterpart to `sp_ADJ_DeliveryInvoiceDeletePosting`.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/ProformaOrInvoiceReturn.cs — `InvoiceDeletePosting(string invoiceNo)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_DeliveryInvoiceDeletePosting", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeliveryInvoiceDeletePosting] 

	@InvoiceId NVARCHAR(MAX)
	
AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @ProformaInvId INT
	DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME
	DECLARE @CompanyId INT
	DECLARE @CustomerMasterId INT
	DECLARE @CostPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @DiscontPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(500)

	
	DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT 
	DECLARE @VoucherMasterId1 INT 
	        
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	---------------


	SELECT INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId  ,INV.UpdateDate,SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice,0)) AS TotalPrice,
	SUM((INVD.DelivarySpecialAmount / INVD.Quantity) * INVD.DeliveryQuantity) AS Discount,INV.CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INVD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	--INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD.DCStoreId
	--INNER JOIN tblStockInTransfar AS TNS ON TNS.StockInTransfarId = DCS.StockInTransfarId
	--INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON TNS.ReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
    INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
	WHERE INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo = @InvoiceId
	GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.UpdateDate,CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo
	
	----------
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @ProformaInvId,@CompanyId,@InvDate,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo
	
	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN
	

	-- Master Variable Info
    -------------------------------------

	DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(MAX) ='Journal'
	DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

	DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
	DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
	DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
    SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText+@monthText+@dateText)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
	WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
	
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='DVB-'+@VoucherCode	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='DVC-'+@VoucherCode
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='CVB-'+@VoucherCode	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='CVC-'+@VoucherCode
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='FUT-'+@VoucherCode
		END

		IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='JVP-'+@VoucherCode
		END

	DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''

	SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND @InvDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

	----------------------------------

	-- Master Information Posting

	INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
	(
	    VoucherCode,
	    CompanyInfoId,
	    FinancialYearId,
	    PostingDate,
	    VoucherCategoryID,
	    CurrencyId,
	    CurrencyCovRate,
	    Narration,
	    TotalAmount,
	    TotalAmountBDT,
	    EntryBy,
	    EntryDate,
	    VoucherType, 
	    JournalType,
	    SalesReturnId,
		IsCostCenter,
		IsInvoice,
		isManualVCRNo,
		IsExportInvoice,
		IsImportInvoice

	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
	    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
	    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
	   CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), GetDate(), 101), -- PostingDate - datetime
	    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
	    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
	    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Delivery Invoice Delete -( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Delete Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), GetDate(), 101) + ', Return Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
	    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
	    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
	    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
	    'Receivable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
	    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
		0,0,0,0,0
	)


	-- Detail Information Posting

	SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

	DECLARE	@CrGl1 INT 
	SELECT @CrGl1 = AccountId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts WHERE CustomerId IS NOT NULL AND CustomerId = @CustomerMasterId

	-- Credit 1 (Customer GL)

	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @CrGl1,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    @CostPrice -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	)


	DECLARE	@CrGl INT 
	SELECT @CrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturn') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr2')

	-- Credit 2

	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    @DiscontPrice -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

	)

	--- Debit

	DECLARE	@DrGl INT 
	SELECT @DrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturn') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')
	
	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	)


    -- Inventory Valuation

	DECLARE @VoucherCode2 NVARCHAR(MAX)
	

	DECLARE @yearText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
	DECLARE @monthText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
	DECLARE @dateText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
    SELECT @VoucherCode2 = (@yearText2+@monthText2+@dateText2)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
	WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
	
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='DVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='DVC-'+@VoucherCode2
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='CVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='CVC-'+@VoucherCode2
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='FUT-'+@VoucherCode2
		END

		IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='JVP-'+@VoucherCode2
		END


	DECLARE	@StockValue DECIMAL(18,2)


	SELECT @StockValue = SUM(ISNULL(INVD1.DeliveryTotalPrice,0)) + SUM(ISNULL(INVD1.DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount,0)) FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD1 ON INVD1.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD1.DCStoreId
	INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS1 ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS1.ReceiveId
	INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
	WHERE INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo = @InvoiceId
	GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.InvoiceDate


	

	-- Master Information Posting

	INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
	(
	    VoucherCode,
	    CompanyInfoId,
	    FinancialYearId,
	    PostingDate,
	    VoucherCategoryID,
	    CurrencyId,
	    CurrencyCovRate,
	    Narration,
	    TotalAmount,
	    TotalAmountBDT,
	    EntryBy,
	    EntryDate,
	    VoucherType, 
	    JournalType,
	    SalesReturnId,
		IsCostCenter,
		IsInvoice,
		isManualVCRNo,
		IsExportInvoice,
		IsImportInvoice

	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherCode2,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
	    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
	    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
	    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
	    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
	    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
	    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Delivery Invoice Delete, Inventory Valuation - ( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Delete Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), GETDATE(), 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @StockValue) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
	    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @StockValue,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
	    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
	    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
	    'Adjustable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
	    @ProformaInvId,      -- ProformaId -int
		0,0,0,0,0
	)


	SET @VoucherMasterId1 = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

	DECLARE	@DrGlInv1 INT 
	SELECT @DrGlInv1 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMaping WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturnInventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

	-- Debit 2

	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   @VoucherMasterId1,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @DrGlInv1,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @StockValue, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

	)

	--- Credit

	DECLARE	@DrGlInv2 INT 
	SELECT @DrGlInv2 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturnInventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
	
	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherMasterId1,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @DrGlInv2,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    @StockValue  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	)


	--DECLARE @Count INT = 0 

	--SELECT @Count = COUNT(SalesReturnId) FROM ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE SalesReturnId = @ReturnId

	--IF (@Count > 0)

	--	BEGIN
		   
	--	   UPDATE dbo.tblCustomerReturnMaster SET IsPosting = 1 WHERE CustReturnId = @ReturnId

	--	END

	--ELSE

	--	BEGIN

	--	UPDATE tblCustomerReturnMaster SET IsPosting = 0 WHERE CustReturnId = @ReturnId

	--	END
	
	
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @ProformaInvId,@CompanyId,@InvDate,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo
	
	END
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor

	
END
```

## sp_DeliverySalesReturnPosting

**Parameters:**
- `@ReturnId INT`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, dbo.tblCustomerReturnDetail, dbo.tblInvoice, tblCentralStore, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustomerReturnMaster, tblDCStore, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail; UPDATE → dbo.tblCustomerReturnMaster, tblCustomerReturnMaster

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@ReturnId`, posts a 'Delivery Sales Return' voucher (credit customer GL, credit/debit sales-return-mapped GLs) sized from the returned quantity's value (joining `tblCustomerReturnMaster`/`tblCustomerReturnDetail` back to the original invoice detail and central-store cost) plus a separate inventory-valuation voucher, into `CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster/Detail` — the self-contained counterpart to `sp_ADJ_DeliverySalesReturnPosting` (note: one detail INSERT targets `ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail` instead of `CSTL_ERP_ACCDB`, an apparent inconsistency/bug in the body versus the rest of the procedure).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_DeliverySalesReturnPosting] 

	@ReturnId INT
	
AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @ProformaInvId INT
	DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME
	DECLARE @CompanyId INT
	DECLARE @CustomerMasterId INT
	DECLARE @CostPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @DiscontPrice DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(500)
	
	
	
	DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT 
	DECLARE @VoucherMasterId1 INT        
	--------------------------------------------------------
	DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
	SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
	FOR
	---------------
	
	SELECT INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,RTNM.ApprovedDate,SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice,0)) AS TotalPrice,
	SUM((INVD.DelivarySpecialAmount / INVD.Quantity) * INVD.DeliveryQuantity) AS Discount,INV.CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo FROM tblCustomerReturnMaster AS RTNM 
	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCustomerReturnDetail AS RTND ON RTND.CustReturnId = RTNM.CustReturnId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INVD.InvoiceDetailId = RTND.InvoiceDetailId
	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice AS INV ON INVD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	LEFT JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD.DCStoreId
	INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
	WHERE RTNM.CustReturnId = @ReturnId
	GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,RTNM.ApprovedDate,INV.CustomerMasterId,INV.InvoiceNo

		
	----------
	OPEN @MyCursor
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @ProformaInvId,@CompanyId,@InvDate,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo
	
	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
	BEGIN
	

	-- Master Variable Info
    -------------------------------------

	DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(MAX) ='Journal'
	DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

	DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
	DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
	DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
    SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText+@monthText+@dateText)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
	WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
	
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='DVB-'+@VoucherCode	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='DVC-'+@VoucherCode
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='CVB-'+@VoucherCode	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='CVC-'+@VoucherCode
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='FUT-'+@VoucherCode
		END

		IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode='JVP-'+@VoucherCode
		END

	DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''

	SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND @InvDate BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

	----------------------------------

	-- Master Information Posting

	INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
	(
	    VoucherCode,
	    CompanyInfoId,
	    FinancialYearId,
	    PostingDate,
	    VoucherCategoryID,
	    CurrencyId,
	    CurrencyCovRate,
	    Narration,
	    TotalAmount,
	    TotalAmountBDT,
	    EntryBy,
	    EntryDate,
	    VoucherType, 
	    JournalType,
	    SalesReturnId,
		IsCostCenter,
		IsInvoice,
		isManualVCRNo,
		IsExportInvoice,
		IsImportInvoice

	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
	    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
	    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
	    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
	    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
	    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
	    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Delivery Sales Return -( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Return Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Return Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
	    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
	    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
	    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
	    'Receivable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
	    @ReturnId,      -- ProformaId -int
		0,0,0,0,0
	)


	-- Detail Information Posting

	SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

	DECLARE	@CrGl1 INT 
	SELECT @CrGl1 = AccountId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblChartOfAccounts WHERE CustomerId IS NOT NULL AND CustomerId = @CustomerMasterId

	-- Credit 1 (Customer GL)

	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @CrGl1,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    @CostPrice -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	)


	DECLARE	@CrGl INT 
	SELECT @CrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturn') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr2')

	-- Credit 2

	INSERT INTO ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    @DiscontPrice -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

	)

	--- Debit

	DECLARE	@DrGl INT 
	SELECT @DrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturn') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')
	
	INSERT INTO ZAS_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @CostPrice + @DiscontPrice, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	)


    -- Inventory Valuation

	DECLARE @VoucherCode2 NVARCHAR(MAX)
	

	DECLARE @yearText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
	DECLARE @monthText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
	DECLARE @dateText2 NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
    SELECT @VoucherCode2 = (@yearText2+@monthText2+@dateText2)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
	WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
	
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='DVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='DVC-'+@VoucherCode2
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='CVB-'+@VoucherCode2	
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='CVC-'+@VoucherCode2
		END
		IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='FUT-'+@VoucherCode2
		END

		IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
		BEGIN
			SET @VoucherCode2='JVP-'+@VoucherCode2
		END

	DECLARE	@StockValue DECIMAL(18,2)


	SELECT @StockValue = SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryTotalPrice,0)) + SUM(ISNULL(INVD.DeliveryTotalPriceVatAmount,0))FROM tblCustomerReturnMaster AS RTNM 
	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCustomerReturnDetail AS RTND ON RTND.CustReturnId = RTNM.CustReturnId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INVD.InvoiceDetailId = RTND.InvoiceDetailId
	LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoice AS INV ON INVD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
	INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = INVD.DCStoreId
	INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS1 ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS1.ReceiveId
	INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
	WHERE RTNM.CustReturnId = @ReturnId
	GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,UNT.CompanyId,INV.InvoiceDate

	-- Master Information Posting

	INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
	(
	    VoucherCode,
	    CompanyInfoId,
	    FinancialYearId,
	    PostingDate,
	    VoucherCategoryID,
	    CurrencyId,
	    CurrencyCovRate,
	    Narration,
	    TotalAmount,
	    TotalAmountBDT,
	    EntryBy,
	    EntryDate,
	    VoucherType, 
	    JournalType,
	    SalesReturnId,
		IsCostCenter,
		IsInvoice,
		isManualVCRNo,
		IsExportInvoice,
		IsImportInvoice

	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherCode2,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
	    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
	    @FinYearId,         -- FinancialYearId - int
	    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
	    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
	    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
	    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Delivery Sales Return, Inventory Valuation - ( Invoice No:' + @InvoiceNo + ' , Retu Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @InvDate, 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @StockValue) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
	    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @StockValue,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
	    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
	    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
	    'Adjustable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
	    @ReturnId,      -- ProformaId -int
		0,0,0,0,0
	)


	SET @VoucherMasterId1 = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

	DECLARE	@DrGlInv1 INT 
	SELECT @DrGlInv1 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturnInventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

	-- Debit 2

	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   @VoucherMasterId1,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @DrGlInv1,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    @StockValue, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

	)

	--- Credit

	DECLARE	@DrGlInv2 INT 
	SELECT @DrGlInv2 = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('DeliverySalesReturnInventory') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
	
	INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
	(
	    VoucherMasterId,
	    AccountId,
	    DebitorCredit,
	    DebitAmount,
	    CreditAmount,
	    DebitAmountBDT,
	    CreditAmountBDT
	)
	VALUES
	(   
	    @VoucherMasterId1,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
	    @DrGlInv2,    -- AccountId - int
	    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
	    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	    @StockValue  -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
	)


	DECLARE @Count INT = 0 

	SELECT @Count = COUNT(SalesReturnId) FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE SalesReturnId = @ReturnId

	IF (@Count > 0)

		BEGIN
		   
		   UPDATE dbo.tblCustomerReturnMaster SET IsPosting = 1 WHERE CustReturnId = @ReturnId

		END

	ELSE

		BEGIN

		UPDATE tblCustomerReturnMaster SET IsPosting = 0 WHERE CustReturnId = @ReturnId

		END
	
	
	FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
	INTO @ProformaInvId,@CompanyId,@InvDate,@CostPrice,@DiscontPrice,@CustomerMasterId,@InvoiceNo
	
	END
	CLOSE @MyCursor
	DEALLOCATE @MyCursor

	
END
```

## sp_ERROR_Issues

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** PaymentBachMaster, PaymentBatchDetails, dbo.tblCustPayDetail, tblCustMaster, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblOrder, tblPaymentTranscationDetail, tblPaymentTranscationMaster
**Tables written:** UPDATE → Invoice, tblInvoice, tblOrder, tblPaymentTranscationMaster; DELETE → tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Daily data-quality check with no parameters: finds duplicate customer codes (globally), duplicate order codes and duplicate invoice numbers (for today), and duplicate payment postings (for today) — a health-check/monitoring query, with several commented-out manual fix-up statements left in the body for reference.

**Result set:** Four separate result sets: (1) duplicate CustomerCode + count, (2) duplicate OrderCode/SubmissionDate + count for today, (3) duplicate InvoiceNo + count for today, (4) duplicate payment combination + count for today.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01-04-2019,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ERROR_Issues] 

AS
BEGIN
	 

	-- Customer Duplicate Check

	SELECT CustomerCode,COUNT(CustomerMasterId) TotalCount FROM tblCustMaster GROUP BY CustomerCode HAVING COUNT(CustomerMasterId) > 1

	-- Order Duplicate Check

	SELECT OrderCode,SubmissionDate,COUNT(OrderId) TotalCount FROM tblOrder 
	WHERE SubmissionDate = CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE())
	GROUP BY OrderCode,SubmissionDate HAVING COUNT(OrderId) > 1 
	ORDER BY SubmissionDate DESC

	--SELECT * FROM tblOrder WHERE OrderCode = 'ODR-240408342'
	--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice WHERE OrderId = 477672


	--UPDATE tblOrder SET OrderCode = 'ODR-240400000' WHERE OrderId = 477672
	--UPDATE tblInvoice SET OrderNo = 'ODR-240400000' WHERE OrderId = 477672

	--SELECT TOP 50 * FROM tblOrder WHERE SubmissionDate = CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE()) ORDER BY OrderId DESC

	-- Invoice Duplicate Check

	SELECT InvoiceNo,COUNT(InvoiceId) TotalCount FROM tblInvoice
	 where InvoiceDate = CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE())  GROUP BY InvoiceNo HAVING COUNT(InvoiceId) > 1

	-- Payment Duplicate Check

	SELECT PD.InvoiceId,PD.PaymentAmount,AIT,Discount,Count(CustPayDetailId) NoOfPayment FROM dbo.tblCustPayDetail AS PD
    LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS PM ON PD.CustPayId = PM.CustPayId
    WHERE  CONVERT(DATE,PaymentDate) = CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE()) 
	GROUP BY PD.InvoiceId,PD.PaymentAmount,AIT,Discount
	HAVING Count(CustPayDetailId) > 1

	---- Payment Duplicate Remove Steps

	--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice WHERE InvoiceId = 464302 -- Find Invoice
	--SELECT * FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE InvoiceId = 464302 -- Find Payment by Invoice

	------ Delete Payment 

	--DELETE FROM tblCustPayDetail WHERE CustPayId IN (457039,457040)-- Replace by CustPayId
	--DELETE FROM tblCustomerPay WHERE CustPayId IN (457039,457040) -- Replace by CustPayId

	---- Update Invoice Payment

	--UPDATE tblInvoice SET PaymentAmount = 640.00 WHERE InvoiceId = 464302


	  
         

END


--SELECT * FROM PaymentBatchDetails AS D
--LEFT JOIN PaymentBachMaster AS M ON D.BatchMasterId = M.BatchMasterId
--WHERE TranscationDetailId = 422530

--SELECT * FROM PaymentBachMaster  AS M
--LEFT JOIN PaymentBatchDetails AS D ON M.BatchMasterId = D.BatchMasterId
--WHERE BatchNo = 'PB-240414591'

--UPDATE tblPaymentTranscationMaster SET IsReject = 0 WHERE CustPayId = 422547 

--SELECT * FROM tblPaymentTranscationDetail AS TD
--LEFT JOIN tblPaymentTranscationMaster AS TM ON TD.CustPayId = TM.CustPayId
--WHERE CustPayDetailId = 422530

--LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS M ON D.CustPayId = M.CustPayId
```
