## sp_Employee_EmployeeCompanySyncedWithDistibution

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @EmployeeMasterCode NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblEmpGeneralInfo

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@EmployeeMasterCode`, looks up that employee's unit id from the cross-database `ZASHRIS` HR system and updates the local `tblEmpGeneralInfo.CompanyId` to match — one-way company-id sync from HR into the distribution app (cursor-driven though the source query returns at most one row).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/HREmployeeDataSyncDal.cs — `SyncCompanyEmployeById(string empMasterCode)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_Employee_EmployeeCompanySyncedWithDistibution", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Employee_EmployeeCompanySyncedWithDistibution]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@EmployeeMasterCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

AS
BEGIN
   
DECLARE @CompanyId INT

            
--------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT UnitId FROM ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo WHERE EmpMasterCode = @EmployeeMasterCode

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @CompanyId
           
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
		
		UPDATE tblEmpGeneralInfo SET CompanyId = @CompanyId WHERE EmpMasterCode = @EmployeeMasterCode
		

    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
    INTO @CompanyId
           
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
    
END
```

## sp_Employee_EmployeeListFromHR

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @Parameter NVARCHAR(MAX)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblCompanyUnit, ZASHRIS..tblDepartment, ZASHRIS..tblDesignation, ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblEmpGeneralInfo
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH; executes dynamic SQL (EXEC/sp_executesql).

**Business logic:** Builds and executes (`EXEC`) a dynamic comparison query listing active HR employees (`ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo`) alongside whether each is already synced into the local `tblEmpGeneralInfo` (by matching master code + cell number) and whether its company assignment differs, with a caller-supplied `@Parameter` filter spliced into the WHERE clause.

**Result set:** One dynamic result set: unit name, HR employee id/code/name/national id, designation, department, joining date, `SyncStatus` ('Synced'/'Not Synced'), and `CompanySwap` ('Yes'/'No').

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/HREmployeeDataSyncDal.cs — `GetEmployeeDataFromHr(string parameter)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.GetDataTableAction("sp_Employee_EmployeeListFromHR", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Employee_EmployeeListFromHR]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here

	@Parameter NVARCHAR(MAX)

AS
BEGIN
   
   DECLARE @Query NVARCHAR(MAX)

   SET @Query = 'SELECT DISTINCT UNT.UnitName,EGI.EmpInfoId,EGI.EmpMasterCode,EGI.EmpName,EGI.NationalIdNo,DesigName,DeptName,EGI.JoiningDate,
   CASE WHEN DEG.EmpMasterCode IS NULL OR DEG.CellNumber != EGI.PhoneNo THEN ''Not Synced'' ELSE ''Synced'' END AS SyncStatus,CASE WHEN DEMP.CompanyId != UNT.UnitId THEN ''Yes'' ELSE ''No'' END AS CompanySwap  FROM ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo AS EGI 
   LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDesignation AS DSG ON EGI.DesigId = DSG.DesigId
   LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDepartment AS DPT ON EGI.DepId = DPT.DeptId
   LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON EGI.UnitId = UNT.UnitId
   LEFT JOIN tblEmpGeneralInfo AS DEG ON EGI.EmpMasterCode = DEG.EmpMasterCode
   LEFT JOIN (SELECT EmpMasterCode,CompanyId FROM tblEmpGeneralInfo ) AS DEMP ON EGI.EmpMasterCode = DEMP.EmpMasterCode
   WHERE EGI.EmployeeStatus = ''Active''' + @Parameter + ' ORDER BY UNT.UnitName'

   EXEC(@Query)
    
END


   --SELECT DISTINCT UNT.UnitName,UNT.UnitId,DEMP.CompanyId,EGI.EmpInfoId,EGI.EmpMasterCode,EGI.EmpName,EGI.NationalIdNo,DesigName,DeptName,EGI.JoiningDate,
   --CASE WHEN DEG.EmpMasterCode IS NULL OR DEG.CellNumber != EGI.PhoneNo THEN 'Not Synced' ELSE 'Synced' END AS SyncStatus, CASE WHEN DEMP.CompanyId != UNT.UnitId THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS CompanySwap FROM ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo AS EGI 
   --LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDesignation AS DSG ON EGI.DesigId = DSG.DesigId
   --LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDepartment AS DPT ON EGI.DepId = DPT.DeptId
   --LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON EGI.UnitId = UNT.UnitId
   --LEFT JOIN tblEmpGeneralInfo AS DEG ON EGI.EmpMasterCode = DEG.EmpMasterCode
   --LEFT JOIN (SELECT EmpMasterCode,CompanyId FROM tblEmpGeneralInfo ) AS DEMP ON EGI.EmpMasterCode = DEMP.EmpMasterCode
   --WHERE EGI.EmployeeStatus = 'Active' AND DeptName IN ('Sales & Marketing','Marketing','Distribution','Accounts & Finance') 
   --AND EGI.EmpMasterCode = 'ZAS091' ORDER BY UNT.UnitName


   


--SELECT * FROM ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo AS E
--LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDepartment AS D ON E.DepId = D.DeptId
--WHERE EmpMasterCode = 'ZAS953'
```

## sp_Employee_EmployeeListSyncedWithDistibution

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @EmployeeMasterCode NVARCHAR(200)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblEmpGeneralInfo
**Tables written:** INSERT → tblEmpGeneralInfo; UPDATE → ELSE, tblEmpGeneralInfo

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@EmployeeMasterCode`, pulls the full HR profile for that employee from `ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo` and either inserts a brand-new `tblEmpGeneralInfo` row (if none exists locally yet, tagged `EntryBy = 'Auto Sync'`) or updates every field on the existing local row to match HR — a full one-way profile sync from HR into the distribution app.

**Result set:** No result set on the UPDATE path; the INSERT path additionally returns `SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY()` for the new row.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/HREmployeeDataSyncDal.cs — `SyncEmployeById(string empMasterCode)`, via `aCommonInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_Employee_EmployeeListSyncedWithDistibution", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Employee_EmployeeListSyncedWithDistibution]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@EmployeeMasterCode NVARCHAR(200)

AS
BEGIN
   
DECLARE @CompanyId INT
DECLARE @EmpMasterCode NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @EmpName NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @ShortName NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @FatherName NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @MotherName NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @Religion NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @Nationality NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @DateOfBirth DATETIME
DECLARE @PlaceOfBirth NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @BloodGroup NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @Gender NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @AddressPresent NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @AddressPermanent NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @PreCity NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @PerCity NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @EmpImage NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @SignatureImage NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @NationalIdNo NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @CellNumber NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @Email NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @EmrgContactNo NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @MaritalStatus NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @RefName NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @RefContactNo NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @DesignationId INT
DECLARE @DepartmentId INT
DECLARE @JoiningDate DATETIME
DECLARE @EntryBy NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @EntryDate DATETIME
DECLARE @UpdateBy NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @UpdateDate DATETIME
DECLARE @ShiftId INT
            
--------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------


	--DECLARE @EmployeeMasterCode NVARCHAR(200) = 'ZAS091'

	SELECT UnitId
           ,EmpMasterCode
           ,EmpName
           ,ShortName
           ,FatherName
           ,MotherName
           ,Religion
           ,Nationality
           ,DateOfBirth
           ,PlaceOfBirth
           ,BloodGroup
           ,Gender
           ,AddressPresent
           ,AddressPermanent
           ,NationalIdNo
           ,PhoneNo
           ,Email
           ,EmergencycontactNumber
           ,MaritalStatus
           ,RefName
           ,RefCellNo
           ,DesigId
           ,DepId
           ,JoiningDate
           ,EntryBy
           ,EntryDate
           ,ShiftId FROM ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(EmpMasterCode))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@EmployeeMasterCode))) 

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @CompanyId
           ,@EmpMasterCode
           ,@EmpName
           ,@ShortName
           ,@FatherName
           ,@MotherName
           ,@Religion
           ,@Nationality
           ,@DateOfBirth
           ,@PlaceOfBirth
           ,@BloodGroup
           ,@Gender
           ,@AddressPresent
           ,@AddressPermanent
           ,@NationalIdNo
           ,@CellNumber
           ,@Email
           ,@EmrgContactNo
           ,@MaritalStatus
           ,@RefName
           ,@RefContactNo
           ,@DesignationId
           ,@DepartmentId
           ,@JoiningDate
           ,@EntryBy
           ,@EntryDate
           ,@ShiftId
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN


	DECLARE @Count INT = 0

	SELECT @Count = COUNT(EmpInfoId) FROM tblEmpGeneralInfo WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(EmpMasterCode))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@EmployeeMasterCode))) 
   
   IF(@Count = 0) 
   BEGIN

	INSERT INTO tblEmpGeneralInfo
           (CompanyId
           ,EmpMasterCode
           ,EmpName
           ,ShortName
           ,FatherName
           ,MotherName
           ,Religion
           ,Nationality
           ,DateOfBirth
           ,PlaceOfBirth
           ,BloodGroup
           ,Gender
           ,AddressPresent
           ,AddressPermanent
           ,NationalIdNo
           ,CellNumber
           ,Email
           ,EmrgContactNo
           ,MaritalStatus
           ,RefName
           ,RefContactNo
           ,DesignationId
           ,DepartmentId
           ,JoiningDate
           ,EntryBy
           ,EntryDate
           ,ShiftId)
     VALUES
           (@CompanyId
           ,@EmpMasterCode
           ,@EmpName
           ,@ShortName
           ,@FatherName
           ,@MotherName
           ,@Religion
           ,@Nationality
           ,@DateOfBirth
           ,@PlaceOfBirth
           ,@BloodGroup
           ,@Gender
           ,@AddressPresent
           ,@AddressPermanent
           ,@NationalIdNo
           ,@CellNumber
           ,@Email
           ,@EmrgContactNo
           ,@MaritalStatus
           ,@RefName
           ,@RefContactNo
           ,@DesignationId
           ,@DepartmentId
           ,@JoiningDate
           ,'Auto Sync'
           ,GETDATE()
           ,@ShiftId)
		       
			SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY()

   END
		

		-- Else Update

	ELSE 

	BEGIN

		
		UPDATE tblEmpGeneralInfo
		SET CompanyId = @CompanyId,
		    EmpMasterCode = @EmpMasterCode,
		    EmpName = @EmpName,
		    ShortName = @ShortName,
		    FatherName = @FatherName,
		    MotherName = @MotherName,
		    Religion = @Religion,
		    Nationality = @Nationality,
		    DateOfBirth = @DateOfBirth,
		    PlaceOfBirth = @PlaceOfBirth,
		    BloodGroup = @BloodGroup,
		    Gender = @Gender,
		    AddressPresent = @AddressPresent,
		    AddressPermanent = @AddressPermanent,
		    NationalIdNo = @NationalIdNo,
		    CellNumber = @CellNumber,
		    Email = @Email,
		    EmrgContactNo = @EmrgContactNo,
		    MaritalStatus = @MaritalStatus,
		    RefName = @RefName,
		    RefContactNo = @RefContactNo,
		    DesignationId = @DesignationId,
		    DepartmentId = @DepartmentId,
		    JoiningDate = @JoiningDate,
		    EntryBy = 'Auto Sync',
		    EntryDate = GETDATE(),
		    ShiftId = @ShiftId
		WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(EmpMasterCode))) = LTRIM(RTRIM(UPPER(@EmployeeMasterCode))) 



	END
	

    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor 
    INTO @CompanyId
           ,@EmpMasterCode
           ,@EmpName
           ,@ShortName
           ,@FatherName
           ,@MotherName
           ,@Religion
           ,@Nationality
           ,@DateOfBirth
           ,@PlaceOfBirth
           ,@BloodGroup
           ,@Gender
           ,@AddressPresent
           ,@AddressPermanent
           ,@NationalIdNo
           ,@CellNumber
           ,@Email
           ,@EmrgContactNo
           ,@MaritalStatus
           ,@RefName
           ,@RefContactNo
           ,@DesignationId
           ,@DepartmentId
           ,@JoiningDate
           ,@EntryBy
           ,@EntryDate
           ,@ShiftId
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
    
END
```

## sp_ErrorCorrection_InvoicePayment

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblCustPayDetail, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblInvoice

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Data-fix job with no parameters: finds invoices whose stored `DiscountOnPayment` doesn't match the sum of discounts actually recorded in `tblCustPayDetail`, and overwrites the invoice's `AIT`/`DiscountOnPayment`/`PaymentAmount` with the recomputed totals.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ErrorCorrection_InvoicePayment] 


AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
	DECLARE @DiscountOnPayment DECIMAL(18,4)
    Declare @Paid DECIMAL(18,4) 
    Declare @AIT DECIMAL(18,4)
            
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	--INV.PaymentAmount, INV.DiscountOnPayment, INV.AIT

	SELECT INV.InvoiceId,PD.Paid, PD.AIT, PD.Discount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
		LEFT JOIN (SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(DeliveryNetAmount) AS Sales FROM tblInvoiceDetail AS INV GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS D ON INV.InvoiceId = D.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN (SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(PaymentAmount) AS Paid, SUM(AIT) AIT, SUM(Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS INV GROUP BY InvoiceId) AS PD ON INV.InvoiceId = pD.InvoiceId
		WHERE INV.DiscountOnPayment != PD.Discount

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId,@Paid,@AIT,@DiscountOnPayment
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
		UPDATE tblInvoice SET AIT = @AIT, DiscountOnPayment = @DiscountOnPayment, PaymentAmount = @Paid WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId,@Paid,@AIT,@DiscountOnPayment
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
END
```

## sp_Error_IsInvoiceNotUpdate

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblInvoice, tblOrder
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblOrder

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Data-fix job with no parameters: finds orders that have a linked invoice but are still flagged `IsInvoice = 0` and flips them to 1 — functionally identical to `sp_ADJ_IsInvoiceUpdate`.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Error_IsInvoiceNotUpdate] 


AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @OrderId INT
            
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	SELECT OrderId FROM tblOrder AS ODR
	WHERE ODR.OrderId IN (SELECT OrderId FROM tblInvoice WHERE OrderId IS NOT NULL) AND ODR.IsInvoice = 0

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @OrderId
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
		UPDATE tblOrder SET IsInvoice = 1 WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @OrderId
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
END
```

## sp_Error_MasterPaymentUpdate

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblCustPayDetail, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** UPDATE → tblInvoice

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Data-fix job with no parameters: for every invoice with a positive grand total, recomputes `PaymentAmount`/`AIT`/`DiscountOnPayment` from the sum of its `tblCustPayDetail` rows and overwrites the invoice header — a full resync of invoice payment totals from the detail rows (broader than `sp_ErrorCorrection_InvoicePayment`, which only touches mismatched discount rows).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Error_MasterPaymentUpdate] 

 

AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @InvoiceId INT

	DECLARE @ActualPayment  DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @AIT DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @DisCount DECIMAL (18,2)
            
    --------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
    SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
    FOR
    ---------------

	

	SELECT INV.InvoiceId ,D.ActualPayment,ISNULL(D.AIT,0) AIT, ISNULL(D.DisCount,0) DisCount FROM tblInvoice AS INV
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(PaymentAmount) ActualPayment , SUM(AIT) AIT ,SUM(Discount) AS DisCount  FROM tblCustPayDetail 
	GROUP BY InvoiceId) AS D ON INV.InvoiceId = D.InvoiceId
	WHERE ISNULL(DeliveryTpGrandTotal,0) > 0

    ----------
    OPEN @MyCursor
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId,@ActualPayment,@AIT,@DisCount
    
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
    
		UPDATE tblInvoice SET PaymentAmount = @ActualPayment, AIT = @AIT, DiscountOnPayment = @DisCount WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
	
    FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
    INTO @InvoiceId,@ActualPayment,@AIT,@DisCount
    
    END
    CLOSE @MyCursor
    DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
END
```

## sp_FreezeStockPosting

**Parameters:**
- `@DcStockConditionId INT`
- `@Quantity DECIMAL(18,2)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, dbo.tblArea, dbo.tblCustMaster, dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze, dbo.tblDcWiseAreaInfo, dbo.tblDeliveryManInfo, dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblMarket, dbo.tblStockConditionFreeze, dbo.tblTerritory, tblCentralStore, tblCompanyUnit, tblDCStore, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster; UPDATE → dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze, tblDCStoreFreeze

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Restricted to `CompanyId = 16`; given `@DcStockConditionId`/`@Quantity`, generates a voucher code and posts a 'Freeze Stock' Debit/Credit pair (value = `UnitPrice * @Quantity`) into `CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster/Detail`, then flags the freeze record `IsPosting` accordingly — the self-contained counterpart to `sp_ADJ_FreezeStockPosting`.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/StockConditionFreezeDAL.cs — via `aCommonInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_FreezeStockPosting", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_FreezeStockPosting] 

	@DcStockConditionId INT,
	@Quantity DECIMAL(18,2)
	
AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @TempCompanyId INT = 0
	
	SELECT @TempCompanyId = CompanyId FROM dbo.tblStockConditionFreeze AS STCF
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze AS DFZS ON DFZS.StockConditionFreezeID = STCF.StockConditionFreezeID
	INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = DFZS.DCStoreId
	INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
	INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = DFZS.ComUnitId
	WHERE STCF.StockConditionFreezeID = @DcStockConditionId


	IF(@TempCompanyId = 16)
	BEGIN

			
			DECLARE @DCFreezeId INT
			DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME 
			DECLARE @CompanyId INT
			DECLARE @CostPrice NVARCHAR(500)

			DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT
			        
			--------------------------------------------------------
			DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
			SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
			FOR
			---------------


			SELECT DFZS.DCStoreFreezeId,UNT.CompanyId,CTRS.UnitPrice,STCF.EntryDate FROM dbo.tblStockConditionFreeze AS STCF
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze AS DFZS ON DFZS.StockConditionFreezeID = STCF.StockConditionFreezeID
			INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = DFZS.DCStoreId
			INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
			INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = DFZS.ComUnitId
			WHERE STCF.StockConditionFreezeID = @DcStockConditionId

			
			----------
			OPEN @MyCursor
			FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
			INTO @DCFreezeId,@CompanyId,@CostPrice,@InvDate
			
			WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
			BEGIN
			

			-- Master Variable Info
			-------------------------------------

			DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(MAX) ='Journal'
			DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

			DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
			DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
			DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
			SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText+@monthText+@dateText)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
			WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
			
				IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
				BEGIN
					SET @VoucherCode='DVB-'+@VoucherCode	
				END
				IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
				BEGIN
					SET @VoucherCode='DVC-'+@VoucherCode
				END
				IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
				BEGIN
					SET @VoucherCode='CVB-'+@VoucherCode	
				END
				IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
				BEGIN
					SET @VoucherCode='CVC-'+@VoucherCode
				END
				IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
				BEGIN
					SET @VoucherCode='FUT-'+@VoucherCode
				END

				IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
				BEGIN
					SET @VoucherCode='JVP-'+@VoucherCode
				END

			DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''

			SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
			WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND CONVERT(varchar, GETDATE(), 5) BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

			----------------------------------

			-- Master Information Posting

			INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
			(
			    VoucherCode,
			    CompanyInfoId,
			    FinancialYearId,
			    PostingDate,
			    VoucherCategoryID,
			    CurrencyId,
			    CurrencyCovRate,
			    Narration,
			    TotalAmount,
			    TotalAmountBDT,
			    EntryBy,
			    EntryDate,
			    VoucherType, 
			    JournalType,
			    DcFreezeId,
				IsCostCenter,
				IsInvoice,
				isManualVCRNo,
				IsExportInvoice,
				IsImportInvoice

			)
			VALUES
			(   
			     @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
			    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
			    ISNULL(@FinYearId,0),         -- FinancialYearId - int
			    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
			    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
			    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
			    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
			    'Freeze Stock  - ( Freeze Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), GETDATE(), 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @CostPrice*@Quantity) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
			    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    @CostPrice*@Quantity,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
			    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
			    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
			    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
			    'Receivable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
			    @DCFreezeId,      -- ProformaId -int
				0,0,0,0,0
			)


			-- Detail Information Posting

			SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

			DECLARE	@DrGl INT 
			SELECT @DrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('FreezeStock') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

			PRINT @DrGl

			-- Debit

			INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
			(
			    VoucherMasterId,
			    AccountId,
			    DebitorCredit,
			    DebitAmount,
			    CreditAmount,
			    DebitAmountBDT,
			    CreditAmountBDT
			)
			VALUES
			(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
			    @DrGl,    -- AccountId - int
			    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
			    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    @CostPrice*@Quantity, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
			    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

			)

			--- Credit

			DECLARE	@CrGl INT 
			SELECT @CrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('FreezeStock') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
			
			PRINT @CrGl

			INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
			(
			    VoucherMasterId,
			    AccountId,
			    DebitorCredit,
			    DebitAmount,
			    CreditAmount,
			    DebitAmountBDT,
			    CreditAmountBDT
			)
			VALUES
			(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
			    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
			    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
			    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
			    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
			    @CostPrice*@Quantity -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
			)


			DECLARE @Count INT = 0 

			SELECT @Count = COUNT(DcFreezeId) FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE DcFreezeId = @DCFreezeId

			IF (@Count > 0)
				BEGIN
				   
				   UPDATE dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze SET IsPosting = 1 WHERE DCStoreFreezeId = @DCFreezeId

				END

			ELSE

				BEGIN

				UPDATE tblDCStoreFreeze SET IsPosting = 0 WHERE DCStoreFreezeId = @DCFreezeId

				END
			
			
			FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
			INTO @DCFreezeId,@CompanyId,@CostPrice,@InvDate

			
			END
			CLOSE @MyCursor
			DEALLOCATE @MyCursor


	END
	
	
	

	
END


--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice

--SELECT * FROM dbo.tblDeliveryManInfo
--INNER JOIN dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo ON tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmpInfoId = tblDeliveryManInfo.EmpInfoId
--WHERE DeliveryManId = 4


--SELECT * FROM dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze


--SELECT DISTINCT * FROM dbo.tblCustMaster AS CSTMR
--LEFT JOIN dbo.tblMarket AS MKT ON MKT.MarketId = CSTMR.MarketId
--LEFT JOIN dbo.tblTerritory AS TTR ON TTR.TerritoryId = MKT.TerritoryId
--LEFT JOIN dbo.tblArea AS ARA ON ARA.AreaId = TTR.AreaId
--INNER JOIN dbo.tblDcWiseAreaInfo AS DCA ON DCA.AreaId = ARA.AreaId


--SELECT * FROM dbo.tblStockConditionFreeze
```

## sp_FreezeStockReleasePosting

**Parameters:**
- `@FreezeId INT`
- `@Quantity DECIMAL(18,2)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew, dbo.tblArea, dbo.tblCustMaster, dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze, dbo.tblDcWiseAreaInfo, dbo.tblDeliveryManInfo, dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblMarket, dbo.tblTerritory, tblCentralStore, tblCompanyUnit, tblDCStore, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** INSERT → CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail, CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster; UPDATE → dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze, tblDCStoreFreeze

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Mirror of `sp_FreezeStockPosting` for releasing previously-frozen stock (`@FreezeId`/`@Quantity`), using the 'FreezeStockRelease' GL mapping key; the self-contained counterpart to `sp_ADJ_FreezeStockReleasePosting`.

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/OtherStockActionDAL.cs — via `aCommonInternalDal.UpdateAction("sp_FreezeStockReleasePosting", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,01/15/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_FreezeStockReleasePosting] 

	@FreezeId INT,
	@Quantity DECIMAL(18,2)
	
AS
BEGIN
	
	DECLARE @TempCompanyId INT = 0
	
	SELECT @TempCompanyId = CompanyId FROM dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze AS DFZS 
	INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = DFZS.DCStoreId
	INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
	INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = DFZS.ComUnitId
	WHERE DFZS.DCStoreFreezeId = @FreezeId  



	IF(@TempCompanyId = 16)
	BEGIN

		DECLARE @DCFreezeId INT
		DECLARE @InvDate DATETIME = GETDATE()
		DECLARE @CompanyId INT
		DECLARE @CostPrice NVARCHAR(500)

		DECLARE @VoucherMasterId INT 
		        
		--------------------------------------------------------
		DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
		SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
		FOR
		---------------
		
		SELECT DFZS.DCStoreFreezeId,UNT.CompanyId,CTRS.UnitPrice FROM dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze AS DFZS 
		INNER JOIN tblDCStore AS DCS ON DCS.DCStoreId = DFZS.DCStoreId
		INNER JOIN tblCentralStore AS CTRS ON DCS.TempReceiveId = CTRS.ReceiveId
		INNER JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON UNT.ComUnitId = DFZS.ComUnitId
		WHERE DFZS.DCStoreFreezeId = @FreezeId  


		----------
		OPEN @MyCursor
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @DCFreezeId,@CompanyId,@CostPrice
		
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
		BEGIN
		

		-- Master Variable Info
		-------------------------------------

		DECLARE @VoucherType NVARCHAR(MAX) ='Journal'
		DECLARE @VoucherCode NVARCHAR(MAX)

		DECLARE @yearText NVARCHAR(MAX)= SUBSTRING(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),YEAR(@InvDate)), 3, 3)   
		DECLARE @monthText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(MONTH(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),MONTH(@InvDate)) END)
		DECLARE @dateText NVARCHAR(MAX)=(CASE WHEN  LEN(DAY(@InvDate))=1 THEN '0'+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) ELSE CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),DAY(@InvDate)) END)
		SELECT @VoucherCode = (@yearText+@monthText+@dateText)+CONVERT(NVARCHAR(MAX),CONVERT(INT,ISNULL(MAX(SUBSTRING(VoucherCode,11,6)),10000))+1)  FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster 
		WHERE @InvDate = @InvDate
		
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='DVB-'+@VoucherCode	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='DebitVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='DVC-'+@VoucherCode
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherBank')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='CVB-'+@VoucherCode	
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='CreditVoucherCash')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='CVC-'+@VoucherCode
			END
			IF(@VoucherType='FundTransfer')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='FUT-'+@VoucherCode
			END

			IF(@VoucherType='Journal')
			BEGIN
				SET @VoucherCode='JVP-'+@VoucherCode
			END

		DECLARE @FinYearId NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''

		SELECT @FinYearId = FinancialYearId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblFinancialYear 
		WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId AND Status IN ('Active') AND CONVERT(varchar, GETDATE(), 5) BETWEEN StartDate AND EndDate

		----------------------------------

		-- Master Information Posting

		INSERT	INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster
		(
		    VoucherCode,
		    CompanyInfoId,
		    FinancialYearId,
		    PostingDate,
		    VoucherCategoryID,
		    CurrencyId,
		    CurrencyCovRate,
		    Narration,
		    TotalAmount,
		    TotalAmountBDT,
		    EntryBy,
		    EntryDate,
		    VoucherType, 
		    JournalType,
		    DcFreezeId,
			IsCostCenter,
			IsInvoice,
			isManualVCRNo,
			IsExportInvoice,
			IsImportInvoice

		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    @VoucherCode,       -- VoucherCode - nvarchar(max)
		    @CompanyId,         -- CompanyInfoId - int
		    ISNULL(@FinYearId,0),         -- FinancialYearId - int
		    @InvDate, -- PostingDate - datetime
		    2,         -- VoucherCategoryID - int
		    1,         -- CurrencyId - int
		    1.00,      -- CurrencyCovRate - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Freeze Stock Release - ( Release Date: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), GETDATE(), 101) + ', Stock Value: ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), @CostPrice*@Quantity) + ' )',       -- Narration - nvarchar(max)
		    0.00,      -- TotalAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice*@Quantity,      -- TotalAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    'Auto Process',       -- EntryBy - nvarchar(max)
		    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -- EntryDate - datetime	  
		    'Journal',       -- VoucherType - nvarchar(50)
		    'Receivable',       -- JournalType - nvarchar(50)
		    @DCFreezeId,      -- ProformaId -int
			0,0,0,0,0
		)

		-- Detail Information Posting

		SET @VoucherMasterId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		DECLARE	@DrGl INT 
		SELECT @DrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('FreezeStockRelease') AND BalanceType IN ('Dr')

		-- Debit

		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @DrGl,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Debit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice*@Quantity, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00 -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)

		)

		--- Credit

		DECLARE	@CrGl INT 
		SELECT @CrGl = GlId FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblSalesIntegrationMapingNew WHERE Particular IN ('FreezeStockRelease') AND BalanceType IN ('Cr')
		
		INSERT INTO CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherDetail
		(
		    VoucherMasterId,
		    AccountId,
		    DebitorCredit,
		    DebitAmount,
		    CreditAmount,
		    DebitAmountBDT,
		    CreditAmountBDT
		)
		VALUES
		(   @VoucherMasterId,    -- VoucherMasterId - int
		    @CrGl,    -- AccountId - int
		    'Credit',  -- DebitorCredit - nvarchar(50)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- CreditAmount - decimal(18, 2)
		    0.00, -- DebitAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		    @CostPrice*@Quantity -- CreditAmountBDT - decimal(18, 2)
		)


		DECLARE @Count INT = 0 

		SELECT @Count = COUNT(DcFreezeId) FROM CSTL_ERP_ACCDB..tblDebitCreditVoucherMaster WHERE DcFreezeId = @DCFreezeId

		IF (@Count > 0)
			BEGIN
			   
			   UPDATE dbo.tblDCStoreFreeze SET IsPosting = 1 WHERE DCStoreFreezeId = @DCFreezeId

			END

		ELSE

			BEGIN

			UPDATE tblDCStoreFreeze SET IsPosting = 0 WHERE DCStoreFreezeId = @DCFreezeId

			END
		
		
		FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
		INTO @DCFreezeId,@CompanyId,@CostPrice

		
		END
		CLOSE @MyCursor
		DEALLOCATE @MyCursor

	END


	

	
END


--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice
```

## sp_GET_DCStockErrorInfo

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblChalanDetail, dbo.tblCustMaster, dbo.tblCustomerCreditLimit, tblCompanyUnit, tblDCStore, tblDCStoreFreeze, tblDestroyProduct, tblDirectStockOut, tblInvoice, tblInvoiceDetail, tblSampleIssue, tblSampleIssueDetail, tblSampleIssueTranscation
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Stock-reconciliation report with no parameters: for every DC-store row, sums up every kind of stock movement (B2B challan-out, sold, booked-for-delivery, sample issue, freeze/restricted/blocked, direct stock-out, destroyed) and compares `TotalQuantity - StockQty` against the sum of those movements, surfacing only rows where they don't reconcile (an `Error` column).

**Result set:** Single result set: per-DC-store product with received/available/each-movement-type quantities, computed TotalIssue, Closing, and Error (mismatch) columns, restricted to non-zero-error rows.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_DCStockErrorInfo] 

 

AS
BEGIN

--SELECT * FROM tblInvoice AS INV
--	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS IVD ON INV.InvoiceId = IVD.InvoiceId
--	WHERE InvoiceNo = 'INV-BD202-2508260011'


--	SELECT INV.InvoiceId,InvoiceNo,InvoiceDate,SUM(TotalQuantity) FROM tblInvoice AS INV
--	LEFT JOIN tblInvoiceDetail AS IVD ON INV.InvoiceId = IVD.InvoiceId
--	WHERE InvoiceDate BETWEEN '2025-08-20' AND '2025-08-30' AND ProductCode = '397031'
--	GROUP BY INV.InvoiceId,InvoiceNo,InvoiceDate


	
	SELECT DCS.DCStoreId,ProductCode,ProductName,ReceiveDate,TotalQuantity,StockQty AS AvilableStock,ISNULL(B2BS.Challan,0) B2bIssue, ISNULL(SoldQuantity,0) SoldQuantity, ISNULL(BookForDelivery,0) BookForDelivery,
	 ISNULL(SampleQty,0) SampleQty, ISNULL(FRZ.DcFreeze,0) ReturnStock, ISNULL(FRZR.DcFreeze,0) Restricted, ISNULL(FRZB.DcFreeze,0) Blocked, ISNULL(DOT.DirectStockOutQty,0) DirectStockOutQty,
	 ISNULL(Destroy,0) Destroy, ISNULL(B2BS.Challan,0) + ISNULL(SoldQuantity,0) + ISNULL(BookForDelivery,0) 
	 + ISNULL(SampleQty,0) + ISNULL(FRZ.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZR.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZB.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(DOT.DirectStockOutQty,0) + ISNULL(Destroy,0) AS TotalIssue,
	
	 TotalQuantity - (ISNULL(B2BS.Challan,0) + ISNULL(SoldQuantity,0) + ISNULL(BookForDelivery,0) 
	 + ISNULL(SampleQty,0) + ISNULL(FRZ.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZR.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZB.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(DOT.DirectStockOutQty,0) + ISNULL(Destroy,0)) AS Closing,

	 TotalQuantity - (StockQty + ISNULL(B2BS.Challan,0) + ISNULL(SoldQuantity,0) + ISNULL(BookForDelivery,0) 
	 + ISNULL(SampleQty,0) + ISNULL(FRZ.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZR.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZB.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(DOT.DirectStockOutQty,0) + ISNULL(Destroy,0)) Error
	
	FROM tblDCStore AS DCS with(nolock) 
	LEFT JOIN tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON DCS.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
	
	-- B2B Stock Out
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT CLD.DcStoreId,SUM(Quantity) Challan FROM dbo.tblChalanDetail AS CLD  with(nolock) GROUP BY CLD.DcStoreId) AS B2BS ON DCS.DcStoreId = B2BS.DcStoreId 
	
	-- Sold Qty
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT INVD.DcStoreId,SUM(INVD.DeliveryQuantity) AS SoldQuantity FROM tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD with(nolock) 
	WHERE INVD.DeliveryStatus IN ('Full','Partial') GROUP BY INVD.DcStoreId) AS DLQ ON DCS.DcStoreId = DLQ.DCStoreId 
	
	-- Book for delivery
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT INVD.DcStoreId,SUM(Quantity) AS BookForDelivery FROM tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD with(nolock) 
	LEFT JOIN tblDCStore AS IDC with(nolock)  ON INVD.DCStoreId = IDC.DcStoreId
	LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON INVD.InvoiceId = INV.Invoiceid
	WHERE INVD.DeliveryStatus IS NULL GROUP BY INVD.DcStoreId) AS IBD 
	ON DCS.DcStoreId = IBD.DcStoreId 
	
	-- Sample Issue
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT ST.DCStoreId,SUM(ST.Quantity)*-1 AS SampleQty FROM tblSampleIssueDetail AS D
	LEFT JOIN tblSampleIssueTranscation AS ST ON D.OrderDetailId = ST.IssueDetailId
	LEFT JOIN tblSampleIssue AS M ON D.OrderId = M.OrderId
	GROUP BY ST.DCStoreId) AS SMP ON DCS.DCStoreId = SMP.DCStoreId
	
	
	-- Direct Stock Out
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DCStoreId,SUM(StockOutQty) AS DirectStockOutQty FROM tblDirectStockOut  GROUP BY DCStoreId) AS DOT ON DCS.DCStoreId = DOT.DCStoreId
	
	
	-- Freeze Stock (Delivery Confirm Return, B2B Return, STO Retun)
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT FZ.DcStoreId,SUM(FZ.StockQty) AS DcFreeze FROM tblDCStoreFreeze AS FZ
	LEFT JOIN tblDCStore AS FZDC with(nolock)  ON FZ.DCStoreId = FZDC.DcStoreId 
	WHERE FZ.StockCondition IS NULL OR FZ.StockCondition = 'ReturnStock' GROUP BY FZ.DcStoreId) AS FRZ ON DCS.DcStoreId = FRZ.DcStoreId 
	
	-- Freeze Stock (Restricted)
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT FZ.DcStoreId,SUM(FZ.StockQty) AS DcFreeze FROM tblDCStoreFreeze AS FZ
	LEFT JOIN tblDCStore AS FZDC with(nolock)  ON FZ.DCStoreId = FZDC.DcStoreId 
	WHERE FZ.StockCondition = 'Restricted' GROUP BY FZ.DcStoreId) AS FRZR ON DCS.DcStoreId = FRZR.DcStoreId 
	
	--Freeze Stock (Blocked)
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT FZ.DcStoreId,SUM(FZ.StockQty) AS DcFreeze FROM tblDCStoreFreeze AS FZ
	LEFT JOIN tblDCStore AS FZDC with(nolock)  ON FZ.DCStoreId = FZDC.DcStoreId 
	WHERE FZ.StockCondition = 'Blocked' GROUP BY FZ.DcStoreId) AS FRZB ON DCS.DcStoreId = FRZB.DcStoreId 
	
	-- Destroy
	
	LEFT JOIN (SELECT DCStoreId,SUM(StockOutQty) AS Destroy FROM tblDestroyProduct AS DT
	LEFT JOIN tblDCStoreFreeze AS DFZ ON DT.DcFreezeId = DFZ.DCStoreFreezeId 
	GROUP BY DCStoreId) AS DST ON DCS.DcStoreId = DST.DcStoreId 
	
	
	WHERE  TotalQuantity - (StockQty + ISNULL(B2BS.Challan,0) + ISNULL(SoldQuantity,0) + ISNULL(BookForDelivery,0) 
	 + ISNULL(SampleQty,0) + ISNULL(FRZ.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZR.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(FRZB.DcFreeze,0) + ISNULL(DOT.DirectStockOutQty,0) + ISNULL(Destroy,0)) != 0
	
	--AND ProductCode = '373063'

	ORDER BY ReceiveDate
	
END


--SELECT * FROM dbo.tblCustomerCreditLimit
--INNER JOIN dbo.tblCustMaster ON tblCustMaster.CustomerMasterId = tblCustomerCreditLimit.CustomerMasterId
--WHERE CustomerCode IN ('17686')
```

## sp_GET_DashboardCardInfo

**Parameters:**
- `@CurrentDate DATETIME`
- `@CompanyId NVARCHAR(MAX)`
- `@FromDate DATETIME`
- `@ToDate DATETIME`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** date, dbo.View_CustomerMaster, dbo.tblCompanyInfo, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblInvoice, dbo.tblInvoiceDetail, tblCompanyInfo, tblCompanyUnit, tblCustPayDetail, tblCustomerPay, tblInvoice, tblOrder
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given a current date, company id, and an optional from/to date range, computes a set of dashboard KPI cards (number of accepted orders, number of invoices, number of delivery-confirmed invoices, actual sales, total collection, total due) for whichever date scope was supplied (single from-date only, single to-date only, a from/to range, or neither → falls back to `@CurrentDate`).

**Result set:** Single result set (via a table variable): one row with `NoOfOrder, NoOfInvoice, DeliveryConfirmed, ActualSales, TotalCollection, TotalDue`.

**Application caller(s):** Library.DAL/SInventory_DAL/SalesDashboardDal.cs — via `_aCommonInternalDal.GetDataTableAction("sp_GET_DashboardCardInfo", ...)`.

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_DashboardCardInfo] 

	@CurrentDate DATETIME,
	@CompanyId NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@FromDate DATETIME ,
	@ToDate DATETIME

AS
BEGIN
	

	  DECLARE @Sales TABLE 
	(

	  NoOfOrder DECIMAL(18,2),
	  NoOfInvoice DECIMAL(18,2),
	  DeliveryConfirmed DECIMAL(18,2),
	  ActualSales DECIMAL(18,2),
	  TotalCollection DECIMAL(18,2),
	  TotalDue DECIMAL(18,2)
	)


	DECLARE @NoOfOrder DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @NoOfInvoice DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @DeliveryConfirmed DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @ActualSales DECIMAL(18,2) 
	DECLARE @TotalCollection DECIMAL(18,2)
	DECLARE @TotalDue DECIMAL(18,2)


	-- In case of only from date

	IF(@FromDate IS NOT NULL AND @ToDate IS NULL)
	BEGIN 

		SELECT @NoOfOrder =  COUNT(OrderCode) FROM tblOrder 
	    WHERE ManufacId = @CompanyId AND SubmissionDate = @FromDate AND ActionStatus IN ('Accepted')

		SELECT @NoOfInvoice = COUNT(INV.InvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceDate = @FromDate AND INV.InvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @DeliveryConfirmed = COUNT(INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.UpdateDate = @FromDate AND INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @ActualSales = SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) 
		FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND INVD.Quantity > 0  
		AND INV.UpdateDate = @FromDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @TotalCollection = SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE PaymentDate = @FromDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


		SELECT @TotalDue = SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal- (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.Discount,0)))  FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId  
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId 
		LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
		WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= @FromDate GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') 
		AND INV.InvoiceDate <= @FromDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


	END

	-- -- In case of only to date

	ELSE IF(@FromDate IS NULL AND @ToDate IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN 

		SELECT @NoOfOrder =  COUNT(OrderCode) FROM tblOrder 
	    WHERE ManufacId = @CompanyId AND SubmissionDate = @ToDate AND ActionStatus IN ('Accepted')

		SELECT @NoOfInvoice = COUNT(INV.InvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.InvoiceDate = @ToDate AND INV.InvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @DeliveryConfirmed = COUNT(INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.UpdateDate = @ToDate AND INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @ActualSales = SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) 
		FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND INVD.Quantity > 0  
		AND INV.UpdateDate = @ToDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @TotalCollection = SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE PaymentDate = @ToDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


		SELECT @TotalDue = SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal- (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.Discount,0)))  FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId  
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId 
		LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
		WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= @ToDate GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') 
		AND INV.InvoiceDate <= @ToDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


	END

	-- -- In case of from & to date

	ELSE IF(@FromDate IS NOT NULL AND @ToDate IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN 

		SELECT @NoOfOrder =  COUNT(OrderCode) FROM tblOrder 
	    WHERE ManufacId = @CompanyId AND (SubmissionDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate) AND ActionStatus IN ('Accepted')

		SELECT @NoOfInvoice = COUNT(INV.InvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
		WHERE (INV.InvoiceDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate) AND INV.InvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @DeliveryConfirmed = COUNT(INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
		WHERE (INV.UpdateDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate) AND INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @ActualSales = SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) 
		FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND INVD.Quantity > 0  
		AND (INV.UpdateDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate) AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

		SELECT @TotalCollection = SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
		LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
		WHERE PaymentDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


		SELECT @TotalDue = SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal- (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.Discount,0)))  FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId  
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId 
		LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
		LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
		WHERE (CP.PaymentDate BETWEEN @FromDate AND @ToDate) GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') 
		AND INV.InvoiceDate <= @ToDate  AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


	END

	-- -- In case of from & to date not exist

	ELSE 
	BEGIN 

			SELECT @NoOfOrder =  COUNT(OrderCode) FROM tblOrder 
			WHERE ManufacId = @CompanyId AND SubmissionDate = @CurrentDate AND ActionStatus IN ('Accepted')

			SELECT @NoOfInvoice = COUNT(INV.InvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
			LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
			LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
			WHERE INV.InvoiceDate = @CurrentDate AND INV.InvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

			SELECT @DeliveryConfirmed = COUNT(INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo) FROM tblInvoice AS INV 
			LEFT JOIN  tblCompanyUnit AS UT ON UT.ComUnitId = INV.ComUnitId
			LEFT JOIN tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON Ci.CompanyId = UT.CompanyId
			WHERE INV.UpdateDate = @CurrentDate AND INV.DelivaryInvoiceNo IS NOT NULL AND INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId 

			SELECT @ActualSales = SUM(CASE WHEN INVD.DeliveryNetAmount > 0 THEN  INVD.DeliveryNetAmount  ELSE 0 END) 
			FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblInvoiceDetail AS INVD ON INV.InvoiceId = INVD.InvoiceId
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
			WHERE INV.DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') AND INVD.Quantity > 0  
			AND INV.UpdateDate = @CurrentDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId

			SELECT @TotalCollection = SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD
			LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId
			LEFT JOIN tblInvoice AS INV ON CPD.InvoiceId = INV.InvoiceId
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId
			WHERE PaymentDate = @CurrentDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


			SELECT @TotalDue = SUM(DeliveryTpGrandTotal- (ISNULL(PMNT.PaymentAmount,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.AIT,0) + ISNULL(PMNT.Discount,0)))  FROM dbo.tblInvoice AS INV 
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyUnit AS UNT ON INV.ComUnitId = UNT.ComUnitId  
			LEFT JOIN dbo.tblCompanyInfo AS CI ON UNT.CompanyId = CI.CompanyId 
			LEFT JOIN ( SELECT InvoiceId,SUM(CPD.PaymentAmount) AS PaymentAmount,SUM(CPD.AIT) AIT, SUM(CPD.Discount) Discount FROM tblCustPayDetail AS CPD  
			LEFT JOIN tblCustomerPay AS CP ON CPD.CustPayId = CP.CustPayId 
			WHERE CP.PaymentDate <= @CurrentDate GROUP BY InvoiceId ) AS PMNT ON PMNT.InvoiceId = inv.InvoiceId 
			LEFT JOIN dbo.View_CustomerMaster AS VC ON INV.CustomerMasterId = VC.CustomerMasterId WHERE DeliveryInvoiceStatus IN ('Full','Partial') 
			AND INV.InvoiceDate <= @CurrentDate AND CI.CompanyId = @CompanyId


	END


	INSERT INTO @Sales
		(
		    NoOfOrder,
			NoOfInvoice,
			DeliveryConfirmed,
			ActualSales,
			TotalCollection,
			TotalDue
		)
		VALUES
		(   
		    ISNULL(@NoOfOrder,0), -- InvoiceValue - decimal(18, 2)
		    ISNULL(@NoOfInvoice,0), -- ActualSales - decimal(18, 2)
		    ISNULL(@DeliveryConfirmed,0), -- InTransit - decimal(18, 2)
		    ISNULL(@ActualSales,0),  -- Reject - decimal(18, 2)
			ISNULL(@TotalCollection,0),  -- Reject - decimal(18, 2)
			ISNULL(@TotalDue,0) 
			
		)


	SELECT * FROM @Sales 


END
```

## sp_GET_Dashboard_TeleJiggasha_NetworkWiseOutboundSurvey

**Parameters:**
- `@FromDate DATE = NULL`
- `@ToDate DATE = NULL`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** X, Y, dbo.tbl_NetworkWiseOutboundSurvey_Telejiggasha
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Lists TeleJiggasha network-wise outbound survey programs, optionally filtered to a month/year range (`@FromDate`/`@ToDate`, both optional): converts each row's text `MonthName`/`Year` into a real date for filtering/sorting purposes via a CASE-based month-name lookup.

**Result set:** Single result set: `NetworkWiseOutboundSurveyId, ProgramName, SampleSize, ObjectiveOfSurvey, MonthName, Year`, ordered by year/month descending then id descending.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE   PROCEDURE dbo.sp_GET_Dashboard_TeleJiggasha_NetworkWiseOutboundSurvey
    @FromDate DATE = NULL,
    @ToDate DATE = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @FilterStart DATE = CASE
        WHEN @FromDate IS NULL THEN NULL
        ELSE DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(@FromDate), MONTH(@FromDate), 1)
    END;

    DECLARE @FilterEnd DATE = CASE
        WHEN @ToDate IS NULL THEN NULL
        ELSE EOMONTH(@ToDate)
    END;

    ;WITH X AS
    (
        SELECT
            NetworkWiseOutboundSurveyId,
            ProgramName,
            SampleSize,
            ObjectiveOfSurvey,
            MonthName,
            [Year],
            MonthNo = CASE UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(MonthName, ''))))
                WHEN 'JANUARY' THEN 1
                WHEN 'FEBRUARY' THEN 2
                WHEN 'MARCH' THEN 3
                WHEN 'APRIL' THEN 4
                WHEN 'MAY' THEN 5
                WHEN 'JUNE' THEN 6
                WHEN 'JULY' THEN 7
                WHEN 'AUGUST' THEN 8
                WHEN 'SEPTEMBER' THEN 9
                WHEN 'OCTOBER' THEN 10
                WHEN 'NOVEMBER' THEN 11
                WHEN 'DECEMBER' THEN 12
                ELSE NULL
            END
        FROM dbo.tbl_NetworkWiseOutboundSurvey_Telejiggasha
    ),
    Y AS
    (
        SELECT
            NetworkWiseOutboundSurveyId,
            ProgramName,
            SampleSize,
            ObjectiveOfSurvey,
            MonthName,
            [Year],
            MonthNo,
            MonthDate = CASE
                WHEN [Year] IS NOT NULL AND MonthNo IS NOT NULL
                    THEN DATEFROMPARTS([Year], MonthNo, 1)
                ELSE NULL
            END
        FROM X
    )
    SELECT
        NetworkWiseOutboundSurveyId,
        ProgramName,
        SampleSize,
        ObjectiveOfSurvey,
        MonthName,
        [Year]
    FROM Y
    WHERE (@FilterStart IS NULL OR MonthDate >= @FilterStart)
      AND (@FilterEnd IS NULL OR MonthDate <= @FilterEnd)
    ORDER BY ISNULL([Year], 0) DESC,
             ISNULL(MonthNo, 99),
             NetworkWiseOutboundSurveyId DESC;
END
```

## sp_GET_DoctorMaster_ById

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @id NVARCHAR(max)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** dbo.tblDoctorBrandDetail, dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail, dbo.tblDoctorProgramTypeDetail, dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail, tblArea, tblDoctorChamber, tblDoctorChemberDetail, tblDoctorMaster, tblMarket, tblRegion, tblTerritory, tbl_Group
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given a doctor `@id`, returns the full doctor master row enriched with comma-concatenated brand/degree/speciality/program-type ids (via correlated STUFF/FOR XML PATH subqueries) and the doctor's chamber, market, territory, area, region, and group ids/name via a chain of LEFT JOINs.

**Result set:** Single result set: one row with the doctor's full profile plus the concatenated id lists and resolved geography/chamber columns.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
 CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_DoctorMaster_ById]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
   @id NVARCHAR(max)

AS
    BEGIN

	 Select mas.ProgramTypeId, dChem.ChamberTypeId ChamberTypeId, dChemT.chamberName ChamberTypeName , dChem.Name ChamberName, dChem.Phone Phone,dchem.Address ChamberAddress, STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',', brn.BrandId , '') FROM dbo.tblDoctorBrandDetail brn(NOLOCK)  WHERE brn.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY brn.BrandId FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'') AS BrandId, STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',', mgd.DegId , '') FROM dbo.tblDoctorDegreeDetail mgd(NOLOCK)  WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorDegId FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'') AS DegreeId,  STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',', mgd.SpecialityId , '') FROM dbo.tblDoctorSpecialityDetail mgd (NOLOCK)   WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd.DoctorSpId FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'') as DoctorSpecialityId ,  STUFF( (SELECT CONCAT(',', mgd.ProgramTypeId , '') FROM dbo.tblDoctorProgramTypeDetail mgd with (NOLOCK)  WHERE mgd.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId ORDER BY mgd. DoctorTypeDetailId FOR XML PATH ('') ),1,1,'') as ProgramTypeId, mas.MarketId,  Tr.TerritoryId, Ar.AreaId, rg.RegionId,gr.GroupId,   mas.* from tblDoctorMaster mas with (nolock)
	 
	
	 left join  tblMarket mr on mas.MarketId=mr.MarketId
	 
	 left join  tblTerritory  Tr on mr.TerritoryId=Tr.TerritoryId
	 left join  tblArea  Ar on Ar.AreaId=Tr.AreaId
	 left join  tblRegion  rg on Ar.RegionId=rg.RegionId
	 left join  tbl_Group  gr on gr.GroupId=rg.GroupId

	 left join  tblDoctorChemberDetail  dChem on dChem.DoctorId=mas.DoctorId
	 left join  tblDoctorChamber  dChemT on dChemT.ChamberId=dChem.ChamberTypeId








	  where mas.DoctorId = @id
      
    END
```

## sp_GET_DueInvoiceNoCorrection

**Parameters:**
None (no parameters).

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** My_table, dbo.tblCustMaster, dbo.tblCustomerCreditLimit, fn_split_string_to_column, tblInvoice
**Tables written:** UPDATE → dbo.tblInvoice

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Data-fix job with no parameters: finds invoices remarked 'Due' whose `InvoiceNo` starts with 'I' (an inconsistent/malformed number), splits the number on '-' using `fn_split_string_to_column`, and reconstructs it in a normalized `D-<part1>-<part2>-<part3>` shape, then writes it back — note the reconstructed value is built into `@Date` but the final UPDATE still writes the original `@InvoiceNo`, so the normalization appears to have no actual effect (likely a bug).

**Result set:** No result set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
-- =============================================
-- Author: <Author,JEWEL>
-- Create date: <Create Date,02/08/2016,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- ============================================

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_DueInvoiceNoCorrection] 



AS
BEGIN
	
DECLARE @InvoiceId INT
DECLARE @InvoiceNo NVARCHAR(50)

DECLARE @ColumnId INT
DECLARE @Data NVARCHAR(100)

DECLARE @CountData INT
DECLARE @Date NVARCHAR(MAX)
   
--------------------------------------------------------
DECLARE @MyCursor CURSOR
SET @MyCursor = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
FOR
---------------

SELECT InvoiceId,InvoiceNo FROM tblInvoice WHERE Remarks IN ('Due') AND InvoiceNo LIKE 'I%'
	
----------
OPEN @MyCursor
FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
INTO @InvoiceId,@InvoiceNo

WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN

DECLARE @SplitData CURSOR 
SET @SplitData = CURSOR FAST_FORWARD
FOR
--Select Name ,Mobile from  My_table (Nolock)
SELECT  column_id,value	 FROM fn_split_string_to_column(@InvoiceNo,'-')
 
OPEN @SplitData
FETCH NEXT FROM @SplitData 
INTO @ColumnId,@Data
WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN

SELECT  @CountData=COUNT(*)	 FROM fn_split_string_to_column(@InvoiceNo,'-')

IF(@CountData>2)
BEGIN
    
	IF(@ColumnId=1)
	BEGIN
	    
		SET @Date = 'D-' + @Data + '- '
	END
	IF(@ColumnId=2)
	BEGIN
	    
		SET @Date = @Date + @Data + '-'
	END
	IF(@ColumnId=3)
	BEGIN
		SET @Date = @Date + @Data
	END
END


FETCH NEXT FROM @SplitData 
INTO @ColumnId,@Data
END
CLOSE @SplitData
DEALLOCATE @SplitData

UPDATE dbo.tblInvoice SET InvoiceNo = @InvoiceNo WHERE InvoiceId = @InvoiceId

FETCH NEXT FROM @MyCursor
INTO @InvoiceId,@InvoiceNo

END
CLOSE @MyCursor
DEALLOCATE @MyCursor
	
END


--SELECT * FROM dbo.tblCustomerCreditLimit
--INNER JOIN dbo.tblCustMaster ON tblCustMaster.CustomerMasterId = tblCustomerCreditLimit.CustomerMasterId
--WHERE CustomerCode IN ('17686')
```

## sp_GET_EmpLatLongByempId

**Parameters:**
- `-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here @EmpInfoId int`
- `@ShiftId int`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** tblEmpLatLong
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@EmpInfoId`/`@ShiftId`, returns that employee/shift's recorded lat/long rows if any exist, otherwise returns an (always-empty) result set filtered on a sentinel `EmpInfoId = 1000000000` so the caller still gets a well-formed empty result rather than no rows/error.

**Result set:** Single result set: all columns of `tblEmpLatLong`, either the real rows for the employee/shift or an empty set.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_EmpLatLongByempId]
	-- Add the parameters for the stored procedure here
	@EmpInfoId int,
	@ShiftId int

AS
BEGIN
		declare @Count int =0
			 select @Count= ISNULL(COUNT(*),0) from  tblEmpLatLong with (nolock) where  EmpInfoId=@EmpInfoId and  ShiftId=@ShiftId

			 if(@Count>0)
			 begin
			 select * from  tblEmpLatLong with (nolock) where  EmpInfoId=@EmpInfoId  and  ShiftId=@ShiftId
			 end
			 else
			  begin
			 select * from  tblEmpLatLong with (nolock) where  EmpInfoId=1000000000
			 end
				
			

END
```

## sp_GET_Employee_LeaveAvailablePreload_HRAPI

**Parameters:**
- `@LeaveYear Nvarchar(MAx)`
- `@EmpInfoId Nvarchar(MAx)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblDepartment, ZASHRIS..tblDesignation, ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo, ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory, tblEmpGeneralInfo
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Given `@LeaveYear`/`@EmpInfoId`, resolves the employee's master code then looks up (one query per leave type) the yearly allotment and remaining balance for Casual/Medical/Earn/Maternity leave from the cross-database `ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory` table.

**Result set:** Single result set: `YearCasual, CasualRemain, YearMedical, MedicalRemain, YearEarn, EarnRemain, YearMaternity, MaternityRemain` (each defaulted to 0 if not found).

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_Employee_LeaveAvailablePreload_HRAPI] 
	
	@LeaveYear Nvarchar(MAx),
	@EmpInfoId Nvarchar(MAx)
AS
BEGIN

  DECLARE @EmpMasterCode Nvarchar(MAX)

	SELECT @EmpMasterCode=EmpMasterCode FROM tblEmpGeneralInfo WHERE EmpInfoId=@EmpInfoId  

DECLARE	@YearCasual Nvarchar(MAX),	@CasualRemain Nvarchar(MAX),	@YearMedical Nvarchar(MAX),	@MedicalRemain Nvarchar(MAX),	@YearEarn Nvarchar(MAX),	@EarnRemain	Nvarchar(MAX), @YearMaternity Nvarchar(MAX),	@MaternityRemain Nvarchar(MAX)
 
	
  SELECT  @YearCasual=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Casual'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @CasualRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Casual'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   
 
 
  SELECT  @YearMedical=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Medical'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @MedicalRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Medical'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  
 
  SELECT  @YearEarn=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Earn'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @EarnRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Earn'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

   
  SELECT  @YearMaternity=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Maternity'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @MaternityRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Maternity'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   


  select ISNULL(@YearCasual,0) YearCasual	,ISNULL(@CasualRemain,0) CasualRemain	,ISNULL(@YearMedical,0) YearMedical	,ISNULL(@MedicalRemain,0)	 MedicalRemain,ISNULL(@YearEarn,0) YearEarn	,ISNULL(@EarnRemain,0) EarnRemain	,ISNULL(@YearMaternity,0) YearMaternity	,ISNULL(@MaternityRemain,0) MaternityRemain
 
 
  --select  distinct LeaveName   FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory



  --SELECT  																			
		--				ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.YearCasual),'') AS YearCasual ,
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.CasualRemain),'') AS CasualRemain,
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.YearMedical),'') AS YearMedical,
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.MedicalRemain),'') AS MedicalRemain,
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.YearEarn),'') AS YearEarn,
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.EarnRemain),'') AS EarnRemain,
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.YearMaternity) ,'') AS YearMaternity ,					         
  --                      ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar,tblLeaveMain.MaternityRemain),'') AS MaternityRemain
  --                      FROM ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo
  --                      LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDesignation ON tblEmpGeneralInfo.DesigId = tblDesignation.DesigId
  --                      LEFT JOIN ZASHRIS..tblDepartment ON  tblEmpGeneralInfo.DepId= tblDepartment.DeptId
                       
  --                      LEFT JOIN 
  --                      (SELECT tblMainLeave.EmpMasterCode,
  --                      ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearCasual),0) AS YearCasual
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.CasualRemain),0) AS CasualRemain,
  --                      (ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearCasual),0)-ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.CasualRemain),0)) AS CasualEnjoyed
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearMedical),0) AS YearMedical
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.MedicalRemain),0) AS MedicalRemain,
  --                      (ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearMedical),0)-ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.MedicalRemain),0)) AS MedicalEnjoyed
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearEarn),0) AS YearEarn
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.EarnRemain),0) AS EarnRemain 
  --                      ,(ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearEarn),0)-ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.EarnRemain),0)) AS EarnEnjoyed                 
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearMaternity),0) AS YearMaternity 
  --                      ,ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.MaternityRemain),0) AS MaternityRemain 
  --                      ,(ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.YearMaternity),0)-ISNULL(SUM(tblMainLeave.MaternityRemain),0)) AS MaternityEnjoyed

  --                       FROM(SELECT EmpMasterCode,(CASE WHEN LeaveName='Casual' THEN YearDayQty END) AS YearCasual,(CASE WHEN LeaveName='Casual' THEN DayQty END) AS CasualRemain,
  --                      (CASE WHEN LeaveName='Medical' THEN YearDayQty END) AS YearMedical,(CASE WHEN LeaveName='Medical' THEN DayQty END) AS MedicalRemain,(CASE WHEN LeaveName='Earn' THEN YearDayQty END) AS YearEarn,
  --                      (CASE WHEN LeaveName='Earn' THEN DayQty END) AS EarnRemain ,(CASE WHEN LeaveName='Maternity' THEN YearDayQty END) AS YearMaternity,
  --                      (CASE WHEN LeaveName='Maternity' THEN DayQty END) AS MaternityRemain 
  --                       FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE LeaveYear=@LeaveYear) AS tblMainLeave GROUP BY tblMainLeave.EmpMasterCode) AS tblLeaveMain ON  tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmpMasterCode=tblLeaveMain.EmpMasterCode 
		--				 inner join tblEmpGeneralInfo  mainEmp on tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmpMasterCode=mainEmp.EmpMasterCode
		--				 WHERE  ZASHRIS..tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmployeeStatus='Active'   AND mainEmp.EmpInfoId=@EmpInfoId  ORDER BY tblEmpGeneralInfo.EmpMasterCode
     
END
```

## sp_GET_Employee_LeaveList_HRAPI

**Parameters:**
- `@LeaveYear Nvarchar(MAx)`
- `@EmpInfoId Nvarchar(MAx)`

**Tables read (FROM/JOIN):** ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory, dbo.tblCompanyUnit, dbo.tblDepartment, dbo.tblDesignation, dbo.tblEmpGeneralInfo, dbo.tblLeaveInventory, tblEmpGeneralInfo, tblUserUnit
**Tables written:** (none — read-only procedure)

**Transaction / error handling:** no explicit transaction; no TRY/CATCH.

**Business logic:** Identical body to `sp_GET_Employee_LeaveAvailablePreload_HRAPI` — same per-leave-type lookup against `ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory`, apparently a duplicate procedure under a different name.

**Result set:** Single result set: `YearCasual, CasualRemain, YearMedical, MedicalRemain, YearEarn, EarnRemain, YearMaternity, MaternityRemain`.

**Application caller(s):** UNUSED — no application caller found

**Full T-SQL body:**
```sql
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_GET_Employee_LeaveList_HRAPI] 
	
	@LeaveYear Nvarchar(MAx),
	@EmpInfoId Nvarchar(MAx)
AS
BEGIN
	


  DECLARE @EmpMasterCode Nvarchar(MAX)

	SELECT @EmpMasterCode=EmpMasterCode FROM tblEmpGeneralInfo WHERE EmpInfoId=@EmpInfoId  

DECLARE	@YearCasual Nvarchar(MAX),	@CasualRemain Nvarchar(MAX),	@YearMedical Nvarchar(MAX),	@MedicalRemain Nvarchar(MAX),	@YearEarn Nvarchar(MAX),	@EarnRemain	Nvarchar(MAX), @YearMaternity Nvarchar(MAX),	@MaternityRemain Nvarchar(MAX)
 
	
  SELECT  @YearCasual=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Casual'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @CasualRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Casual'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   
 
 
  SELECT  @YearMedical=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Medical'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @MedicalRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Medical'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  
 
  SELECT  @YearEarn=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Earn'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @EarnRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Earn'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

   
  SELECT  @YearMaternity=  YearDayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Maternity'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   

  	
  SELECT  @MaternityRemain=  DayQty    FROM ZASHRIS..tblLeaveInventory WHERE EmpMasterCode=@EmpMasterCode  and LeaveYear=@LeaveYear and LeaveName='Maternity'
  and LeaveYear = @LeaveYear   


  select ISNULL(@YearCasual,0) YearCasual	,ISNULL(@CasualRemain,0) CasualRemain	,ISNULL(@YearMedical,0) YearMedical	,ISNULL(@MedicalRemain,0)	 MedicalRemain,ISNULL(@YearEarn,0) YearEarn	,ISNULL(@EarnRemain,0) EarnRemain	,ISNULL(@YearMaternity,0) YearMaternity	,ISNULL(@MaternityRemain,0) MaternityRemain
 
     
END
```
